# Claude Code Environment Variables (v2.1.282)

Reverse-engineered from `cli.unpack.js` at tag `2.1.282` (last updated 2026-09-25). This catalog covers first-party Claude Code knobs plus the ambient and bundled-dependency environment variables that are read somewhere in the shipped runtime.

## How this was derived

**The bundle format changed at 2.1.242, and every symbol-based scan has to account for it.** Through 2.1.241 `cli.unpack.js` was one CommonJS-wrapped file with a single top-level scope. From 2.1.242, `bin/claude.exe` carries Bun's code-split ESM payload: 1378 modules at 2.1.242 and 2067 at 2.1.282. `cli.unpack.js` is now a re-bundle in which every module is an IIFE registered in `__B` and imported as `var { x: y } = __m("/$bunfs/root/<chunk>")`. Minified names are module-local, so one short symbol (`a`, `D`, `le`) is defined in dozens of modules with unrelated meanings. Every scan below is therefore scoped to the modules whose import resolves, through re-export chains, to the defining module. At 2.1.242-2.1.247 the re-bundle also renamed exports (`N6c: Hi`), so an importer's local name differs from the definer's; from 2.1.248 imports keep their names. A scan that assumes same-name imports finds **zero** inert-namespace reads at 2.1.242-2.1.247. That is how the first pass of this revision went wrong, before the resolver was rewritten.

Most first-party variables are not read through bare `process.env.NAME`. The surface is the union of the forms below, and the arithmetic was checked at every one of the 37 tags from 2.1.237 to 2.1.282:

1. **Typed schema declarations.** A builder object `D` (line 22075, module `chunk-fps7hcj2.js`) has six kinds: `str()`, `rawStr()`, `bool()`, `triBool()`, `int({min, max, digitsOnly})` and `enum([...])`. `rawStr` is new at 2.1.246. At 2.1.282 it is invoked **1063** times: 1060 in `chunk-bkgbm924.js` (lines 22110-25250), two in `chunk-s6jke8mm.js` (the messaging pair) and one in `chunk-wjy9nrfz.js` (the tether flag). Names are bound through `Oo(map, { NAME: () => SYM })` export maps. The main module has eight: `l` 63, `f` 64, `G` 38, `u` 290, `d` 45, `b` 189, `P` 65 and `m` 306, which sum to 1060. The side maps are `o` (2) and `e` (1). The completion invariant holds exactly at all 37 tags: raw builder invocations = declaring symbols = export-map-mapped names, with no unmapped remainder. At 2.1.282 that is 1063 = 1063 = 1063 (881 at 2.1.237). The export-map pattern accepts any `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*` key, because 19 typed names are not SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE (`ComSpec`, `ConEmuANSI`, `ConEmuPID`, `ConEmuTask`, `NoDefaultCurrentDirectoryInExePath`, `OneDrive`, `OneDriveCommercial`, `OneDriveConsumer`, `ProgramData`, `ProgramFiles`, `SystemRoot`, `__CFBundleIdentifier`, `all_proxy`, `gcloud_project`, `google_application_credentials`, `google_cloud_project`, `http_proxy`, `https_proxy`, `no_proxy`).
2. **Direct reads.** There are 431 literal names read as `process.env.NAME`, `process.env?.NAME` or `process.env["NAME"]` (473 at 2.1.237). A further 8 names are read only through a bound alias or destructuring of `process.env` (12 at 2.1.237). Three further read paths add 41 names that none of those forms reach (7 at 2.1.237, all through loops). Two of the paths are new in this range:
    - the case-tolerant lookup helpers `xF(env, "NAME")` and `fa(env, "NAME")` (lines 59771, 110090), from 2.1.247, which fall back to a case-insensitive match on Windows;
    - the capability-checked engine call `env.get("NAME")`, from 2.1.273, whose host handler returns `process.env[name]`, except that `USER_TYPE` is pinned to `"external"` (line 467331);
    - literal-name loops such as `for (k of ["GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM"]) process.env[k]`, already present at 2.1.237 but not scanned before.

    Most of the fall in literal reads is migration onto these paths, not removal.
3. **Bundled-SDK readers whose key is a parameter or a constant.** OpenTelemetry's `getStringFromEnv` family reads 25 literal names (unchanged); the call form is `(0, ns.getStringFromEnv)("NAME")`. The Anthropic SDK's runtime-agnostic reader (`globalThis.process.env?.[e]?.trim()` with a `Deno.env.get` fallback) is called with 42 literal names (unchanged). Three of those, `ANTHROPIC_SESSION_ID`, `ANTHROPIC_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_KEY` and `ANTHROPIC_WORK_ID`, had never been catalogued. The bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client reads eight names through a constants map, as `process.env[w.IDENTITY_ENDPOINT]` (lines 1137631-1137944). Those reads are present at 2.1.237 too, and seven of the eight had never been catalogued.
4. **Name registries.** These are literal sets, assembled from spreads resolved transitively and across modules. Across tags, each registry is identified by **containment** of the previous tag's set first and Jaccard similarity second. Jaccard alone scores a superset poorly: at 2.1.251 the 24-name secret list grew to 95, and a Jaccard-only matcher picked an unrelated 9-name set. The six tracked registries at 2.1.282 are:
    - **settings-injectable:** `Zd`, 211 names, line 38237. It is tested by the predicate `ndt` (line 38255), which since 2.1.251 also admits five names only when truthy (`Qd`, line 38238) and `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS` only when every `Name: value` line passes a name-and-value check (`nc`, line 38241). The name must be an RFC 7230 token that matches no credential or routing word, and the value must have no interior line break, NUL or character above U+00FF. Lines without a colon are ignored, and a bare CR anywhere rejects the whole value. Since 2.1.257 it also admits two names only when falsy (`ec`, line 38239).
    - **provider-sensitive:** `Vd`, 106, line 38194.
    - **proxy/host-managed:** `wa`, 127, line 46781, consumed by the settings sanitizer at line 46793.
    - **child-environment strip:** `ZUe`, 90, line 643375.
    - **subprocess-forwarding:** `le`, 71, line 876862.
    - **subprocess secret-scrub:** `Eo`, 95, line 69940.

    A seventh list, the project-settings env deny list (`eBn`, 158 names, line 220184), is described in the change log but not unioned, because it is defensive: it holds uppercase Windows spellings and OTel mTLS names that nothing reads.
5. **Inert-namespace and registry-only names.** These have no typed declaration and no literal read, and are reachable only through the inert accessor or a registry. 53 names are read through the inert namespace at 2.1.282 (31 at 2.1.237).

All accessor namespaces come from one factory, `wpn(o, t)` (line 25223, `chunk-bkgbm924.js`). It returns `Object.create(t)` carrying a lazily parsing, value-caching getter for each own key of `o`, plus `set` / `unset` helpers that write through to `process.env`. At 2.1.282 there are four namespaces:

- `a = wpn(SU, c)` (line 25255) is the main accessor over the eight merged maps. Its prototype `c` holds platform facts such as `isCI` and `platform`, not variable names.
- `Dn = wpn(IU, null)` with `IU = {}` (line 25257) is the **inert namespace**. It defines no getters and has a `null` prototype, so every `Dn.NAME` read evaluates to `undefined` whatever the environment holds.
- `bw = wpn(udsInboxShape, null)` (line 25259) serves `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` and `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN`.
- `vZe = wpn(tetherShape, null)` (line 451020, `chunk-x9fwahqm.js`), new at 2.1.281, serves `CLAUDE_CODE_TETHER_LIVE`.

The inert names have no typed declaration, so a scan that counts accessor reads only for typed names finds **zero** inert reads and would wrongly conclude the quirk was fixed. The quirk is not fixed: it grew from 31 to 53 names in this range.

Minified identifiers are regenerated on every build; never carry one forward. The builder / main accessor / inert namespace triple was `Ge/G/Kd` at 2.1.237 and `Ge/V/Rd` at 2.1.238, then `t/Hi/fi` at 2.1.242 (renamed exports) and `t/Yi/yi` at 2.1.246. It was `R/a/Bn` at 2.1.248, `x/a/Zn` at 2.1.251, `P/a/Dn` at 2.1.269 and `D/a/Dn` here. The main accessor has kept the name `a` since 2.1.248 only because imports stopped being renamed. `Dn` names the inert namespace at 2.1.269, 2.1.270, 2.1.280 and 2.1.282, but at 2.1.281 it is `On`. Treat every symbol and line number as a version-specific evidence pointer, and dereference before comparing.

**1541 variables are documented.** Of these, 1479 are live entries: 1459 catalog rows, counted with `rg -c '^\| `'` bounded to the *Authentication & API*...*Third-Party Credentials* range and covering 1456 distinct names, plus the 23 runner-injected names listed inline in *Self-Hosted Runner*. The other 62 are historical entries under *Removed / Legacy*. Rows marked *Settings-injectable* may also be set through the `env` block of `settings.json`. Coverage was checked mechanically in both directions at this tag: every one of the 1401 names in the union of the forms above, plus the messaging and tether namespaces, has a catalog row, with no remainder.

Known gaps, not closed by this revision: reads through a function parameter that defaults to `process.env` (`n = e?.env ?? process.env; n.NAME`), and typed-namespace reads with a computed key (`a[name]`, as in the home-directory helper at line 317787). Names reached only that way are caught when they also appear in another form, which at 2.1.282 is true of every name found by hand while drafting rows.

Per-row `line NNNNN` citations record where a variable was observed **at the tag the row was written against**, not necessarily at 2.1.282. Rows new or rewritten in this revision cite 2.1.282 lines. Older citations predate the ESM re-bundle and point into a different file layout entirely, so re-resolve by content before relying on one.

## Reading the Type column

| Type | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `bool` | Truthy string (`1`, `true`) enables; unset or falsy disables. |
| `tri-bool` | Three-state. Unset defers to the default or a server-side experiment flag; `1`/`true` forces on; `0`/`false` forces off. This distinction matters — an explicit `0` is not the same as leaving it unset. |
| `int` | Integer. Unparseable values fall back to the built-in default. |
| `string` | Free-form string. |
| `raw string` | New builder kind at 2.1.246. The value is passed through verbatim: not trimmed, and an empty value stays distinct from unset. `string` trims and treats an empty or whitespace-only value as unset. |
| `enum a \| b` | Only the listed values are accepted; anything else is discarded and treated as unset. |
| `—` | Not in the typed schema — read directly from `process.env`, by a bundled dependency, or only via a name registry. |

## Changes since v2.1.237

Covers 36 releases, 2.1.238 through 2.1.282 (the archive has no tag for 2.1.244, 2.1.249, 2.1.253, 2.1.254, 2.1.255, 2.1.256, 2.1.262, 2.1.264, 2.1.279). As at every prior tag, the raw bundle diff is a full re-emission, so the surface was re-derived from scratch at **every** tag and the *results* diffed. Every change below is attributed to the release that introduced it. The derivation widened in this revision, as described in *How this was derived*: module-scoped resolution for the ESM re-bundle, three new direct-read paths, the Azure constant-map form, SDK-reader call sites and containment-based registry matching. Re-derived with the widened method, the 2.1.237 surface is **1125** names, not the 1103 stated in the previous revision. The 22 extra names (`ANTHROPIC_SESSION_ID`, `ANTHROPIC_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_KEY`, `ANTHROPIC_WORK_ID`, `CC`, `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_TIMEOUT_MS`, `DEFAULT_IDENTITY_CLIENT_ID`, `FORCE_HYPERLINK`, `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM`, `IDENTITY_ENDPOINT`, `IDENTITY_HEADER`, `IDENTITY_SERVER_THUMBPRINT`, `IMDS_ENDPOINT`, `MSI_ENDPOINT`, `MSI_SECRET`, `RUNNER_RELEASE_IDLE_SESSION_MIN`, `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_RELEASE_IDLE_SESSION_MIN`, `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MIN`, `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_SEC`, `TEAMCITY_VERSION`, `npm_config_arch`, `npm_config_libc`, `npm_config_platform`) were all present and read at 2.1.237; 5 of them already had catalog rows.

| Change | Count | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Variables added to the bundle | 271 | Zero whole-token occurrences at 2.1.237. 57 of them are third-party credential names that entered the subprocess secret-scrub list at 2.1.251. Listed by release below. |
| Existing tokens that became env reads | 33 | Present in the bundle at 2.1.237; the listed release is when a scanned form first reaches them, mostly CA-bundle and home-directory names that gained typed declarations. Not counted as new variables. For a few the listed release is too late: `ZDOTDIR`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME` and `XDG_STATE_HOME` were read earlier through an env parameter that defaults to `process.env` (`n = e?.env ?? process.env; n.ZDOTDIR`), and `HOMEDRIVE` / `HOMEPATH` through the bundled AWS SDK's home-directory helper. Neither form is scanned; each of those rows says how the variable was reached before. |
| Variables removed from the bundle | 26 | Zero whole-token occurrences at 2.1.282. The 23 that had catalog rows moved to *Removed / Legacy*. |
| Left the surface, still present | 2 | `CC`, `CLAUDE_CODE_PROFILE_QUERY`: no reader at 2.1.282, but the token survives (see *Partial removals*). |
| Added and removed within the range | 22 | Never documented. Listed below for completeness. |
| Full surface (all forms, six registries) | 1401 (was 1125) | 1125 + 271 + 33 − 26 − 2 = 1401. The arithmetic closes exactly. |
| Typed schema declarations | 1063 (was 881) | The completion invariant holds at all 37 tags. See the per-release table for the chain. |
| Kind / constraint drift | 19 | 9 `bool` → `tri-bool`, 6 → `raw string` (2 from `bool`, 4 from `string`), 1 `bool` → `string`, 1 `tri-bool` → `string`, 2 `int` constraint changes (see *Retyped*). |
| Literal `process.env` names | 431 (was 473) | The drop is mostly migration onto the typed accessor, the engine `env.get` call and the lookup helpers. Only 9 of the names that lost a literal read left the bundle. |
| Helper / engine / loop reads | 41 (was 7) | The lookup helpers arrived at 2.1.247 and the engine `env.get` call at 2.1.273; the loops were present before but not scanned. See form 2 in the derivation notes. |
| Bundled-SDK reader names | OTel 25, Anthropic SDK 42, Azure constant map 8 (all unchanged) | 3 + 7 of these were catalogued for the first time in this revision. |
| Declared but never consumed | 50 (was 55) | +5: `BAT_THEME`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ATTRIBUTION_BASELINE_REUSE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_REPL`, `HUB_CONFIG`, `VCR_RECORD`. −10: `ANT_OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`, `BUN_CHROME_PATH`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_TIP_ID`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_WINDOWS_CREDMAN`, `CLAUDE_CODE_HFI_BEARER_TOKEN`, `CLAUDE_CODE_JSONL_TRANSCRIPT`, `CLAUDE_CONTEXT_COLLAPSE`, `ENVIRONMENT_SERVICE_KEY`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_LOGS_ENDPOINT`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_TRACES_ENDPOINT`. Of the names that left, only three gained a working reader: `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_TIP_ID`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_WINDOWS_CREDMAN` and `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_TRACES_ENDPOINT`. `BUN_CHROME_PATH` gained a read site at 2.1.271, but inside the artifact-preview code, which is not compiled into the shipped build. Two others were removed. The remaining four gained only a list or regex mention, which is a second *occurrence* but not a *consumer*. |
| Inert-namespace reads | 53 (was 31) | +27 names, −5 (`CLAUDE_CODE_SABLE_THRUSH` (2.1.242), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_CALLER_SESSIONS_MAX` (2.1.260), `CLAUDE_CODE_MODEL_CAPABILITIES` (2.1.268), `CLAUDE_REPL_VERBOSE` (2.1.275), `CLAUDE_CODE_TETHER_LIVE` (2.1.281)). **The quirk is not fixed.** |

### Per release

`Typed` is the declaration count at that release. A variable is listed at the first release in which a **scanned read form** reaches it, which is not always its first occurrence. Five had earlier non-read occurrences in name lists: `GIT_SSH` (from 2.1.242), `GIT_ATTR_SOURCE` (from 2.1.242), `ALLUSERSPROFILE` (from 2.1.251), `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_SESSION_ID` (from 2.1.268) and `OPENSSL_CONF_INCLUDE` (at 2.1.280). The same applies to `BASH_ENV` (listed at 2.1.237) and `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_ARCHIVE` (from 2.1.239) in the within-range table. `GH_CONFIG_DIR` is listed at 2.1.251, where the plugin-eval sandbox builder first reads it through an unscanned local helper, not at 2.1.261, where its typed declaration arrived. Releases with no environment-surface change are omitted: 2.1.241, 2.1.243, 2.1.245, 2.1.250, 2.1.252, 2.1.258, 2.1.266, 2.1.270, 2.1.272, 2.1.276 and 2.1.278 re-derive identically to their predecessor.

### Partial removals and lost readers

- `CLAUDE_CODE_PROFILE_QUERY` lost its typed declaration and every reader at 2.1.248. Its two surviving occurrences are a hint message and the telemetry-name allowlist, so it fails the zero-occurrence test for *Removed / Legacy* while doing nothing. Its row says so.
- `TASK_MAX_OUTPUT_LENGTH` lost its typed declaration and literal reads at 2.1.277, and the bundled release notes confirm it "no longer ha[s] any effect". It stays in the settings-injectable registry: settable, but inert.
- `DISABLE_COMPACT` and `DISABLE_INSTALLATION_CHECKS` lost their typed declarations at 2.1.248 but are still read directly. Only their Type column changed.
- `CI` lost its typed declaration at 2.1.248 and `NODE_ENV` its literal read at 2.1.246. Both are read again from 2.1.280 through the engine `env.get` call.
- `CC` was read only through a destructured `process.env`, in the same bundled `sharp` prebuild-detection code as the three `npm_config_*` names. That code went away at 2.1.257. It was never catalogued, and the remaining 117 occurrences are unrelated identifiers.
- Eleven names that were typed from 2.1.242 to 2.1.263 lost their declarations at 2.1.265 but survive in untracked name lists (credential-file pointers, toolchain lists). They are listed under *Added and removed within the range*.

| Subprocess secret-scrub | 95 (was 24) | 2.1.251: +71 |

Two of these changes are structural, not membership churn. At 2.1.239 the proxy/host-managed set gained 28 AWS and GCP credential and endpoint names (`AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_*`, `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`, the GCE metadata trio), so a filtered settings `env` block can no longer point the CLI at different cloud credentials. At 2.1.251 the secret list grew from 24 to 95 names, and settings-injectability became conditional, with truthy-only, falsy-only (from 2.1.257) and content-checked branches.

The untracked project-settings env deny list grew from about 14 names at 2.1.237 to 158 at 2.1.282, in steps: 28 at 2.1.242, 64 at 2.1.251, 82 at 2.1.275, 108 at 2.1.281, and 158 at 2.1.282. Keys in it are dropped with a warning when they appear in the `env` block of `.claude/settings.json` or `.claude/settings.local.json` (line 220184). Rows of catalogued variables that are on it say so where the drafting pass found it. 29 members are outside the surface, because nothing reads them: the OTel `*_CERTIFICATE` / `*_CLIENT_KEY` / `*_INSECURE` / `PROFILES_*` family, uppercase Windows spellings such as `ONEDRIVE` and `PROGRAMFILES`, and `CLAUDE_CODE_TOOL_USE_ID`.

The settings-`env` pipeline also has three smaller drop sets that are not tracked registries: names dropped from every settings file's `env` block (lines 220345 and 220347) and a silent-drop set (line 220250). They consist of CLI-internal session variables (`CCR_AGENT_PROXY_*`, `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_*`, `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY*`, `CLAUDE_CODE_QUESTION_*` and the like), and the rows of affected variables cite them.

### Inert namespace

Joined, with the release: `CLAUDE_CODE_SABLE_THRUSH` (2.1.240), `CLAUDE_CODE_HANDBACK_PROVENANCE` (2.1.242), `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_PROMPT_PROVENANCE` (2.1.242), `CLAUDE_RUNNER_STALL_GIVEUP` (2.1.242), `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_FIRST_LAUNCH` (2.1.246), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_SERVE` (2.1.246), `CLAUDE_CODE_TICKLISH_WHISPER` (2.1.246), `CLAUDE_CODE_TICKLISH_WHISPER_TIMEOUT_MS` (2.1.246), `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_ARTIFACT_CONSENT_RULE` (2.1.247), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_SESSION_CHANNEL` (2.1.247), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_POLICY` (2.1.248), `CLAUDE_CODE_DISPATCH_V2D` (2.1.251), `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_SEGMENTED_TRANSCRIPT` (2.1.257), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_CALLER_SESSIONS_MAX` (2.1.257), `CLAUDE_CODE_COMPILED_ACORN` (2.1.261), `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_FIVE_CLASS_ASKS` (2.1.265), `CLAUDE_CODE_TETHER_LIVE` (2.1.265), `CLAUDE_CODE_MODEL_CAPABILITIES` (2.1.267), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_ADOPT_MCP` (2.1.267), `CLAUDE_CODE_SENDMESSAGE_HANDBACK` (2.1.267), `CLAUDE_CODE_SLEEPY_SNOWFLAKE` (2.1.271), `CLAUDE_CODE_PER_TURN_TIMING` (2.1.277), `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_CLASSIFIER_OVERLAP` (2.1.280), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_ASK_SEATS` (2.1.280), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_JUMP_QUEUE` (2.1.280), `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_INHERITED_TYPE_GRANT` (2.1.281), `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_SPECULATIVE_CLASSIFIER` (2.1.282).

23 of these are still inert at 2.1.282 and cannot take effect: each read evaluates to `undefined` whatever the environment holds, and each row states the resulting fixed behaviour. `CLAUDE_CODE_TETHER_LIVE` left the inert namespace at 2.1.281 by becoming a real typed variable in the new tether namespace. `CLAUDE_CODE_MODEL_CAPABILITIES` stopped being read through it at 2.1.268. `CLAUDE_CODE_SABLE_THRUSH` and `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_CALLER_SESSIONS_MAX` came and went within the range.

## Changes since v2.1.232

Covers five releases, 2.1.232 -> 2.1.233 -> 2.1.234 -> 2.1.235 -> 2.1.236 -> 2.1.237. As at every prior tag the raw bundle diff is a full re-emission — minified identifiers are regenerated on each build — so the surface was re-derived from scratch and the *results* diffed rather than the text. All four intermediate bundles **were** checked out and derived independently, so every change below is attributed to the specific release that introduced it rather than to a range. Unlike the last two revisions there is no accessor-migration artefact to discount: the count of literal `process.env` names moved only from 471 to 473, and both of those are genuinely new names.

Thirty variables entered the bundle and one left. Every one of the 30 was confirmed to have **zero** whole-token occurrences at the immediately preceding tag, so none of them is a rename or a pre-existing registry entry gaining a declaration — there are no "newly typed, not new" cases this time.

| Change | Count | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Variables added to the bundle | 30 | Eight at 2.1.233, nine at 2.1.234, six at 2.1.235, six at 2.1.236 and one at 2.1.237. Listed with their release below. |
| Variables removed from the bundle | 1 | `CLAUDE_CODE_FABLE_BRIDGE_DIALOG_TIMEOUT_MS`, at 2.1.236: present at 2.1.235, zero occurrences at 2.1.236 and 2.1.237. Moved to *Removed / Legacy*. |
| Full documented surface (five access forms) | 1103 (was 1074) | 1074 + 30 − 1 = 1103. The arithmetic closes exactly. |
| Typed schema declarations | 881 (was 856) | 856 -> 864 (2.1.233, +8) -> 868 (2.1.234, +4) -> 874 (2.1.235, +6) -> 880 (2.1.236, +7 −1) -> 881 (2.1.237, +1). The completion invariant (raw builder invocations = declaring symbols = export-map-mapped names) holds exactly at all five tags. |
| Type / enum drift | 1 | `CLAUDE_CODE_MOCK_REMOTE_SETTINGS` changed from `bool` to `str` at 2.1.235. See the note below — the retype is inert. The one apparent second difference is the same false positive as the last three revisions: `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX`'s enum array is `["us", "eu", "apac", "jp", "au", "global"]` at both tags, bound to `Mjc` at 2.1.232 and `Ggu` (line 43680) here. |
| Direct `process.env.NAME` reads | 473 (was 471) | +2 at 2.1.234 (`CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SLACK_TAG_TOKEN`), no name lost a literal read at any tag in the range. |
| Bundled-SDK helper names | 25 (unchanged) | Identical membership at both tags. |

### Removed

| Variable | Removed in | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FABLE_BRIDGE_DIALOG_TIMEOUT_MS` | 2.1.236 | Had a typed `int` declaration read as `Y.CLAUDE_CODE_FABLE_BRIDGE_DIALOG_TIMEOUT_MS` (line 522716 at 2.1.232) with a 60000 ms default. Zero whole-token occurrences at 2.1.236 and 2.1.237. The whole `CLAUDE_CODE_FABLE*` family went with it — no name matching that prefix survives anywhere in the bundle. |

### Retyped at 2.1.235

`CLAUDE_CODE_MOCK_REMOTE_SETTINGS` moved from `bool` to `str` (`XZy = Ge.str()`, line 43277). The change is inert: the name has exactly two occurrences at 2.1.232 and again at 2.1.237 — its export-map entry and its membership in the proxy/host-managed registry — so nothing in the bundle reads it at either tag. It is not in the *declared but never consumed* set only because that set is defined by a single bundle-wide occurrence, and a registry entry supplies a second one. This is the distinction the derivation notes warn about: a second *occurrence* is not a *consumer*.

### A fifth access form, and why it mattered here

The three `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_*` knobs added at 2.1.234 have no typed declaration and no literal `process.env` read. They exist only as inert-namespace reads plus subprocess-strip registry entries, and a union built from the four access forms this file used through 2.1.232 does not contain them at all — they would have been reported as "not in the bundle" while shipping in it for four releases. The surface is therefore now derived as the union of five forms, and the registry and inert sets are diffed as first-class surfaces rather than being consulted only to annotate rows.

All three are functionally dead as environment variables: they are read through `Kd`, the namespace built over an empty map, so they always evaluate to `undefined` regardless of the real environment. `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_ARTIFACT_STUB_DIR` is the sharpest case, because the same bundle *writes* it into a spawned child's environment (line 938839) and the child's reader can never see it.

Auditing that form also exposed a documentation gap that had been open for several revisions. The derivation section has claimed since 2.1.226 that rows for the inert-namespace variables "say so individually", but only 6 of the 31 actually had a catalog row — the other 24 (the five `CCR_*` runner-timing names, the eleven `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_*` classifier knobs, `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_MCP`, `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_PROPAGATE_NESTED_MEMORY`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISPATCH_V2S`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FLEET_PAST_SESSIONS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_RC_LONG_TURN_NUDGE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_RC_PERMISSION_NUDGE`, `CLAUDE_REPL_VERBOSE` and `CLAUDE_WORKSHOP_PROGRESS`) existed only inside that summary sentence. They are documented individually as of this revision, which is most of the catalog-row growth from 1155 to 1179. Three of them are worth singling out because the inert read does not merely disable a feature, it pins one on: `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_MCP` is read as `?? true`, `CLAUDE_WORKSHOP_PROGRESS` as `!== false`, and `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_TEMPERATURE` as `Number(...)` with a finite-check fallback of 1 — so each of those features is permanently enabled at its default and cannot be switched off from the environment.

## Changes since v2.1.228

Covers three releases, 2.1.228 -> 2.1.229 -> 2.1.231 -> 2.1.232 (there is no 2.1.230 on npm). As at every prior tag the raw bundle diff is a full re-emission — minified identifiers are regenerated on each build — so the surface was re-derived from scratch and the *results* diffed rather than the text. All three intermediate bundles **were** checked out and derived independently, so every change below is attributed to the specific release that introduced it rather than to a range.

**2.1.231 changed nothing in the environment surface.** Its bundle differs from 2.1.229's (different SHA-256, same line count), but all four access-form surfaces re-derive byte-identical: the same 852 typed declarations with the same builder kinds and constraints, the same 493 literal `process.env` names, the same six registries with identical membership, and the same 55-name unconsumed set. Every environment-surface change in this range therefore belongs to either 2.1.229 or 2.1.232.

Eight variables entered the bundle and one left. As last revision, the headline number is a methodology trap rather than a change in surface: the count of literal `process.env.NAME` reads fell by 22, which a text-level diff would report as 22 removed variables. It is not. 2.1.232 continued the accessor migration; **all 22** names that lost a literal read remain typed declarations with live readers through `Y` and unchanged behaviour, and their whole-token occurrence counts are unchanged at both tags because one read site simply replaced another. Zero of them are removals.

| Change | Count | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Variables added to the bundle | 8 | Four at 2.1.229 (`CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_VIEWER_BASE_URL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_DIR_SYNC`, `CLAUDE_CODE_TURN_UPDATES`, `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_PREFIX_STAGGER_MS`) and four at 2.1.232 (`CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENT_FAST_ACK`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MTLS_RELOAD_ON_STALE_CONNECTION`, `CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE_CLOUD`, `CLAUDE_CODE_WEB_FETCH_AGENT`). Each was confirmed to have **zero** whole-token occurrences at the immediately preceding tag. |
| Variables removed from the bundle | 1 | `CLAUDE_CODE_HERON_TALLOW`, at 2.1.229: zero occurrences bundle-wide at 2.1.229, 2.1.231 and 2.1.232. Moved to *Removed / Legacy*. |
| Typed schema declarations | 856 (was 846) | 846 -> 852 at 2.1.229 (+7 typed, −1 removed) -> 852 at 2.1.231 (no change) -> 856 at 2.1.232 (+4). The +7 at 2.1.229 is four new variables plus three names that already existed as registry entries and gained a declaration (below). |
| Newly typed, not new | 3 | `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_LOGS_HEADERS`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_HEADERS` and `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_TRACES_HEADERS` gained typed `str` declarations at 2.1.229. All three had exactly one occurrence at 2.1.228 — their settings-injectable registry entry — so they are **not** new variables and are not listed as added. |
| Type / enum drift | 0 | Every carried-forward name kept its builder kind and constraint. The one apparent difference is the same false positive as the last two revisions: `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX`'s enum array is `["us", "eu", "apac", "jp", "au", "global"]` at both tags, bound to `wAc` at 2.1.228 and `Mjc` (line 43480) here. Note the near miss: a *different* array at line 43479, `wSr`, is the same list plus `us-gov`, so dereferencing the wrong symbol would manufacture a drift claim. |
| Direct `process.env.NAME` reads | 471 (was 493) | Unchanged at 493 through 2.1.229 and 2.1.231; the drop is entirely a 2.1.232 accessor migration. 22 names lost their literal read, all 22 still typed and still read via `Y`; none gained one. 493 − 22 = 471. |
| Bundled-SDK helper names | 25 (was reported as 20) | No change in the bundle — a correction to the derivation. The OTel SDK is bundled in the `(0, ns.getStringFromEnv)("NAME")` call form, which a regex anchored on `getStringFromEnv(` cannot match; five names including `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME` were missed at both tags. |
| Settings-injectable registry | 192 (was 183) | Unchanged at 2.1.229 and 2.1.231; +9 at 2.1.232 (`MIy`, line 80604). None of the nine is a new variable — all were already read, several only through template-computed keys. |
| Provider-sensitive registry | 101 (was 100) | Gained `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_VIEWER_BASE_URL` at 2.1.229 (`xIy`, line 80595), via the spread-in sub-list `ixe` (line 80587). Resolved recursively through every spread. |
| Proxy/host-managed registry | 93 (was 92) | Same name, same release, same sub-list (`FIy`, line 80745). |
| Subprocess-forwarding registry | 67 (was 66) | Same name, same release (`xZa`, line 847268). |
| Subprocess-stripping registry | 67 (unchanged) | Identical name set at all four tags (`dqr`, line 843750). |
| Secret-redaction registry | 23 (unchanged) | Identical name set at all four tags (`_zs`, line 197404). |
| Declared but never consumed | 55 (unchanged) | Identical membership at all four tags. No name joined or left the set. |
| Inert-namespace reads | 28 (unchanged) | Identical membership and identical per-name read counts at 2.1.228 and 2.1.232; the quirk is not fixed. |

### Added

| Variable | Added in | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_VIEWER_BASE_URL` | 2.1.229 | string | Dev override for the artifact viewer host, and the third member of a family that is declared but never read: like `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_ASSET_BASE_URL` and `..._LIVE_BASE_URL` it has no value reader, and its two occurrences are the export map and the endpoint list `ixe` (line 80587). Joining `ixe` is not cosmetic — it makes the name settable from a host-credentials file and frozen against change on refresh (line 463095) — but nothing reads it from the ambient environment. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_DIR_SYNC` | 2.1.229 | bool | Disables the background worker that mirrors the working directory for remote/SDK sessions, passed as the `disabled` field of the dir-sync gate (line 1227466). Narrower than the pre-existing `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_WORKING_SYNC`, which disables the whole working-sync path that also gates this worker. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TURN_UPDATES` | 2.1.229 | tri-bool | Overrides the `turn_updates` model capability. Unusual for a tri-bool in this bundle: it defers not to a `tengu_*` experiment but to the cached client-data payload, through the resolver `FYf(name, envValue, ctx)` (line 816252, read at 816288). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_PREFIX_STAGGER_MS` | 2.1.229 | int (min 0) | How long a workflow agent waits for a same-prefix sibling's first response so the two share a warm prompt cache. The default of 5000 ms comes from the call site (`Nwf(e) => e ?? 5000`, line 671379), not from the `int({min: 0})` constraint, and the wait is skipped entirely when `DISABLE_PROMPT_CACHING` is set (line 672436). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENT_FAST_ACK` | 2.1.232 | tri-bool | Acknowledges an artifact comment immediately instead of waiting for the responder. Unset defers to `tengu_gorse_pylon`, off by default (line 778005). Makes a quartet of the artifact-comment gates. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MTLS_RELOAD_ON_STALE_CONNECTION` | 2.1.232 | bool | Suppresses reloading the mTLS client certificate and key when a connection is found stale (line 463201). Only meaningful in sessions that present a client certificate, since the same guard short-circuits when `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_CERT` is unset. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE_CLOUD` | 2.1.232 | bool | Cloud-scoped sibling of `CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE`, read with the same one-way `Boolean(env) \|\| experiment` operator (line 535889), so it can force the capability on but never off. Also propagated to child sessions (line 361732). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WEB_FETCH_AGENT` | 2.1.232 | tri-bool | Routes WebFetch through an agent-backed path. Unset defers to `tengu_clever_orbit`, off by default, and the result is memoised on first read (line 358061). |

| `CLAUDE_CODE_HERON_TALLOW` | 2.1.229 | Had a typed `bool` declaration plus a read that OR-ed it with client data and the `tengu_heron_tallow` flag (line 585693 at 2.1.228). Zero whole-token occurrences at 2.1.229, 2.1.231 and 2.1.232. Unusually, the experiment went with it: `tengu_heron_tallow` also has zero occurrences, so no server-side flag survives to decide the behaviour. |

### Newly settings-injectable at 2.1.232

Nine names joined the settings-injectable registry, meaning they can now be set through the `env` block of `settings.json` rather than only in the ambient environment. **None is a new variable** — every one was already read at 2.1.228, and the whole group is an OpenTelemetry-configuration gap being closed, plus one subagent flag.

`CLAUDE_CODE_FORK_SUBAGENT`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_COMPRESSION`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_LOGS_COMPRESSION`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_COMPRESSION`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_TRACES_COMPRESSION`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_TEMPORALITY_PREFERENCE`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_TRACES_PROTOCOL`, `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME`, `OTEL_TRACES_EXPORT_INTERVAL`.

Three of them — the signal-specific `_COMPRESSION` trio — deserve a note, because they are the clearest example in this file of why an occurrence count is not a liveness test. `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_TRACES_COMPRESSION` and `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_LOGS_COMPRESSION` have **zero** occurrences at 2.1.228 and exactly one at 2.1.232, which reads like two brand-new variables. They are not new: the exporter has always read them through the template expansion `` pef(`OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_${e}_COMPRESSION`) `` (line 598994), so the literal names never appeared in the bundle at all. What changed is that 2.1.232 spelled them out in the registry. The knob predates the name.

**The 22 accessor migrations at 2.1.232.** Each was checked individually before classification, and the check has a trap worth recording. The natural test — count whole-token occurrences with a `(?<![\w.$])NAME` boundary — is the standard guard against matching a minified symbol suffix, but applied to *variable* names it excludes exactly the dot-preceded `Y.NAME` reads that the migration created. Run that way, eight of the 22 appear to have lost their only consumer and become dead knobs. They have not: their unfiltered occurrence counts are identical at 2.1.228 and 2.1.232 (2 or 3 apiece), one read site having replaced another. The affected names are `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_FABLE_MODEL` and its `_DESCRIPTION` / `_NAME` variants, `ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL_AWS_REGION`, `CLAUDE_CODE_CERT_STORE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_CERT`, `CLAUDE_CODE_COLD_COMPACT`, `CLAUDE_CODE_IDE_HOST_OVERRIDE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_IDE_SKIP_AUTO_INSTALL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_IDE_SKIP_VALID_CHECK`, `CLAUDE_CODE_PROACTIVE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SSE_PORT`, `CLAUDE_CODE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP`, `CLAUDE_CODE_USER_EMAIL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_VOICE_FORWARD_INTERIMS_TYPED`, `CLAUDE_GATEWAY_ALLOW_LOOPBACK`, `DISABLE_AUTO_COMPACT`, `DISABLE_BRIEF_MODE_STOP_HOOK`, `MCP_TRUNCATION_PROMPT_OVERRIDE`, `USE_BUILTIN_RIPGREP` and `VOICE_STREAM_BASE_URL`. No catalog row changed as a result.

## Changes since v2.1.226

Covers two releases, 2.1.226 -> 2.1.227 -> 2.1.228. As at every prior tag the raw bundle diff is a full re-emission — minified identifiers are regenerated on each build — so the surface was re-derived from scratch and the *results* diffed rather than the text. Unlike the previous revision, `cli.unpack.js` at the intermediate tag **was** checked out and derived independently, so every change below is attributed to the specific release that introduced it rather than to a range.

Nine variables entered the bundle and one left. The headline number, though, is a methodology trap rather than a change in surface: the count of literal `process.env.NAME` reads fell by 63, which a text-level diff would report as dozens of removed variables. It is not. 2.1.228 migrated duplicate literal read sites onto the typed accessor; 66 of the 67 names that lost a literal read remain typed declarations with live readers and unchanged behaviour, and only `CLAUDE_CODE_COMMIT_LOG` genuinely disappeared. Each of the 67 was checked individually for whole-token occurrences at the new tag before being classified.

| Change | Count | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Variables added to the bundle | 9 | Seven at 2.1.227 (three `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_*` fields, `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENT_RESPONDER`, the `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_*` pair, `CLIPBOARD_NAPI_NODE_PATH`) and two at 2.1.228 (`CLAUDE_CODE_COWORK_FRAME_ARTIFACTS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN`). Each was confirmed to have **zero** whole-token occurrences at the preceding tag. |
| Variables removed from the bundle | 1 | `CLAUDE_CODE_COMMIT_LOG`, at 2.1.227: zero occurrences bundle-wide at both 2.1.227 and 2.1.228. Moved to *Removed / Legacy*. |
| Typed schema declarations | 846 (was 841) | 841 -> 844 at 2.1.227 (+4 typed, −1 removed) -> 846 at 2.1.228 (+2). |
| Type / enum drift | 0 | Every carried-forward name kept its builder kind and constraint argument. The one apparent difference is the same false positive as last revision: `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX`'s enum array is `["us", "eu", "apac", "jp", "au", "global"]` at both tags, bound to `e_c` at 2.1.226 and `wAc` here. Resolved by dereferencing the symbol, not by comparing symbol names. |
| Direct `process.env.NAME` reads | 493 (was 556) | Unchanged at 556 through 2.1.227; the drop is entirely a 2.1.228 accessor migration. 67 names lost their literal read (66 still typed and still read via `X`), 4 gained one. 556 − 67 + 4 = 493. |
| Settings-injectable registry | 183 (unchanged) | Identical name set at all three tags (`MYg` here, `oBg` at 2.1.227, `bBg` at 2.1.226). |
| Provider-sensitive registry | 100 (was 98) | Gained the `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_*` pair at 2.1.227, via the spread-in sub-list `cFc` (line 78908). Resolved recursively through every spread. |
| Proxy/host-managed registry | 92 (was 90) | Same two names, same release, same sub-list. |
| Subprocess-forwarding registry | 67 (was 65) | Unchanged at 2.1.227; gained `CLAUDE_CODE_COWORK_FRAME_ARTIFACTS` and `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN` at 2.1.228 (`_Br` here, `MNr` at 2.1.227, `OLr` at 2.1.226). |

|---|---|---|---|

| `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_BASE_URL` | 2.1.227 | — | Intended memory-API base-URL override. Occurs exactly once bundle-wide (the `cFc` registry pair, line 78908) and has no reader at all. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_TOKEN` | 2.1.227 | — | Intended memory-API bearer token, already registered for secret redaction (`vRs`, line 165008). Its one read is inert twice over — see the note below. |
| `CLIPBOARD_NAPI_NODE_PATH` | 2.1.227 | — | Intended override for the `clipboard-napi` native module path, and unreachable: `GOo()` executes `return null;` immediately before the branch that reads it (lines 244349-244351). |

### Removed

| Variable | Removed in | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COMMIT_LOG` | 2.1.227 | Had a typed `str` declaration (`sag`, line 33424 at 2.1.226) plus a direct read. Zero whole-token occurrences at 2.1.227 and 2.1.228, so it meets the *Removed / Legacy* definition. No successor name appears. |

**Three of the seven names added at 2.1.227 cannot currently do anything.** They are documented as knobs because the bundle declares them, but each is provably inert at this tag, and the reasons differ:

- `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_BASE_URL` has no reader anywhere — its single occurrence is the registry entry itself.
- `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_TOKEN` is read once, in `sbt()` (line 149167), and fails twice: the read sits below an `if (!BLe()) return …` early return, where `BLe()` is `pun() !== undefined` and `pun` is literally `function pun() { return; }`, so the line is unreachable; and it reads through `pp`, the always-empty namespace described in *How this was derived*, so it would yield `undefined` even if reached.
- `CLIPBOARD_NAPI_NODE_PATH` is read inside `GOo()` after an unconditional `return null;`, making both the override and the vendored-binary fallback below it dead code.

This is the same class of finding as `CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL` last revision, and the `trs()` factory now explains the whole family mechanically rather than case by case.

**`WSL_INTEROP` left the "declared but never consumed" set at 2.1.227 — and this one is a real consumer, not a registry entry.** Its occurrence count rose from 1 to 2, and the added occurrence is a genuine read: WSL platform detection now accepts either marker, `this.sources.env.WSL_DISTRO_NAME || this.sources.env.WSL_INTEROP` (line 32064), falling back to a kernel-string check when neither is set. Its catalog row, which had said setting it has no effect, is corrected in this revision. The distinction matters because four other names — `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_MODEL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_BG_CLASSIFIER_MODEL`, `CLAUDE_CONTEXT_COLLAPSE_MODEL` and their kin — also occur more than once while still having no reader at all; their occurrences are registry memberships. Every name leaving the unconsumed set was checked by dumping the context of each occurrence before the row was changed.

**Two long-present variables were catalogued for the first time.** Neither is new to the bundle; both were missed by earlier revisions because of how they are accessed. `ANTHROPIC_SCOPE` is read only through parameterized readers (`Dv("ANTHROPIC_SCOPE")`, and `i4t = e => process.env[e]?.trim() || undefined` at line 134956), so no literal-grep pass would ever surface it. `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED` appears only as a registry member, in the TLS/client-certificate set and the proxy/host-managed set, and is honoured by the bundled Node runtime rather than by first-party code. Both now have rows.

## Changes since v2.1.224

Covers two releases, 2.1.224 -> 2.1.225 -> 2.1.226. As at every prior tag the raw bundle diff is a full re-emission — minified identifiers are regenerated on each build — so the surface was re-derived from scratch and the *results* diffed rather than the text. `cli.unpack.js` at 2.1.225 was not separately checked out; every row below is a diff of the 2.1.224 and 2.1.226 bundles only. Where a change cannot be pinned to one of the two intermediate releases it is attributed to "between 2.1.224 and 2.1.226" rather than guessed.

This is the quietest environment-surface release in the recent series. Every one of the four access-form surfaces re-derived from scratch — the 841 typed declarations, the 555 direct `process.env.NAME` reads, the settings-injectable registry (183), the provider-sensitive registry (98), and the subprocess-forwarding registry (65) — came back **byte-identical in both count and content** to 2.1.224, confirmed by set-diffing the extracted name lists, not by comparing counts alone. Zero type/enum drift was found across all 841 typed names (one apparent drift, `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX`'s enum constraint, is a false positive: the constraint array is `["us", "eu", "apac", "jp", "au", "global"]` at both tags, just bound to a different minified symbol name — `ldc` at 2.1.224, `e_c` at 2.1.226). The declared-but-never-consumed set is also byte-identical: the same 56 names, re-verified with a corrected whole-token occurrence regex (see the note below the table).

| Change | Count | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Variables added to the bundle | 1 | `CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL`, brought in by a brand-new "device bridge" subsystem (see below). Landed sometime between 2.1.224 and 2.1.226; not attributable to a specific one of the two releases from the bundle alone. |
| Variables removed from the bundle | 0 | No name occurs at 2.1.224 that does not still occur at 2.1.226, across all four access forms and every registry. |
| Typed schema declarations | 841 (unchanged) | Same 841 names as 2.1.224, confirmed by set-diffing the two 841-name lists: zero additions, zero removals. `CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL` is **not** a typed declaration — see below. |
| Type / enum drift | 0 | Every one of the 841 carried-forward names kept both its builder kind and its constraint argument; the one apparent difference (see above) resolved to a minified-symbol-name artifact, not a real change. |
| Direct `process.env.NAME` reads | 555 (unchanged) | Identical name set at both tags. |
| Settings-injectable registry | 183 (unchanged) | Identical name set at both tags (`bBg` here, `VDg` at 2.1.224). |
| Provider-sensitive registry | 98 (unchanged) | Identical name set at both tags (`mBg` here, `qDg` at 2.1.224), including every spread-in sub-list resolved recursively. |
| Proxy/host-managed registry | 90 (was 89) | Gained `CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL` (`EBg` here, `XDg` at 2.1.224). No other change. |
| Subprocess-forwarding registry | 65 (unchanged) | Identical name set at both tags (`OLr` here, `THr` at 2.1.224). |
| Declared but never consumed | 56 (unchanged) | Identical 56-name set at both tags — see the correction note below. |

**Correction to the unconsumed-count methodology.** Re-deriving the unconsumed set for this revision surfaced a bug in the identifier-boundary regex used to count occurrences: a naive `(?<![\w.$])NAME(?![\w])` pattern excludes the property-access form `te.NAME` (and its predecessor `ee.NAME`), because the `.` immediately before `NAME` trips the negative lookbehind. This under-reports every typed variable's true occurrence count by at least one live read and, left uncorrected, would have produced a false unconsumed count in the hundreds (an initial pass returned 360). The correct boundary excludes only `\w` and `$` from the lookbehind, not `.`: `(?<![\w$])NAME(?![\w])`. Both tags' unconsumed sets were recomputed with the corrected pattern for this revision; the counts and content documented for 2.1.224 in the table above were independently re-verified with it and matched the previously published 56 exactly, so no earlier row in this file needs correction — only the extraction script itself did.

### New subsystem: the device bridge

An entirely new, self-contained subsystem appears at 2.1.226 with zero occurrences anywhere in the 2.1.224 bundle: a "device bridge" that opens a WebSocket connection to relay tool calls to/from a paired device (functions and diagnostics are grouped around lines 1185300-1186400, all prefixed `[deviceBridge]` in their log output). It registers under the telemetry event `tengu_device_bridge_stopped` / `tengu_device_bridge_skipped` and is gated by `Bs("allow_remote_sessions")` plus a first-party-provider check, so it is inert unless both conditions hold.

| Variable | Change | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL` | added, between 2.1.224 and 2.1.226 | — | environment-derived (`ZQv`/`eZv` by OAuth environment) | Intended override for the device-bridge base URL, read as `qf.CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL !== undefined` at line 1186217 inside `kqh()`, the function that resolves the bridge URL before falling back to an OAuth-environment default. **This read is currently inert**: `qf` is built as `BYi(_0g, null)` (line 43953) where `_0g` is assigned only once, to `{}` (line 43952), and never populated anywhere else in the bundle — so `qf` never has an own property for any name, and `qf.CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL` evaluates to `undefined` unconditionally regardless of what is set in the real `process.env`. The same always-empty-backing-object pattern already existed at 2.1.224 (as `Zm`, backed by `z_g`) for roughly two dozen other `qf`-routed names (`CLAUDE_RUNNER_FAIL_FAST_FETCH`, the `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_*` family, etc.), so this is a pre-existing bundle quirk applied to a new name, not a regression specific to this variable. Listed in the proxy/host-managed registry (`EBg`, line 79387) alongside its neighbors `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_BASE_URL` / `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_OAUTH_TOKEN` / `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_SESSION_INGRESS_URL`, but not in the settings-injectable or provider-sensitive registries, and not a typed declaration (absent from the 841-name `Fe`-builder set). |

This is the largest environment-surface change in the recent series, and almost all of it is one feature: **a self-hosted session runner** (`claude self-hosted-runner`) that did not exist at 2.1.222 and brings 60 new variables with it. Outside that subsystem the release is small — nine typed declarations added and three removed.

| Change | Count | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Variables added to the bundle | 63 | 60 belong to the new self-hosted-runner subsystem (enumerated in their own table below); the other three are `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX`, `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_DB` and `CLAUDE_CODE_ULTRAREVIEW_QUOTA_FIXTURE`. |
| Variables removed from the bundle | 2 | `CLAUDE_CODE_INVESTIGATE_FIRST` (gone at 2.1.223) and `CLAUDE_CODE_MARL_CORMORANT` (gone at 2.1.224). Both now return zero matches bundle-wide and have moved to *Removed / Legacy*. |
| Typed schema declarations | 841 (was 835) | 835 -> 838 at 2.1.223, 838 -> 841 at 2.1.224. Nine declarations added, three removed; the arithmetic closes exactly against the raw builder-invocation count. |
| Declaration removed but name still live | 1 | `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION` lost its typed declaration at 2.1.224 but remains in the settings-injectable registry. This is a partial removal, not a deletion — see the note below the table, and do not report it as a removed variable. |
| Type / enum drift | 0 | Every carried-forward variable kept both its builder kind and its constraint argument; a full kind-by-kind comparison of the 832 names present at both tags found no differences. |
| Settings-injectable registry | 183 (was 181) | Grew by `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX` and `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_UNKNOWN_MODEL_WINDOW_ENFORCEMENT`. |
| Subprocess-forwarding registry | 65 (was 54) | Now carries `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` among others. |
| Declared but never consumed | 56 (was 63) | Same name-only criterion as the previous revision (exactly one occurrence bundle-wide can only be the export-map entry). Seven variables left the set and none entered it — but only two of the seven gained an actual reader; see the note below. |

**On the "declared but never consumed" count — a registry entry is not a consumer.** Seven names left the unconsumed set, but the reason differs and the distinction matters. Only `CLAUDE_RUNNER_FETCH_DEPTH` and `CLAUDE_RUNNER_DISABLE_AWAITING_ACTION_OVERRIDE` gained real readers (`ee.CLAUDE_RUNNER_FETCH_DEPTH`, with an "Ignoring …" diagnostic for a malformed value, and a negated read in the awaiting-action path). The other five — `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`, `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_OAUTH_TOKEN`, `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_SESSION_INGRESS_URL`, `ANT_OTEL_LOGS_EXPORTER` and `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_ENDPOINT` — merely had their names added to a name registry, so each now occurs twice rather than once. Their occurrence count rose without a single line of code reading them, and their catalog rows still correctly say they have no consumer. This is a limitation of the name-only criterion worth carrying forward: it detects *a second occurrence*, not *a read*, so every departure from the set needs its context inspected before being reported as a variable coming alive. `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_ENDPOINT` is the subtlest of the five: its second occurrence is the runner *writing* the name as an object key when it builds a child's environment (line 1183382), which is the opposite of the CLI reading it.

**On `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION`.** Its typed declaration is gone at 2.1.224, so a scan of the typed surface alone reports it as removed — but the name still occurs in the settings-injectable registry (`VDg`, line 79168), which means it can still be injected into `process.env` from a `settings.json` `env` block. What was lost is the typed read path, not the name. This is precisely the failure mode the four-access-form rule exists to prevent, and it is the reason "removed" in the table above is reserved for names with **zero** bundle-wide occurrences.

| Variable | Change | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX` | added 2.1.224 | string | inference-profile discovery | Forces the cross-region inference-profile prefix used for Bedrock model ids (`us`, `eu`, `apac`, …) instead of letting the CLI discover it, read as `ee.ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_REGION_PREFIX ?? <discovery>` at line 124762. The bundle is unusually explicit about its limits: when discovery is unavailable it warns that the prefix is applied without an availability check and that requests will 400 unless the matching `<prefix>.*` profiles are enabled in the account (line 125138), and when some models resolve elsewhere it warns that this is "a preference, not a residency guarantee" (line 125168). Do not treat it as a data-residency control. Settings-injectable and provider-sensitive. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_DB` | added 2.1.224 | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_umber_lattice` (`false`) | Enables the artifact database backend. Unset defers to the experiment, which is off by default (line 691827). |

A parse or validation failure yields `null` rather than falling through to the network, so a malformed value disables the quota check rather than being ignored. Development aid, not a production knob. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ADMIN_ENV_UNION` | added 2.1.223 | bool | unset (union enabled) | Set to `true` to stop the CLI from unioning administrator-supplied environment settings into the effective environment; read as `ee.CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ADMIN_ENV_UNION === true` at line 83278, so only a real boolean-true value disables it. Appears in the host-managed and subprocess-strip registries, meaning a host-managed CLI controls it rather than the user. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_UNKNOWN_MODEL_WINDOW_ENFORCEMENT` | added 2.1.223 | bool | unset (enforcement on) | Restores the pre-2.1.223 behavior for models this build does not recognize. With enforcement on (the default), auto-compact assumes a context window for an unknown model and keeps the session inside it; setting this to `1` returns to waiting for the API to report the real window. The bundle surfaces the variable name directly in its user-facing diagnostic (line 1122227), which also names the supported alternative — mapping the model in the `modelOverrides` setting. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PARCHMENT_FERN` | added 2.1.223 | tri-bool | — | Gates an unreleased capability behind a codename, checked at two sites (lines 145537, 145546) that first exclude a set of older models and, at the second site, additionally require the `tengu_velvet_mallet` experiment for the specific model. As with every codenamed flag, the bundle gives no user-facing description of what it enables. |

| `CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE` | added 2.1.224 | bool | experiment `tengu_harbor_kite` (`false`) | Another codenamed capability, but note the combining operator: it is read as an OR of the experiment with `Boolean(ee.CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE)` (line 253805), so unlike the tri-bool flags above the variable can only turn the feature **on** — it cannot force it off once the experiment enables it. Related machinery (`tengu_harbor_kite_limits`, a peer-guard limits object at line 641890) suggests a concurrency- or peer-limited feature. Also in the host-managed registry. |

It is carried into child sessions by the subprocess-forwarding registry (`THr`, line 785074) and simultaneously listed in the host-managed strip set, i.e. forwarded to children the CLI spawns but not honoured from an untrusted parent. |

| Variable | Change | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
