## Compaction & Context

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WISE_COMET` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_wise_comet) | **New at 2.1.260.** Decides whether compaction strips the thinking blocks in the kept tail, emitting a `thinking_stripped` marker with scope `all` (lines 454441, 495875). When set it applies for any thinking type; unset, stripping is off unless thinking is `adaptive`, where GrowthBook `tengu_wise_comet` (default `false`) decides. The decision and its source are logged. |
| `CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE` | string | — | Override auto-compact percentage threshold |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW` | string | — | Auto-compact window size |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BLOCKING_LIMIT_OVERRIDE` | string | — | Override blocking limit for compaction |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLASSIFIER_SUMMARY` | string | — | Overrides summary classifier mode: truthy = `llm`, falsy = `heuristic`; unset falls back to the default selector |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COLD_COMPACT` | bool | — | Truthy forces the "cold compact" conversation-compaction path |

## OpenTelemetry

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `OTEL_LOG_MANAGED_SETTINGS` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.274.** When on, the `claude_code.managed_settings_resolved` OTel event carries the redacted, truncated policy settings (`managed_settings.settings`) and keeps the `managed_settings.resolved_sha256` and `managed_settings.helper.script_sha256` digests; when off those digests are deleted (lines 839534, 839561). The value is taken from the applied global-config env, overridden in order by each settings source's `env` that is not on an exclusion list, then falls back to the pre-settings snapshot or the accessor, and is parsed as truthy (line 839499). Project-scoped settings cannot set it (lines 38122, 220184), and it is excluded from the event's own `envVars` summary (lines 45399, 45548). |
| `OTEL_METRICS_INCLUDE_REPOSITORY` | — | `false` | **New at 2.1.269.** Attaches repository attributes (`vcs.repository.url.full`, `vcs.repository.name`, `vcs.owner.name` and the branch) to emitted metrics. Read through the string-keyed metric-attribute helper `m("OTEL_METRICS_INCLUDE_REPOSITORY")` (line 282764), which falls back to the defaults table where it is `false` (line 282667). When it is on and `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES` does not already carry the repository URL, exporting waits for repository detection to settle (line 282870). Settings-injectable. |
| `ANT_CLAUDE_CODE_METRICS_ENDPOINT` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `ANT_OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. Still no value reader at 2.1.282. Since 2.1.269 it is matched by name in the endpoint-name pattern at line 45929. |
| `ANT_OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_HEADERS` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `ANT_OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_PROTOCOL` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |

Read by the bundled exporter through `pef()` (line 598985), which accepts only `none` or `gzip` and logs "expected 'none' or 'gzip'" for anything else, treating it as unset. The signal-specific `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_{TRACES,METRICS,LOGS}_COMPRESSION` names take precedence (`UAS`, line 598993, returns the signal value `?? ` the global one). **Settings-injectable as of 2.1.232.** |
| `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_LOGS_COMPRESSION` | — | falls back to `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_COMPRESSION` | Logs-specific OTLP compression mode, same `none`/`gzip` validation. Never appears as a literal string in the bundle — it exists only as the template expansion `` `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_${e}_COMPRESSION` `` at line 598994, which is why a literal grep does not find it. **Settings-injectable as of 2.1.232**, the release that first spelled the name out. |
| `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_COMPRESSION` | — | falls back to `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_COMPRESSION` | Metrics-specific OTLP compression mode; same template-expanded reader and same validation as the logs variant. **Settings-injectable as of 2.1.232.** |

Settings-injectable. |

## Network & Proxy

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GZIP_REQUEST_BODY_LEVEL` | int (min 0, max 9, digits only) | unset (defers to tengu_golden_popcorn, 0) | **New at 2.1.282.** Gzip compression level for compressed API request bodies (lines 221051, 336397). A value of 1 to 9 is passed as `compress: { encoding: "gzip", level }`; 0, which is also the `tengu_golden_popcorn` fallback default, keeps the runtime's default level. It applies to the main API client only; the `ccr_worker` middleware sends plain `gzip` (line 221547). |
| `OPENSSL_CONF` | — | ambient | **New at 2.1.281.** OpenSSL configuration file path. When it points to an absolute path and `OPENSSL_CONF_INCLUDE` is unset, the CLI derives `OPENSSL_CONF_INCLUDE` from its directory for child environments (line 324694). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `OPENSSL_CONF_INCLUDE` | — | derived from `OPENSSL_CONF` | **New at 2.1.281.** OpenSSL include directory. If the operator already sets it, the CLI leaves it alone; otherwise it is derived from `OPENSSL_CONF` (lines 324683, 324698). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `CLAUDE_GATEWAY_PROXY_IS_EGRESS_BOUNDARY` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.277.** For a Claude apps gateway whose only egress is a forward proxy: outbound requests hand the proxy the hostname and rely on its allowlist instead of resolving and SSRF-checking locally (lines 1289631, 1289650). It stays inactive, with a warning, when no usable `HTTPS_PROXY`/`HTTP_PROXY` is set, when `CLAUDE_GATEWAY_ALLOW_LOOPBACK` is set, or when `NO_PROXY`/`no_proxy` is non-empty (lines 1289633, 1289647); `Hs()` re-checks the same conditions (line 1289654). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GATEWAY_HINT_HEADERS` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_splendid_sutton) | **New at 2.1.273.** Controls hint headers for API gateways (line 335303): `x-claude-code-request-class` and `x-claude-code-agent-type` on API requests (line 335408), plus `x-claude-code-context-compacted`, `x-claude-code-compaction` and `x-claude-code-prev-tool-durations` when those values exist (lines 504962, 504981). Unset, the headers are on when the first-party provider talks to the Anthropic API host and off for other providers; a first-party provider with a custom base URL follows `tengu_splendid_sutton` (default false). |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_NO_PROXY_LOCAL_ONLY` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.267.** Narrows the `NO_PROXY` list the remote agent proxy hands to tools to loopback addresses, plus the RFC 1918 and CGNAT ranges unless a standalone `AGENT_PROXY_URL` is in use, instead of the default lists that can also exempt Anthropic API hosts, package registries and link-local or cluster-local ranges (line 1627861). Read at agent-proxy init, which runs only when `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` and `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_ENABLED` are set (line 1627786); unlike the receive/upload gate flags it is not unset from the environment after the read. |
| `GIT_SSL_CAPATH` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Declared as a typed string (line 24064) but never read through the accessor. The name is on the plugin-eval environment allow-list, so an operator's value is copied into the eval child process (line 1903171), and it matches the `^GIT_(SSL\|PROXY)_` pattern of the git-sensitive filter, so git commands the CLI runs itself get the value from before settings `env` was applied (lines 323337-323338, 323395). |
| `SSL_CERT_DIR` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Declared as a typed string (line 24050) but never read through the accessor. The name is in the git-sensitive set, so git commands the CLI runs itself get the value from before settings `env` was applied (lines 323335, 323395), and it is on the plugin-eval environment allow-list, so an operator's value reaches the eval child process (line 1903171). |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_RECEIVE_GATE_DISABLED` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.248.** Disables the download backpressure gate of the remote agent proxy relay by raising its `receiveHighWater` limit, 4194304 bytes by default (line 1625335), to `Infinity` (line 1627986). Read and then unset from the environment at agent-proxy init, which runs only in `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` sessions with `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_ENABLED` (line 1627784). |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_UPLOAD_GATE_DISABLED` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.248.** Disables the upload backpressure gate of the remote agent proxy relay by raising `sendPauseAfterPendingBytes` (default 0, so sending pauses as soon as bytes are pending) and the 180000 ms `sendStallTimeoutMs` to `Infinity` (lines 1625332, 1627989). Read and then unset from the environment at agent-proxy init, which runs only in `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` sessions with `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_ENABLED` (line 1627785). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GZIP_CCR_REQUEST_BODIES` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_gentle_hammock) | **New at 2.1.247.** Gzip compression of request bodies on the `ccr_worker` request surface, for first-party API hosts only (lines 221060, 221547). `false` never compresses; `true` compresses any string body of 1024+ chars under the Bun runtime unless the server rejected gzip earlier in the process; unset additionally requires no Unix socket, proxy, mTLS or custom CA and the `tengu_gentle_hammock` gate (default false). Other surfaces use `CLAUDE_CODE_GZIP_REQUEST_BODIES` with a 4096-char minimum. |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_FRAME_HOSTS` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.246.** Comma-separated host list for tunnelling artifact frame requests through the remote agent proxy. In a CCR container session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` without `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND`) with the agent proxy running and no HTTP(S) proxy configured, the first hostname becomes the `frame` route sent with the session JWT, and a refused or failed hop falls back to the `ccr-gateway` route (lines 368008, 368231). One invalid entry (bad DNS label, all-numeric labels, over 253 characters) marks the whole value malformed, which logs a one-time warning and keeps the gateway routes. |
| `CLAUDE_STREAM_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | unset (byte-stream idle timeout) | **New at 2.1.242.** How long an armed streaming API request (and any Bedrock `invoke-with-response-stream` POST) waits for response headers before it is aborted and retried (lines 336015, 336061). The value is clamped to 10000–1800000 ms; the first window also grows by 1 s per 32 KiB of request body and is capped at `API_TIMEOUT_MS` minus 1 s. Unset falls back to the byte-stream idle timeout: 180000 ms first-party, otherwise `max(CLAUDE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS, 300000)`, overridable by `CLAUDE_BYTE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` or `tengu_byte_stream_idle_timeout_ms`. Named in the `no_response` retry schema and in error hints (lines 107885, 456867). Settings-injectable via settings.json `env` (line 38237). |
| `AGENT_PROXY_AUTH_TOKEN` | string | — | Bearer token paired with `AGENT_PROXY_URL` for the remote agent egress proxy; consumed and deleted at startup |
| `AGENT_PROXY_URL` | string | — | Upstream proxy URL used by the remote agent egress gateway; consumed and deleted at startup |
| `ALL_PROXY` | string | — | Generic proxy URL for all protocols; part of the proxy env set forwarded to child processes and consulted by bundled HTTP clients. |
| `API_FORCE_IDLE_TIMEOUT` | int | — | Truthy keeps the default idle timeout on Anthropic API requests; otherwise the request timeout is disabled |
| `CARGO_HTTP_CAINFO` | string | — | Cargo's custom CA bundle path. Read when collecting CA bundles already present in the environment, and written into the sandbox environment pointing at the configured custom CA so Rust tooling trusts it (lines 239719, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24057). |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_ENABLED` | bool | — | Enables the remote agent egress proxy. When unset the proxy is not started. |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_INCLUDE_HOSTS` | string | — | Comma-separated host allowlist relayed through the remote agent proxy when `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_RELAY_MODE` is `selective`; unlisted hosts use normal networking. |
| `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_RELAY_MODE` | string | — | Relay mode for the remote agent proxy. `selective` tunnels only the hosts in `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_INCLUDE_HOSTS`; any other value tunnels all traffic, and an empty include-list fails closed to tunnel-all. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ADDITIONAL_PROTECTION` | bool | `false` | Enable additional API protection headers |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AGENT_PROXY_GH_SHIM` | bool | — | Installs the `gh` CLI shim that routes GitHub CLI traffic through the agent proxy. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AGENT_PROXY_GIT_CONFIG` | bool | — | Rewrites git configuration so git traffic goes through the agent proxy. Either this or the gh shim being set enables agent-proxy git integration. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CERT_STORE` | string | — | Select the CA certificate store strategy (`system`, `bundled`, or a custom path); when unset, Node defaults are used |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_CERT` | string | — | Path to client TLS certificate |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_KEY` | string | — | Path to client TLS private key |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MTLS_RELOAD_ON_STALE_CONNECTION` | bool | — | **New at 2.1.232.** Suppresses the reload of the mTLS client certificate and key when a connection is found stale. The guard reads `if (!Y.CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_CERT \|\| Y.CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MTLS_RELOAD_ON_STALE_CONNECTION) return` (line 463201), so it only has an effect in sessions that actually present a client certificate; setting it keeps a long-lived process on the certificate it loaded at startup even after that certificate has been rotated on disk. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_KEY_PASSPHRASE` | string | — | Passphrase for client TLS key |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_PROXY_AUTH_HELPER` | bool | `false` | Enable the proxy-auth helper (`proxyAuthHelper` settings entry) that supplies dynamic `Proxy-Authorization` headers; must be `1` to activate |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PROXY_AUTH_HELPER_TTL_MS` | int | helper default | Cache TTL (ms) for credentials produced by the proxy-auth helper |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PROXY_RESOLVES_HOSTS` | bool | `false` | Let proxy resolve hostnames |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMULATE_PROXY_USAGE` | bool | `false` | Simulate proxy usage for testing |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WEBFETCH_USE_CCR_PROXY` | bool | — | Routes WebFetch requests through the coordinator (CCR) proxy when a coordinator session is active. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WEBSEARCH_USE_CCR_PROXY` | bool | — | Routes WebSearch requests through the coordinator (CCR) proxy when a coordinator session is active. |
| `CLAUDE_ENABLE_BYTE_WATCHDOG` | tri-bool | — | Enable byte-level stream watchdog. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it became settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_SLOW_FIRST_BYTE_MS` | int (min 1) | `30000` | Timeout threshold for first byte from API (ms) |
| `CLOUDSDK_CORE_CUSTOM_CA_CERTS_FILE` | string | — | gcloud's custom CA bundle path. Part of both the read set and the sandbox-injection set, so an existing value is discovered and a configured CA is propagated to gcloud (lines 239719, 560722, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24053). |
| `CURL_CA_BUNDLE` | string | — | Path to CA bundle file (forwarded to subprocesses for curl compatibility). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24052). |
| `DENO_CERT` | string | — | Deno's custom CA certificate path. Read when discovering existing CA bundles and injected into the sandbox environment for the configured CA (lines 239719, 560723, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24056). |
| `DENO_TLS_CA_STORE` | — | `system,mozilla` | Written, never read. Injected as `system,mozilla` into agent-proxy child environments so Deno trusts both the OS store and its bundled Mozilla roots — but only when the generated CA bundle carries the host system roots and the variable is not already set (lines 560727, 1093627). |
| `DOCKER_HTTPS_PROXY` | — | — | HTTPS proxy propagated to Docker-based child tooling when Claude Code configures a proxy. |
| `DOCKER_HTTP_PROXY` | — | — | HTTP proxy propagated to Docker-based child tooling when Claude Code configures a proxy. |
| `ELECTRON_GET_USE_PROXY` | — | `1` when a proxy is detected | Set to `1` by the proxy-propagation logic so the `@electron/get` package honors the detected system proxy. |
| `GIT_SSL_CAINFO` | string | — | Git's custom CA bundle path. Read when discovering existing CA bundles and injected into the sandbox environment for the configured CA (lines 239719, 560723, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24062). |
| `GLOBAL_AGENT_HTTPS_PROXY` | — | — | HTTPS proxy propagated for the global-agent proxy library used by child tooling. |
| `GLOBAL_AGENT_HTTP_PROXY` | — | — | HTTP proxy propagated for the global-agent proxy library used by child tooling. |
| `GLOBAL_AGENT_NO_PROXY` | — | — | No-proxy list propagated for the global-agent proxy library used by child tooling. |
| `HEX_CACERTS_PATH` | string | — | Hex/Erlang CA bundle path. Member of the read set used to discover CA bundles already configured in the environment (lines 560723, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24061). |
| `HTTPLIB2_CA_CERTS` | string | — | httplib2 CA bundle path. Member of the read set used to discover CA bundles already configured in the environment (lines 560722, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24054). |
| `HTTPS_PROXY` | string | — | HTTPS proxy URL. Its presence (with the lowercase form, HTTP_PROXY, a unix socket, or a client cert) puts the HTTP stack on the custom-transport path. Settings-injectable. |
| `HTTP_PROXY` | string | — | HTTP proxy URL, checked alongside the lowercase form. Settings-injectable. |
| `NIX_SSL_CERT_FILE` | string | — | Nix CA bundle path. Read when discovering existing CA bundles and injected into the sandbox environment for the configured CA (lines 239719, 560723, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24060). |
| `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` | string | — | Path to additional CA certificates |
| `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED` | — | ambient (Node.js) | Standard Node.js TLS switch; `0` disables certificate verification process-wide. No first-party read: the name occurs only in two registries — the TLS / client-certificate set (`HYg`, line 78922) and the proxy/host-managed set (`$Yg`, line 79066) — so the CLI classifies it as host-managed for forwarding and stripping while the bundled Node runtime is what actually honours it. Present well before 2.1.228; first documented here. |
| `NO_PROXY` | string | — | Comma-separated no-proxy host list, checked alongside the lowercase form. Settings-injectable. |
| `PIP_CERT` | string | — | pip's custom CA bundle path. Read when discovering existing CA bundles and injected into the sandbox environment for the configured CA (lines 239719, 560723, 1093997). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24058). |
| `REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE` | string | — | Path to CA bundle file (forwarded to subprocesses for Python compatibility). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it gained a typed `str` declaration (line 24051). |
| `SSL_CERT_FILE` | string | — | Path to SSL certificate file (used with proxy config) |
| `UV_NATIVE_TLS` | — | `true` | Written, never read. Injected as `true` into agent-proxy child environments so the uv Python installer uses the platform native TLS store — but only when the generated CA bundle carries the host system roots and the variable is not already set (lines 560726, 1093627). |
| `YARN_HTTPS_PROXY` | — | — | HTTPS proxy propagated to Yarn in child tooling when Claude Code configures a proxy. |
| `YARN_HTTP_PROXY` | — | — | HTTP proxy propagated to Yarn in child tooling when Claude Code configures a proxy. |
| `http_proxy` | string | — | Lowercase HTTP proxy URL, checked alongside HTTP_PROXY. |
| `https_proxy` | string | — | Lowercase HTTPS proxy URL, checked alongside HTTPS_PROXY. |
| `no_proxy` | string | — | Lowercase no-proxy host list, checked alongside NO_PROXY. |

## Remote / Headless Mode

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_SPECULATIVE_CLASSIFIER` | — | off (pinned) | **New at 2.1.282.** Read only through the inert namespace with a fallback to the `tengu_violin_speculative_classifier` gate (default off) (line 318403), but `isSpeculativeClassifierOn` returns false on both branches, so the speculative-classifier path at its only call site (line 1643725) is never taken. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_EARLY_REMOTE_CONNECT` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.281.** With `--sdk-url` and `--resume`, opens the remote transport link to the `--sdk-url` during startup and passes it on as `earlyRemoteLink` (lines 840981, 1196317). Same preconditions as `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_EARLY_HYDRATE_PREFETCH` (remote auth headers must be available); startup metrics record `early_remote_connect: 1`. It is on the per-session list the bridge spawner clears before each spawn, and a settings-file `env` entry is ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_CHAIN` | bool | unset (off) | **New at 2.1.280.** Puts directory sync in chained mode on both the git-backed open path and the launch-folder path (lines 347919, 1054808, 1675978): the sync announces `chained: true` and links each accepted worktree commit into a chain of bundles instead of treating it as a single snapshot. Compared with `=== true`, so it is off unless set. Project-scoped settings files cannot set it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_ASK_SEATS` | — | unset (defers to tengu_unified_waterfall) | **New at 2.1.280.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 318371), so `areAskSeatsOn` always falls through to the `tengu_unified_waterfall` gate (default on). When on, a permission ask first waits, up to a wait limit, for an ask seat before prompting (line 1642001). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_JUMP_QUEUE` | — | unset (defers to tengu_valiant_rain) | **New at 2.1.280.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 318349), so the value always comes from the `tengu_valiant_rain` gate (default off). The result is passed to the session event transport as `servedCallRequestsJumpQueue` when remote tool forwarding is available (line 1196472). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_CHILD_MACHINE_SETTINGS` | bool | false | **New at 2.1.273.** Lets a Remote Control child session follow this machine's settings for Claude in Chrome. When true and the session is not in bypass-permissions mode (line 321414), a non-interactive session no longer forces Chrome integration off; it falls through to `claudeInChromeDefaultEnabled` (line 321738). The Chrome MCP setup also skips its reconnect auto-open (line 840705). The bridge sets it to `1` for children spawned with the machine-settings grant (line 1649547). It is stripped from spawned subprocess environments (line 113545) and dropped from settings `env` at every scope (lines 220184, 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_TURN_HANDOFF` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.271.** In a managed cloud worker (an `--sdk-url` session with `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SESSION_ID` and no `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND`), makes turn-handoff admission return `disabled` (lines 1196367, 1229438). The worker then advertises `turn_handoff: null` instead of the v1 capability and does not use the relay marker (line 1194359). The settings loader silently drops it from settings `env` (set at line 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_CHILD_ARTIFACT` | bool | false | **New at 2.1.269.** Marks a Remote Control (bridge) child session as allowed to use artifacts. The artifact availability check reports `sdk_default_off` for SDK, `claude-code-github-action` and `mcp` entrypoints unless this or `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT` is truthy (line 369359). The bridge sets it to `1` when spawning a child with the artifact grant and clears it otherwise (lines 1649500, 1649544). A child that receives the grant at runtime sets it in-process (`a.set`, line 1737861) and unsets it on undo. It is stripped from spawned subprocess environments (line 113545) and dropped from settings `env` at every scope (lines 220184, 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_CHILD_AUTO_DEFAULT` | bool | false | **New at 2.1.269.** Makes `auto` the fallback permission mode of a Remote Control child session. It is read as a truthy string from the `process.env` copy passed to the startup mode resolver (lines 190443, 190478). When no other mode is chosen and auto mode is neither killswitched nor disabled by settings, the session starts in `auto`. When settings set `disableAutoMode`, a settings `defaultMode: "auto"` is ignored with a warning. The bridge sets it to `1` for children spawned with the auto-default grant (line 1649541). It is stripped from spawned subprocess environments (line 113545) and dropped from settings `env` at every scope (lines 220184, 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_ADOPT_MCP` | — | on (pinned) | **New at 2.1.267.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 318447), so it is always `undefined` and `adoptMcpOverChannelSwitchOn` always returns true. Effect: when remote tool forwarding is enabled, the machine MCP-tool policy is `allowed` and the bridge server is not disabled, the served tool set includes MCP tools rather than the filtered set (line 1732867). |
| `CCR_SESSION_PROFILE` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.259.** Turns on the CCR session-profile relay, which takes effect only when `SESSION_INGRESS_URL` is a bare `https` origin, `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SESSION_ID` matches `cse_*` and the session is first-party (line 1551219). The resulting config enables placeholder "Claude in Chrome", "Browser" and "Computer use" tools that end the relay when called (line 1551465) and the relay start-up at turn handoff (line 1766061). Setting it also turns off inline tools (line 140152), and a settings-file `env` block cannot set it (line 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_EARLY_HYDRATE_PREFETCH` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.259.** With `--sdk-url` and `--resume`, starts reading the session's stored transcript events (main thread and subagents) from the remote session store during startup and passes the reads on as `earlyHydrateReads` (lines 840980, 1196317). Skipped when no remote auth headers are available; a conflict during the read discards the result, and startup metrics record `early_hydrate_prefetch: 1`. It is on the per-session list the bridge spawner clears before each spawn, and a settings-file `env` entry is ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_OWNER_ACCOUNT_UUID` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Account UUID of the Remote Control daemon's owner, written by the bridge spawner into each child it launches (line 1649506). A child running with `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND=bridge` records it, together with `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_OWNER_ORG_UUID`, as the owner of its supervised bridge session (line 1239978); when the owner-pin check is enabled, the supervised-session inbox send stops with `owner_unverified` if no owner was recorded and with `owner_changed` if the current credential is not attributed to that owner (line 1118928). It is on the per-session list the bridge spawner clears from the inherited environment before each spawn, and a settings-file `env` entry is ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_OWNER_ORG_UUID` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Organization UUID of the Remote Control daemon's owner, written by the bridge spawner next to `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_OWNER_ACCOUNT_UUID` (line 1649507). Read only when the account UUID is set, as the `organizationUuid` of the supervised bridge session's owner; an empty value is treated as absent (line 1239981). Same spawn-time clearing and settings-file `env` exclusion as the account UUID. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_STREAM` | string | on | **New at 2.1.248.** On unless set to `0`, `false`, `no` or `off` (line 347916). When on, directory sync attaches a filesystem change feed and streaming scope on the local side (lines 1054802, 1676112), and the worker side, reading the pre-settings environment snapshot, attaches its own change feed (line 1755438). Project-scoped settings files cannot set it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_POLICY` | — | parity (pinned) | **New at 2.1.248.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 318612), so the remote-tool serving policy is pinned to `parity`; the `interim` branch (any other value) is unreachable in the shipped build. The policy name is passed when the served-tools channel opens (line 1312668) and appears in the tools status facts (line 1312848). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_SESSION_CHANNEL` | — | session channel on (pinned) | **New at 2.1.247.** Read only through the inert namespace as `=== false` (line 318345), so `isSessionChannelDisabled` is always false: the served-tools switch never reports `channel_off` (line 1732892) and the status facts always report the channel as `session` (line 1312848). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_TOOLS_SERVE` | — | — | **New at 2.1.246.** Read only through the inert namespace inside `V0()` (line 318451), which would return true when unset; `V0()` is neither called nor exported from its chunk, so the variable has no effect in the shipped build. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_ENGINE` | string | unset (sync on) | **New at 2.1.242.** Comma-separated words, trimmed and lower-cased; any of `none`, `off`, `0`, `false`, `no` switches local file sync off on this machine, and every other word is ignored with a one-time warning (line 347884). Unset leaves sync on; the CLI's own error and hint messages point to `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_ENGINE=none claude --cloud` as the way to start a cloud session without uploading this folder's files (line 552524). Project-scoped settings files cannot set it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_FFWD` | string | on | **New at 2.1.242.** On unless set to `0`, `false`, `no` or `off` (line 347913). When on, the git-backed directory-sync open supplies a fast-forward handler for the synced branch, and the `tengu_dir_sync_git_open` event records the setting as `branch_rule` (line 1054714). Project-scoped settings files cannot set it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_LEGACY_BUNDLE` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.242.** Requests the previous (non-hardened) git-bundle capture when a cloud session is started from an uploaded copy of the working tree (lines 354137, 354444). Honored only when the environment claude was started with, or `~/.claude/settings.json`, sets it; set by another settings tier, or when a settings tier also moves `HOME`/`CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, it is ignored with a warning and the hardened capture runs (line 354144). On Windows the legacy capture is used unless the caller forces hardening. Project-scoped settings cannot set it (lines 220184, 353725). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_MCP_CARRIER` | enum 1 \| spent | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** Remote Control MCP-carrier marker, read straight from `process.env` at startup (line 112002): `1`, which the bridge spawner writes when it hands a child the Remote Control meta MCP server, makes the process the carrier child, which loads only that meta-server entry from a dynamic `--mcp-config` after checking its URL against the ingress origin and session id (line 428012); any other non-empty value marks a descendant, which never treats a session-ingress MCP URL as its own ingress server. The carrier child rewrites the value to `spent` at startup so its own subprocesses become descendants (line 427871), and a BYOC remote worker (`CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND=byoc` with `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE`) deletes a non-`1` value instead. While any carrier state is set, `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `SESSION_INGRESS_URL` and `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_PROMPT_SHA256` join the child credential scrub list; the self-hosted runner clears the variable for its children, and a settings-file `env` entry is ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_PROMPT_SHA256` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** SHA-256 hex digest of the append-system-prompt file the Remote Control daemon wrote, set by the bridge spawner (line 1649509). When `--append-system-prompt-file` is loaded (line 840393) the child reads the value and unsets it at once (line 427875): if set, the file's SHA-256 must match it case-insensitively or the appended prompt is dropped with an error log; if unset, the file is accepted except in a bridge carrier child (`CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_MCP_CARRIER=1`), where it is dropped. It joins the child credential scrub list while a bridge carrier state is set, the self-hosted runner clears it for its children, and a settings-file `env` entry is ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DIR_SYNC_GIT` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** Turns on the git engine for directory sync. In a remote worker it is passed as the git engine switch when worker dir-sync starts (line 1229470), and the worker-side sync reads it from the pre-settings environment snapshot to choose git-based sync when a repository is found (line 1755392). In the main query loop it (or an open dir-sync gate) makes each main-thread turn run the pending sync step and emit queued `dir_sync_notice` messages (line 815482); a settings-file `env` entry is ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_PLUGIN_FORWARDING` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** Turns off forwarding of the plugins enabled on the local machine to cloud sessions. Locally it hides the `/cloud-plugins` command (line 542835) and marks remote-session creation as `switched_off` (line 1409741); in a managed cloud worker it makes plugin-forwarding admission return `disabled` (lines 1195908, 1229517). The settings loader silently drops it from settings `env` (set at line 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_VITALS_EMITTER` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** Kill switch for the guest vitals emitter in cloud sessions (line 625861). Without it the CLI spawns the `vitals-emitter-guest` binary (pinned path `/root/.local/bin/vitals-emitter-guest`) with the session ID, API origin and session ingress token file (lines 632375, 1759975); with it set, or in essential-traffic-only mode, it logs that guest vitals are disabled and starts nothing. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOME_SEED_HOLD_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 0) | 300000 | **New at 2.1.239.** Upper bound in milliseconds on how long a remote session holds a turn while a pending home seed (settings synced from the user's machine, announced as "Started with settings from your machine") has not yet applied (lines 1759498, 1758212). The value is clamped with `Math.min(value, 300000)`, so it can only shorten the default; `0` skips the hold (line 1758155). The settings `env` filter drops it, so settings files cannot set it (line 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOME_SEED_VERDICT_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 0) | 60000 | **New at 2.1.239.** Upper bound in milliseconds that the home-seed hold waits for the seed verdict and its flag; when it expires the hold ends with `verdict_timeout` or `flag_timeout` (line 1758221). Clamped with `Math.min(value, 60000)` (line 1759499), so it can only shorten the default. The settings `env` filter drops it, so settings files cannot set it (line 220345). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NESTED_USER_REPAIR` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.238.** Controls how the stream-json input reader handles a message whose `message.role` is not `user` on a remote transport (line 1193153). By default a frame that wraps a plain user message one level deep is unwrapped and accepted ("Repaired a nested user message (one level)"); with this set the outcome is `repair_disabled` and the frame is dropped with a warning. Both outcomes log `tengu_sdk_malformed_input`; on a non-remote transport the wrong role is a fatal input error either way. |
| `CCR_DELTA_RESET` | — | effectively unset | Marks a runner delta reset. Read twice — as the `delta_reset` field of the runner startup-timing telemetry payload (line 621293) and as `Boolean(Kd.CCR_DELTA_RESET)` in a git-fetch retry predicate (line 682630). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. The predicate is therefore always false and the telemetry field always absent. |
| `CCR_ENABLE_BUNDLE` | bool | `false` | Enable the CCR bundle path used by remote/background task bootstrap logic |
| `CCR_FORCE_BUNDLE` | bool | `false` | Force the CCR bundle path even when normal preflight heuristics would not select it |
| `CCR_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE` | — | — | Marks the active OAuth token as one injected by the remote (CCR) host session rather than a local auth source. |
| `CCR_ON_BRANCH_DEFAULT_GUARD` | enum enforce \| observe \| off | `enforce` | Guard policy for committing on the default branch in remote sessions. "enforce" blocks it, "observe" only records the event, "off" disables the guard. Falls back to "observe" when the tengu_on_branch_default_guard_observe gate is on, otherwise "enforce". |
| `CCR_PREFETCH_NETWORK` | — | effectively unset | Reported as the `prefetch_network` field of the runner startup-timing telemetry payload (line 621294). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. |
| `CCR_PREWARM_STAT` | — | effectively unset | Reported as the `prewarm_stat` field of the same runner startup-timing payload (line 621292). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. |
| `CCR_PREWARM_VDA` | — | effectively unset | Reported as the `prewarm_vda` field of the same runner startup-timing payload (line 621291). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. |
| `CCR_RUNNER_STARTUP_TIMING` | — | effectively unset | JSON payload of startup-phase timings that the runner **writes** into the environment of the child session it spawns (line 1361777, beside `CCR_SPAWN_TIMESTAMP_MS`) and that the child parses back when emitting its startup telemetry (line 621279). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. The write and the read are therefore disconnected: the child never sees what the parent set, the same defect as `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_ARTIFACT_STUB_DIR`. |
| `CCR_SPAWN_TIMESTAMP_MS` | — | — | Unix spawn timestamp (ms) injected by the Claude Code Remote launcher; used to compute `spawn_to_first_checkpoint_ms` / `spawn_to_exec_ms` startup-performance telemetry (v2.1.145) |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_BASE_URL` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry with no reader anywhere in the bundle; setting it has no effect. Its name was added to a name registry at 2.1.224, so it now occurs twice rather than once — an occurrence count alone would wrongly suggest it came alive. |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_OAUTH_TOKEN` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry with no reader anywhere in the bundle; setting it has no effect. Its name was added to a name registry at 2.1.224, so it now occurs twice rather than once — an occurrence count alone would wrongly suggest it came alive. |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_GROUPING` | string | — | Grouping key used when the bridge reattaches sessions. Deleted from the environment after being read so it does not leak into children. |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_NO_BACKFILL` | bool | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Written as `"1"` into the child environment when a bridge reattach requests `noHistoryBackfill` (line 439404), suppressing replay of prior conversation history. Read back as `X.CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_NO_BACKFILL` (line 1053524), deleted from `process.env` immediately afterwards (line 1053532), and stripped again when a fresh child environment is assembled (line 697327), so it never leaks into grandchildren. |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_OWNER_ACCT` | string | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Owner account UUID of the session being reattached, written into the child environment from `ownerAccountUuid` (line 439398). Read as `X.CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_OWNER_ACCT` (line 1053524), then deleted on read (line 1053530) and stripped when building a fresh child environment (line 697325). |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_OWNER_ORG` | string | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Owner organization UUID of the session being reattached, written from `ownerOrganizationUuid` (line 439401). Same lifecycle as `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_OWNER_ACCT`: read through `X` (line 1053525), deleted on read, and stripped from freshly built child environments. |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_SEQ` | int | — | Sequence number passed when the TUI bridge reattaches to an existing session; consumed and deleted on read |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_SESSION` | string | — | Session ID used to reattach the TUI bridge to an existing session; consumed and deleted on read |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_SESSION_INGRESS_URL` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry with no reader anywhere in the bundle; setting it has no effect. Its name was added to a name registry at 2.1.224, so it now occurs twice rather than once — an occurrence count alone would wrongly suggest it came alive. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CONTAINER_ID` | string | — | Container ID for remote environments |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_RUNNER_VERSION` | string | — | Attach an environment-runner version header in remote bridge mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` | bool | `false` | Running in remote/headless mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_ENVIRONMENT_TYPE` | string | — | Remote environment type label |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_HERMETIC_MODE` | bool | — | Runs a remote session in hermetic mode. Only takes effect when CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE is also set. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_MEMORY_DIR` | string | — | Memory directory for remote mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_RAW_EVENTS_FILE` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SEND_KEEPALIVES` | bool | `false` | Send keepalive pings in remote mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SESSION_ID` | string | — | Remote session identifier |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SESSION_ORIGIN` | string | — | Origin that started the remote session. The value `review` changes the default handling of the cloud-agent session origin. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SETTINGS_PATH` | string | — | Path to a local file that overrides remote settings, skipping the settings API fetch entirely. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE_SETTINGS_POLL_MS` | int | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_FROM_SESSION` | string | — | Resume from a specific session ID |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYSTEM_PROMPT_GB_FEATURE` | string | — | When running in remote mode, selects a Growthbook/feature-flag key whose evaluated string value is used to override the Agent SDK `systemPrompt` option |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WEBSOCKET_AUTH_FILE_DESCRIPTOR` | string | — | WebSocket auth file descriptor. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.280 it started being read through the capability-checked engine `env.get` call (host handler at line 467331). |
| `CLAUDE_ENABLE_STREAM_WATCHDOG` | tri-bool | `false` | Enable stream watchdog. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it became settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_REMOTE_CONTROL_SESSION_NAME_PREFIX` | string | — | Prefix for remote control session names |
| `CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL` | — | environment-derived | **New, between 2.1.224 and 2.1.226.** Intended override for the new device-bridge feature's base URL. The read (`qf.CLAUDE_REMOTE_TOOLS_BRIDGE_URL`, line 1186217) is currently inert: its backing accessor object is always empty, so the property is always `undefined` regardless of what is set in the real environment — see *Changes since v2.1.224* for the evidence. Proxy/host-managed. |
| `CLAUDE_REMOTE_WORKFLOW_ARGS` | string | — | JSON-encoded arguments for the workflow named by `CLAUDE_REMOTE_WORKFLOW_SCRIPT` in a remote session; rejected if it exceeds a size cap. |
| `CLAUDE_REMOTE_WORKFLOW_SCRIPT` | string | — | Names the Workflow-tool script that is the deterministic entry point for a remote session launched with an environment-delivered workflow. |
| `CLAUDE_SESSION_INGRESS_TOKEN_FILE` | string | — | Path to session ingress token file |
| `CLAUDE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | `90000` | Stream idle timeout before disconnect (ms) |
| `SESSION_INGRESS_URL` | string | — | Session ingress URL for remote |
