## UI & Display

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COMMIT_BETWEEN_KEYS` | tri-bool | unset (off) | **New at 2.1.281.** When one terminal input read contains more than one key event, flushes React's pending synchronous work before dispatching each later key and once after the batch, so every keystroke is committed separately (lines 689472, 694330). Unset falls back to `false`; no experiment gate is consulted. Project-scoped settings files cannot set it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_EFFORT_REMINDER` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_proud_clover) | **New at 2.1.280.** Shows a warning when effort is `max`: the effort picker renders "⚠ May use excessive tokens resulting in long response times or overthinking. Use sparingly for the hardest tasks." in warning color, and choosing max raises a "Max effort · … · /effort to change" notice (lines 1429455, 1435941, 1873234). Unset defers to `tengu_proud_clover` (default off) (line 189495). Settings-injectable through settings.json `env`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_TERMINAL_IMAGES` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.275.** Marks the terminal as supporting the Kitty graphics protocol without probing, bypassing the checks that otherwise leave it unsettled inside tmux/screen or a background worker (lines 666957, 667000), and marks file-based image transfer as available (line 667134). Without it, support needs a probe reply plus a kitty (0.28.0 or later) or Ghostty terminal name (line 689040). It is in the registry of variables settable through settings.json `env`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FOOTER_INDICATOR` | string | unset (falls back to client_data.footer_indicator) | **New at 2.1.274.** Text of a `◆ <text>` pill in the prompt footer (lines 833343, 1439072). The value is stripped of control characters, trimmed and capped at 6 columns; when unset the bootstrap `client_data.footer_indicator` is used. The same label is carried on `system/init` and the `initialize` response so host UIs can show it (line 108543). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_NONBLOCKING_STDOUT` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_event_loop_stall) | **New at 2.1.261.** When on and the renderer writes to `process.stdout`, replaces `process.stdout.write` with a queued non-blocking writer on the stdout file descriptor that handles backpressure (line 693931). Unset defers to `tengu_event_loop_stall` (default off); a writer that is already installed is reused regardless of the setting. |
| `BAT_THEME` | string | — | Fallback syntax highlight theme (bat/batcat theme name). **Declared but never consumed since 2.1.248**: its export-map entry (line 23812) is its only occurrence, so setting it has no effect at 2.1.282. |
| `CLAUDE_AX_PREPARK_MS` | int (min 0) | `50` (cap `5000`) | **New at 2.1.233.** Milliseconds the accessibility layer waits before pre-parking the cursor. Read as `Math.min(G.CLAUDE_AX_PREPARK_MS ?? 50, 5000)` (line 180106), so the built-in default is 50 ms and any larger value is clamped to 5000 ms. Companion to `CLAUDE_AX_STARTUP_QUIET_MS`, which is clamped the same way. |
| `CLAUDE_AX_STARTUP_QUIET_MS` | int (min 0) | `3000` | How long (ms) the accessibility layer stays quiet after startup before it begins announcing. The effective value is capped at 600000 regardless of what is set (lines 191740, 191775-191776). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ACCESSIBILITY` | bool | `false` | Enable accessibility mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ALT_SCREEN_FULL_REPAINT` | bool | — | Truthy forces a full repaint of the alternate screen buffer on each render instead of incremental updates (v2.1.145) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIEF` | bool | `false` | Brief output mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIEF_UPLOAD` | bool | `false` | Brief mode for uploads |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DECSTBM` | string | — | When truthy, force-enable DECSTBM (top/bottom-margin) scroll-region rendering; bypasses the `tengu_marlin_porch` feature-flag gate |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MOUSE` | tri-bool | `false` | Disable mouse input entirely |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EMIT_TOOL_USE_SUMMARIES` | bool | `false` | Emit tool use summaries |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_FULLSCREEN_UPSELL` | bool | `false` | Force fullscreen upsell display |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_SYNC_OUTPUT` | bool | — | Truthy forces synchronous TTY output, bypassing terminal-based auto-detection |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HIDE_CWD` | bool | — | Truthy hides the current working directory from the TUI footer/status line |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_NATIVE_CURSOR` | bool | — | Truthy enables native terminal cursor rendering instead of the simulated one |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_FLICKER` | tri-bool | — | Control flicker reduction (`true` to enable, `false` to disable) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SCROLL_SPEED` | string | — | Override scroll speed |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE` | bool | `false` | Simplified mode (disables CLAUDE.md, attachments, etc.) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNTAX_HIGHLIGHT` | string | — | Control syntax highlighting (`false` to disable); falls back to `BAT_THEME` |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TUI_JUST_SWITCHED` | string | — | Internal: track TUI mode switch state (set/dropped across process restarts) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TUI_TRIAL` | string | — | **New at 2.1.236.** One-shot handoff marker for the fullscreen TUI trial. Only the exact value `fullscreen` is honoured, and the CLI consumes it destructively: the first read latches the mode and immediately calls `G.unset("CLAUDE_CODE_TUI_TRIAL")` (line 227014), so it never survives into a later read or a child process. The CLI writes it itself when relaunching into the trial (`{ CLAUDE_CODE_TUI_TRIAL: "fullscreen" }`, line 993398) and deletes it from the environment of ordinary respawns (line 757452). Settable by an operator, but designed as an internal one-shot rather than a persistent knob. |

## Self-Hosted Runner

New at 2.1.223-2.1.224. `claude self-hosted-runner` is a long-lived worker that accepts assigned sessions, checks out repositories and spawns child CLI processes, for self-hosted CI/agent fleets. The bundle ships a complete `--help` text for this subsystem, which is the source of the descriptions below.

Read these three rules before using the tables:

1. **`SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_*` are operator knobs**, each read at runner startup and nearly all of them a fallback for an explicit command-line flag.
2. **Most `CLAUDE_RUNNER_*` names are injected into child sessions, not read.** Setting them yourself does nothing; they are values a child *receives*. Only the four listed in the second table are genuine inputs.
3. **Every `*_MS` variable takes milliseconds** while its paired flag takes seconds or minutes — `--drain-wait-sec` pairs with `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DRAIN_WAIT_MS`. The sole exception is `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_RETIRE_AT`, which is an absolute Unix timestamp **in seconds**.

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DRAIN_MARKER_FILE` | — | unset (off) | **New at 2.1.271.** Path of a marker file the host writes before sending SIGTERM to announce a graceful drain: if it exists when the drain begins, the runner reports its exit as a host drain, with the host's force-delete time when the file's first line is an integer in Unix seconds; telemetry only, the drain itself is unchanged (help, lines 636950-636963). Whitespace-only counts as unset and the path is resolved when read (line 636347); the shutdown path reads the marker with a 2000 ms timeout (line 637719), and `--drain-marker-file` writes the resolved path into the environment (line 636710). |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_HOST_CONFIG_SNAPSHOT` | — | disk | **New at 2.1.271.** Where the runner holds its startup snapshot of the host config directory (`SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_HOST_CONFIG_DIR`, default `~/.claude`) that seeds each session's `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`: `disk` copies it under `--base-dir` and verifies it against an in-memory manifest at each session start, `memory` keeps it on the heap up to 64 MiB in total (help, lines 637039-637047; storage choice at line 637646). Accepts `disk` or `memory` case-insensitively, any other non-empty value aborts startup (line 636466), and `--host-config-snapshot` overrides it (line 636614). |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_REMOVE_SESSION_STATE` | — | false | **New at 2.1.268.** When true, the runner deletes a session's per-session state under `<base-dir>/_sessions/` when it ends, best-effort: its Claude config directory (transcript copy, shell snapshots, session env, file history, debug log), staged uploads, per-session gitconfig and working directory (help, lines 637015-637028). Parsed by `sa()` as `1/true/yes/on` or `0/false/no/off`, empty meaning the default `false` and anything else aborting startup (lines 636436, 636488); `--remove-session-state` overrides it and the startup log names the source (line 637257). |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_CLIENT_LABEL` | — | hostname | **New at 2.1.248.** Observability label the self-hosted runner sends at registration and the Anthropic console shows beside it; never used for authorization or routing (help, line 636896). Trimmed, with empty treated as unset, and overridden by `--client-label` (lines 636481, 636778). Unset falls back to `Kc()`, the hostname per the help text (line 637307). |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_STALL_GIVEUP` | — | — (inert; off) | **New at 2.1.242.** Read only through the inert namespace (`Boolean(Dn.CLAUDE_RUNNER_STALL_GIVEUP)`, line 626468), so it is always `false`. The code it would enable stops the runner's git fetch after three consecutive stalled attempts with no client progress or remote activity (reported as `giveup: "mute"`) instead of running all five retries, when the low-speed-limit mode is on (lines 627109, 627186). Pinned off in the shipped build. |
| `VITALS_EMITTER_BIN` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** Absolute path of the `vitals-emitter-guest` binary that reports guest vitals; a relative value logs an error and disables vitals (line 625885). The self-hosted runner's session launch resolves it as `pinned`, where unset disables vitals because PATH is not searched (line 632379); the remote worker uses `search`, which tries PATH for `vitals-emitter-guest` and then `/root/.local/bin/vitals-emitter-guest` (line 1759979). `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_VITALS_EMITTER` or essential-traffic-only mode disables the launcher before this is read. |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DEFER_SHUTDOWN_MAX_MS` | — | 0 (off) | **New at 2.1.238.** Milliseconds to defer shutdown after the first SIGTERM/SIGINT: the runner stops taking work but keeps serving attached sessions, releases every remaining session when the time is up, drains what is still attached after a further grace, and exits as soon as it holds no session; a second signal drains immediately (help, lines 637077-637104). Read with `wt()` (empty, non-numeric or non-positive gives 0, values are capped at 2147483647) and passed as `deferShutdown.maxMs` (lines 637210, 637671). `--defer-shutdown-max-min <m>` writes it as m x 60000 after checking 0 <= m <= 10080 (line 636758). |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_ENVIRONMENT_SECRET` | — | — | Registration secret; the only mandatory setting. Startup aborts when neither this nor `--environment-secret-file` is supplied. Treat as a credential |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_POOL_SECRET` | — | — | Deprecated alias for `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_ENVIRONMENT_SECRET`, still accepted |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_LOCK_TO_ACCOUNT` | — | any account | Locks the runner to one account id at registration so only that account's sessions are assigned, for webhook-driven on-demand spawn |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_BASE_DIR` | — | built-in path | Base directory for repository checkouts |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_EXEC_PATH` | — | this process's binary | Binary to spawn for child sessions |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_HOOKS_DIR` | — | unset | Directory of lifecycle hook scripts (checkout, command, post-session). An absent hook falls through to built-in behavior, so a partial directory is valid |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_HOST_CONFIG_DIR` | — | `~/.claude` | Directory seeded into each session's `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` — settings, `agents/`, `skills/`, with runtime state excluded. Point at an empty directory to disable |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_HEALTH_PORT` | — | built-in | Port for the `/healthz` listener; `0` disables. A non-integer or out-of-range value is a hard startup error |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_LOG_FILE` | — | unset | Tees runner logs to a file in append mode, created mode `0600`; stdout is unchanged |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_TRUST_WORKSPACE` | — | build-dependent | **Security-relevant.** Seeds persisted trust for each session's repo paths so repo-level `.claude/settings.json` `permissions.allow` and `additionalDirectories` are honored by the child. Set to `0` for the stricter gate, where repo-committed grants are dropped with an "Ignoring N permissions.allow" diagnostic and host-level grants must come from the host-config directory. Accepts `1/true/yes/on` or `0/false/no/off`; anything else is a startup error |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_CONFINE_REPO_SETTINGS` | — | `warn` | Repo-committed-settings confine guard: `warn` logs a would-refuse diagnostic and still spawns, `enforce` refuses to spawn, `off` disables the scan. Invalid values fail closed |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_CONFIGURE_GIT` | — | unset | `1` writes `~/.gitconfig` at startup, setting git identity to `Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>` and enabling commit signing via Anthropic's signing service. Without it the image must supply its own identity |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_PUSH_OUTCOME_ON_RELEASE` | — | unset | `1` pushes tracked outcome branches to origin before deleting them on runner-initiated session end, so in-flight commits survive a restart. Carries an explicit caveat: the resume path trusts `refs/heads/<outcome-branch>` on the source remote, so anyone with push access to that ref can place content into a resumed workspace. Adds 30s to the shutdown budget |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_STOP_GRACE_MS` | — | built-in | How long to wait for the Claude process to exit cleanly after a session ends, before force-killing. The post-session hook runs after this |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_POST_SESSION_HOOK_TIMEOUT_MS` | — | built-in | SIGTERM budget for the post-session hook, on every session end including runner shutdown |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DRAIN_WAIT_MS` | — | `0` | On SIGTERM/SIGINT, how long to wait for each session's in-flight turn and background tasks before SIGTERM-ing the session process. Max 86400s |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DRAIN_WAIT_BG_TASKS_MS` | — | — | Deprecated alias for `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DRAIN_WAIT_MS` |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DRAIN_GRACE_MS` | — | `0` | After active sessions finish, how long to stay warm and re-poll the locked account's queue before exiting. `0` exits immediately without polling — effectively one-shot at capacity 1 |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_IDLE_SHUTDOWN_MS` | — | never | Exit the runner if never assigned work within this window (autoscaler scale-down) |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_RETIRE_AT` | — | never | Absolute Unix timestamp **in seconds** at which to release every active session server-side, stop taking work and exit 0. Set it far enough before a hard host kill to cover a typical turn plus the entire shutdown budget |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MS` | — | never | Release a session slot after this long with no user input. The runner exits if this drops it to zero active sessions |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS` | — | 15 min | Release a session slot if the child has not finished initializing — covers a child hung during `--resume` hydration or MCP connect. Cleared once the child emits `system:init`; `0` disables |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_MAX_LIFETIME_MS` | — | never | SIGTERM a session child after this much wall-clock time (runaway backstop). A kill due mid-turn is deferred until the turn finishes |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_MAX_LIFETIME_GRACE_MS` | — | 15 min | Hard cap on that deferral. Documented only inside the `--kill-session-after-min` help entry, never as a flag of its own |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SIGKILL_GRACE_MS` | — | `30000` | Floor for the computed SIGKILL grace, clamped upward so it always covers the post-session hook timeout plus any push-outcome window. Read only through the runner's own name-parameterized reader, so it is invisible to a `process.env.NAME` grep |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DEBUG_DIR` | — | unset | Debug output directory |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_DEBUG_TOKEN_DIR` | — | unset | **Debug only — writes live tokens to disk.** The help text says explicitly not to use it in production |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_TOOLS` | — | — | Runner-side tool configuration; present as an object key rather than a `process.env` read |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_TOOL_NAMES` | — | — | Companion to the above, carrying the tool name list |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SIGKILL_TIMEOUT_MS` | — | — | **Rejected name.** Renamed to `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_STOP_GRACE_MS`; if set, the runner prints the rename and exits 1 |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_RELEASE_IDLE_SESSION_MIN` | — | — | **Rejected name.** Warned about and ignored; use `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MS` |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MIN` | — | — | **Rejected name.** Warned about and ignored; use `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MS` |
| `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_SEC` | — | — | **Rejected name.** Warned about and ignored; use `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MS` |
| `RUNNER_RELEASE_IDLE_SESSION_MIN` | — | — | **Rejected name.** Warned about and ignored; use `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_SESSION_IDLE_MS` |

Runner inputs actually read from the environment:

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_USE_GIT_PROXY` | — | unset | `1` clones through Anthropic's git proxy using the session creator's stored GitHub OAuth token (or the org's GitHub App installation token for bot sessions), so the runner holds no git credentials. Supersedes both URL-rewrite flags |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_SKIP_GIT_VERIFY` | — | unset | Skips git verification during checkout |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_TRUST_CANONICAL_PREWARM` | — | unset | **Security-relevant.** Skips canonical git-state sanitization for a trusted one-shot prewarm. Combined with a non-zero drain grace it is a fatal startup error, because a second session could otherwise reuse a VM whose canonical `.git/` a previous child wrote to, bypassing cross-session isolation. If drain-grace is raised later it is ignored with a warning rather than silently honored |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ACTIVITY_FD` | int (min 3) | unset | File descriptor the child writes activity heartbeats to. The only member of this family predating 2.1.223 |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_FETCH_DEPTH` | string | — | Git fetch depth for session checkouts. Declared but unconsumed at 2.1.222; it has a real reader at 2.1.224, which logs `Ignoring CLAUDE_RUNNER_FETCH_DEPTH='<value>'` for a malformed value |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_DISABLE_AWAITING_ACTION_OVERRIDE` | bool | unset | Suppresses the awaiting-action state override. Also declared-but-unconsumed at 2.1.222 and read (negated) at 2.1.224 |
| `CCR_SHR_SSE_HINTS` | — | unset | Opens the server-sent-events hint stream. An in-code `sseHintsEnabledOverride` takes precedence over it |

Injected into child sessions — **not inputs**; setting them has no effect on the runner: `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ACCOUNT_EMAIL`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ACCOUNT_ID`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_API_BASE_URL`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ATTEMPT`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_CHECKOUT_PATH`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_CLAUDE_BIN`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_CORRELATION_ID`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_DEBUG_LOG_PATH`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_EXIT_REASON`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_FAIL_FAST_FETCH`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_GIT_MOUNT_URL`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ORDER_ID`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ORDER_SERVER_TIME`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_POOL_ID`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_PRIMARY_REPO_REVISION`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_PRIMARY_REPO_URL`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_REPO_REF`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_REPO_SOURCES`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_REPO_URL`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_SESSION_ID`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_SESSION_UUID`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_WORKSPACE_PATHS`, `CLAUDE_RUNNER_WORK_ORDER_FILE`. Note that `CLAUDE_RUNNER_SESSION_ID` is normalized before injection, with the `cse_` prefix rewritten to `session_`.

## Sandbox

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GH_CONFIG_DIR` | string | $XDG_CONFIG_HOME/gh (else ~/.config/gh) | **New at 2.1.251.** First read at 2.1.251 by the plugin-eval sandbox builder through a local env helper; the typed declaration (line 24119) arrived at 2.1.261 and is never read through the accessor. The `claude plugin eval` sandbox builder reads it from the case environment or `process.env` and adds `$GH_CONFIG_DIR/hosts.yml`, plus `~/.config/gh/hosts.yml`, to the credential files it guards; a relative path aborts the build (lines 1901476, 1902788). It is also in the list of credential variables scrubbed from subprocess environments, together with its `INPUT_` form, when `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB` is on or under GitHub Actions (lines 69941, 70137). |
| `CLAUDE_BG_TCC_DISCLAIMED` | string | — | Set by the parent and consumed (then deleted from env) to signal that a background process has been disclaimed from the macOS TCC privacy prompt (v2.1.145) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BASH_SANDBOX_SHOW_INDICATOR` | bool | `false` | Show sandbox indicator in bash |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BUBBLEWRAP` | bool | — | Set to `1` when running inside bubblewrap sandbox |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_HTTP_PROXY_PORT` | — | — | Host HTTP proxy port for sandbox |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_PLATFORM` | string | — | Host platform when running in sandbox |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_SOCKS_PROXY_PORT` | — | — | Host SOCKS proxy port for sandbox |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_ALLOWLIST_ENV` | string | — | Override the sandbox environment allowlist forwarded to MCP server subprocesses |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SANDBOXED` | bool | — | Short-circuit trust check; when truthy, the project is always treated as trusted |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SCRIPT_CAPS` | int | — | Override the sandbox script capability set for bash-tool executions |
| `IS_SANDBOX` | string | — | Set to `1` inside sandbox |

## Agent SDK

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK_DISABLE_MCP_MANIFESTS` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.281.** Read on the Agent SDK side straight from `process.env` through a truthy-string helper (`captureSdkMcpManifests: !Jqn(...)`, line 1663200), not through the typed accessor, whose declaration is otherwise unused. When unset, before the first `initialize` control request the SDK sends `initialize` and `tools/list` to each in-process SDK MCP server and ships those results with the request (line 1661513); setting it skips that capture. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_STARTUP_WORK_GATE` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.281.** Keeps the startup work gate from being armed (line 836239). In a non-interactive `--output-format stream-json` session the gate is armed after setup (line 841156) and holds deferred startup tasks such as permission-context logging, plugin init, telemetry init and idle startup work (lines 841869, 872453, 872570) until a turn starts, the host initializes, a 1000 ms fallback timer fires after the handshake, or shutdown. With this set the gate stays unarmed and held tasks run immediately. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_SCHEDULED_RUN` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.280.** Set by a local host on the processes it starts for its own scheduled runs; stored at startup (line 25571). When set, and the process is neither a child session nor nested under `CLAUDECODE`, a `fireReason` the host declares on a `scheduled-trigger` task notification is honored (line 136150); per the schema the value is kept only if it is 1 to 32 lowercase letters or underscores (line 107188). Stripped from spawned child environments (line 643426), and the settings `env` filter drops it (line 220347). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_SESSION_ID` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.274.** Host-assigned session id. Accepted only when it matches `^local_[0-9a-f-]{8,72}$` (line 150616) and the entrypoint is `claude-desktop`, `claude-desktop-3p` or `local-agent`, in a process that is neither a child session nor nested under `CLAUDECODE` (line 151217). It is recorded as `hostSessionId` in the session registry record (line 151063) and, while `tengu_tidy_fern` is on (default on), written as the `from-session` attribute of cross-session messages (line 222699). A spare process may receive it through `claim_session` (line 1740300); the settings `env` filter drops it (line 220347). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_STARTUP_FAILURE_RESULTS` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.274.** Read directly as `Oe(process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_STARTUP_FAILURE_RESULTS)` (line 179650); the typed declaration (line 24594) is not consumed. When truthy and the run is non-interactive (`-p`/`--print`, `--init-only`, `--sdk-url` or non-TTY stdout) with `--output-format stream-json`, a known startup failure such as `temp_dir_unusable`, `proxy_invalid` or `cli_version_too_old` first writes a zeroed `error_during_execution` result carrying `startup_failure_reason` to stdout, then exits (lines 179674, 618366, 841066). Without it those failures end with stderr only, as the SDK schema text states (line 107559). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DESKTOP_APP_VERSION` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.267.** Version of the Claude Desktop app hosting the CLI, honoured only when `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` is `claude-desktop` or `local-agent` (line 25380). When present, API requests carry `anthropic-client-platform: desktop_app` and `anthropic-client-version: <value>` unless `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS` already sets either header (line 335980), and analytics metadata gains `desktopAppVersion` (line 152516). |
| `AI_AGENT` | bool | `claude-code/harness` | Identifier of the AI agent harness running the CLI; auto-set if missing or already a `claude-code` value, and re-set to `claude-code/agent` inside spawned subagents |
| `CLAUDE_AGENTS_SELECT` | string | — | Pre-selected agent name(s) passed through to a spawned `claude agents` invocation; consumed and re-injected per child |
| `CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK_CLIENT_APP` | string | — | Agent SDK client app identifier |
| `CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK_DISABLE_BUILTIN_AGENTS` | bool | `false` | Disable built-in agent types |
| `CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK_MCP_NO_PREFIX` | bool | `false` | Don't prefix MCP tool names in SDK mode |
| `CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK_VERSION` | string | — | Agent SDK version string |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AGENT` | string | — | Agent name recorded on the session metadata (alongside `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_NAME`/`_LOG`) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EMIT_SESSION_STATE_EVENTS` | bool | `false` | Emit session state change events in SDK mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_APPEND_SUBAGENT_PROMPT` | bool | `false` | Gate for opting-in to appending `appendSubagentSystemPrompt` (from the Agent SDK options) onto every Task-tool subagent's system prompt (propagates to nested subagents) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` | string | `cli` | Entry point identifier (`cli`, `sdk-ts`, `sdk-py`, `sdk-cli`, `local-agent`, `claude-desktop`, `remote`, `mcp`, `claude-vscode`, `claude-code-github-action`) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORK_SUBAGENT` | tri-bool | — | Truthy forces the fork-based subagent execution path (otherwise governed by the `tengu_copper_fox` flag). **Settings-injectable as of 2.1.232.** |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_INCLUDE_PARTIAL_MESSAGES` | bool | `false` | Include partial stream events/messages in SDK output |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SDK_HAS_OAUTH_REFRESH` | bool | `false` | Signal that the SDK host can refresh OAuth tokens on behalf of Claude Code (gated to specific entry points). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.275 it joined the child-environment strip registry (line 643375). |
| `SDK_NATIVE_BIN` | string | `claude` | Path/name of the native Claude binary the Agent SDK spawns |

## Teams / Teammates

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_SKILL_GUIDANCE` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.281.** Appends a paragraph to the coordinator's worker-tools context telling it to load a listed skill with its Skill tool before briefing a worker on work that skill covers, and to name the skill in the worker's prompt because only workers execute skills (lines 370095, 370199). Applied only when the coordinator's tool list includes the Skill tool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_FORCE_WORKER_INHERIT_MODEL` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.248.** In coordinator mode (`CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_MODE`), the Agent tool discards any `model` argument so workers always inherit the session model (line 759992), and the tool's `model` parameter description says the parameter is ignored (line 759907). The coordinator system prompt also tells the model not to set `model` when this or `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE` is set (line 370220). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_WORKER_CHECKIN_SECONDS` | int (min 1, max 86400, digits only) | unset (no check-ins) | **New at 2.1.246.** In coordinator mode, seconds between `Worker check-in` task notifications that tell the coordinator how many minutes ago it dispatched still-running workers and list them; the value is multiplied by 1000 for a repeating timer (lines 586487, 586587). Unset means no timer; the timer stops when no workers remain and is re-armed when the value changes. The 86400 ceiling is one day; outside coordinator mode `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_WORKER_CHECKIN_SECONDS` takes this role when `CLAUDE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_TASKS` is set. Settings-injectable from settings.json `env`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_EXTRA_TOOLS` | — | `""` | Comma-separated MCP tool names always kept available to a coordinator agent beyond its default filtered tool set; blank entries ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_MODE` | — | — | Marks the current process as a team coordinator; set to `"1"` on the coordinator and inherited by child processes (v2.1.154) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_PROPAGATE_NESTED_MEMORY` | — | effectively unset | Would let a coordinator propagate pending nested-memory triggers to its parent context (line 565554). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. The `propagateNestedMemory` step consequently never runs. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS` | bool | `false` | Enable experimental agent teams |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLAN_MODE_REQUIRED` | bool | `false` | Force plan mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLAN_V2_AGENT_COUNT` | int | — | Number of agents in plan v2 |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLAN_V2_EXPLORE_AGENT_COUNT` | int | — | Number of explore agents in plan v2 |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS` | bool | — | Truthy enables synchronous skill syncing for teammate/agent spawns (v2.1.154) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS` | int | — | Timeout in ms to wait for skill sync to finish; parsed via `parseInt(..., 10)` (v2.1.154) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEAM_TEARDOWN_PARK_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1000, max 60000) | `10000` | How long (ms) team teardown waits for parked work to finish before forcing shutdown (line 1113932). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it became settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEAMMATE_COMMAND` | — | — | Command to spawn teammate processes |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TMUX_PREFIX` | string | — | Tmux prefix key |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TMUX_PREFIX_CONFLICTS` | bool | — | Whether tmux prefix conflicts exist |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TMUX_SESSION` | string | — | Tmux session name for teams |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TMUX_TRUECOLOR` | raw string | — | Override truecolor detection for tmux sessions. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it was retyped from bool to raw string. |
| `CLAUDE_INTERNAL_ASSISTANT_TEAM_NAME` | string | — | Anthropic-internal assistant team name. Deleted from the environment immediately after being read so it is not inherited. |

## Plugins / Cowork

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.280.** Extra plugin folders loaded as inline plugins, separated by the platform path delimiter (`:` or `;`); each entry is trimmed and `~/` is expanded, entries that fail the absolute-local-path check are skipped with a warning, and the normalized list is written back into the variable (lines 709921, 709932). The main CLI merges the result with `--plugin-dir` and drops folders already given with `--plugin-dir-no-mcp` (line 872875), while `claude agents` reads `process.env` directly for the same purpose (line 1057). It is forwarded to background-session children (line 706842) but stripped from other spawned child environments (reg:strip; line 663796), and project-scoped settings files cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_REUSE_WITHIN_MS` | int (min 0, max 86400000) | unset (no reuse) | **New at 2.1.268.** Reuse window for the startup plugin-sync round (line 414298). When positive and the bucket's `.last-complete-round` marker has an mtime younger than this many milliseconds, the startup round skips re-syncing, reconciles sidecars from the existing tree and logs `plugins_sync_round_reused` (line 415051). Unset or `0` never reuses. The 86400000 ceiling is one day. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_FUNCTION_HOOKS` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_plugin_hooks_modules) | **New at 2.1.259.** Overrides the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` gate (default false) that turns on hooks modules (function hooks) for installed plugins (lines 307934, 307945). Installed plugins still load no hooks module when hooks are turned off by `disableAllHooks`, `allowManagedHooksOnly` or a policy, or under `--bare`; built-in plugins load regardless (lines 490117, 921414). The plugin test runner forces it to `1` in the child environment (line 923251); it is in the registry of variables settable through settings.json `env`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIR_WATCH` | tri-bool | on interactive, off non-interactive | **New at 2.1.259.** Forces the plugin-directory file watcher on or off (line 429390); when unset it is on for interactive sessions and off for non-interactive ones (`!Ae()`, where `Ae()` is `!isInteractive()`). When off, the watcher setup returns before watching any plugin folder or collection (line 489815). Project-scoped settings files cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKILL_ATTRIBUTION` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.259.** JSON object (at most 262144 bytes) mapping skill directory paths to `{skill_id, server_plugin_id}`; `skill_id` must match `^skill_(?:staging_\|local_)?[A-Za-z0-9]{1,64}$` and `server_plugin_id` `^plugin_(?:staging_\|local_)?[A-Za-z0-9]{1,64}$`, and invalid entries or fields are ignored with a debug message (line 417360). Keys are resolved and NFC-normalized (lower-cased on Windows) and looked up by the user, project and plugin skill loaders (lines 541181, 542574). Deleted from subprocess environments (line 113575); project-scoped settings files cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_SURFACE` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.257.** Names the cloud-session surface; the only value the code tests is `tag`. With `tag`, plugin download requests add `included_default_marketplaces=claude-tag-plugins` (line 292109) and plugin search requests send `included_default_marketplaces: ["claude-tag-plugins"]` (line 1110438). It is on the host-injected list that is ignored in any settings-file `env` block (line 220347). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_CONFINED` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Confinement mode for the plugin evaluation harness, which deletes the names it strips from the child environment and then sets this to `1` (line 1903563). It ignores allow rules other than CLI-argument ones (lines 210511, 216167), ignores `dangerouslyDisableSandbox` and empties the sandbox allowed-domains list (lines 274274, 533449), refuses worktree isolation and cloud sessions (lines 460900, 545054, 554755), and turns read prompts and hard-linked source files into denials (lines 724534, 1554601). It is stripped from spawned child environments, and settings `env` cannot set it (line 220250). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_ATTRIBUTION` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.246.** JSON object (at most 65536 bytes) mapping plugin directory paths to attribution records with optional `server_plugin_id` (must match `^plugin_(?:staging_\|local_)?[A-Za-z0-9]{1,64}$`), `marketplace_name` and `installation_preference`; invalid JSON, a non-object, or an invalid field is ignored with a log line (line 192446). An entry here takes precedence over the plugin's `.meta.json` sidecar (line 192450). Deleted from tool subprocess environments (line 113574); project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOOKS_SAME_THREAD` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.242.** Selects where plugin function hooks run: set, in a `same-thread` host inside the main JS thread; unset, in a `worker` host on a Worker thread (lines 486447, 488361). The plugin test harness (`test(name, { plugins })`) writes it to `true` itself (line 923203). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COWORK_FRAME_ARTIFACTS` | bool | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Marks the session as running inside a cowork frame that renders artifacts; consumed as `Nqe.setCoworkFrameArtifacts(X.CLAUDE_CODE_COWORK_FRAME_ARTIFACTS)` (line 61505), beside the sibling reads for `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION` and `CLAUDECODE`. Grouped with `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` / `CLAUDE_CODE_IS_COWORK` in the cowork env set (line 454716) and forwarded to child processes by the subprocess-forwarding registry (`_Br`, line 827576); one sanitizer (`bBr()`, line 827567) deletes it from an environment case-insensitively, so a differently-cased spelling is dropped too. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_BINARY_ASSETS` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_plugin_binary_assets` experiment, enabling platform-specific plugin binary assets (darwin and linux, arm64/x64, musl). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_CACHE_DIR` | string | — | Plugin cache directory |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_GIT_TIMEOUT_MS` | int | — | Git timeout for plugin operations (ms) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_KEEP_MARKETPLACE_ON_FAILURE` | bool | `false` | Keep marketplace state on plugin failure |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_PREFER_HTTPS` | bool | — | Truthy makes plugin git operations prefer HTTPS over SSH (also implied by `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE`) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_SEED_DIR` | string | — | Plugin seed directory |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_USE_ZIP_CACHE` | bool | `false` | Use zip cache for plugins |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PLUGIN_MCP_SERVERS` | bool | — | Skips MCP server discovery for plugins entirely, except plugins named in `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PLUGIN_MCP_SERVERS_EXCEPT`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PLUGIN_MCP_SERVERS_EXCEPT` | string | — | Comma-separated plugin names exempted from `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PLUGIN_MCP_SERVERS`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS` | bool | — | Enables syncing installed plugins from the remote plugin registry; also auto-enabled when session-ref syncing is active. Forwarded to child sessions. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS_BUFFERED_DOWNLOAD` | bool | — | Buffers plugin downloads fully in memory before writing, instead of streaming to disk. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS_DOWNLOAD_STALL_MS` | int (min 1) | `60000` | How long (ms) a plugin-sync download may make no progress before it is treated as stalled (lines 575377, 575608). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS_INSTALL_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | `30000` | Timeout in milliseconds for installing a plugin during sync. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS_MCP_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 0) | `10000` | Timeout in milliseconds for starting a synced plugin's MCP server. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGIN_INSTALL` | bool | `false` | Synchronous plugin installation |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGIN_INSTALL_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | — | Timeout for synchronous plugin installation |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_COWORK_PLUGINS` | bool | `false` | Enable cowork plugins |
| `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA` | — | injected for plugin execution | Per-plugin data directory, exposed to plugin commands and templated config expansion |
| `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` | — | injected for plugin execution | Absolute path to the active plugin root, exposed to plugin commands and templated config expansion |
| `FORCE_AUTOUPDATE_PLUGINS` | bool | `false` | Force auto-update plugins |

## Memory & Prompt History

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.269.** Turns on the post-turn memory-extraction pass configured by `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY_CONFIG` (line 154546); with a valid config the pass runs regardless of the `tengu_passport_quail` gate (line 154749). If it is set but the config is missing or invalid, a debug message says the pass is off and `ccr_memory_extract` logs `config_invalid` (line 799238); the pass is also skipped when the CCR memory MCP server is not mounted (line 799481). A settings `env` block cannot set it (line 220347). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY_CONFIG` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.269.** JSON object configuring the post-turn memory pass (line 154552): `instruction` (1-32768 chars, required), `every_n_turns` (1-100, default 5), `max_turns` (1-8, default 4), `idle_flush_s` (0-86400, default 120) and `extract_transport` (`tools` \| `text`, default `tools`). Read only when `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY` is on; a JSON or schema failure turns the pass off. A settings `env` block cannot set it (line 220347). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY_SYNC` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.269.** Runs the post-turn memory pass synchronously: it forces `every_n_turns` to 1 and `idle_flush_s` to 0 (line 154556), and the turn awaits the extraction instead of leaving it in the background (line 811822). No effect unless `CLAUDE_CODE_POST_TURN_MEMORY` and a valid config are set. A settings `env` block cannot set it (line 220347). |
| `SYSTEM_REMINDER_MEMORY_CONTEXT` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.267.** Selects the `ccr` leg of the Cowork memory-context attachment for sessions whose entrypoint is not `remote_cowork` or `remote_cowork_trigger` (those select it without the variable): the check also requires an SDK URL plus a session access token and the `tengu_misty_anchor` gate, default false (lines 403227, 403260, 140198). Settings-file `env` entries of this name are dropped (line 220347), and the remote spare-process server deletes it from its own environment at start (lines 879478, 879609). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MEMORY_RO_UNSAVED_NOTICE` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.242.** Skips the unsaved-write scan that memory sync runs for a read-only (`ro`) store mount when the sync call allows deletes (line 314338). Without it the scan lists local files whose SHA-256 differs from the remote copy, reports each path once per mount as `unsavedReadOnlyPaths` and logs `tengu_memory_sync_ro_unsaved`; with it set the scan returns no paths. It is in the registry of variables settable through settings.json `env`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ADDITIONAL_DIRECTORIES_CLAUDE_MD` | string | `false` | Scan additional directories for CLAUDE.md |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_EVALUATE_MEMORY` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_MEMORY_SURVEY` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_BASE_URL` | — | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Intended base-URL override for the memory API host. Occurs exactly once bundle-wide — as a member of the memory-API name pair `cFc` (line 78908), which is spread into both the provider-sensitive and proxy/host-managed registries — and has no reader at all, so setting it configures nothing at this tag. Not a typed declaration. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_TOKEN` | — | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Intended bearer token for the memory API host; already registered as a secret for log redaction (`vRs`, line 165008) beside `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. Its single read — `pp.CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_API_TOKEN` in `sbt()` (line 149167), which would attach an `Authorization: Bearer` header — is inert twice over: it sits below an `if (!BLe()) return …` early return where `BLe()` is `pun() !== undefined` and `pun()` is `function pun() { return; }`, so the line is unreachable; and `pp` is `trs({}, null)` (line 42100), a namespace whose backing object is empty and whose prototype is `null`, so the property would read `undefined` even if reached. Provider-sensitive and proxy/host-managed. Not a typed declaration. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_PUSH_DELETE_MODE` | enum corroborate \| immediate \| never | `corroborate` | How memory deletions are pushed upstream: "immediate" pushes at once, "corroborate" waits for confirmation from another source first, "never" suppresses deletion pushes. When unset the `tengu_mem_push_delete_mode` flag decides, itself defaulting to `corroborate`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PROMPT_HISTORY` | bool | `false` | Skip saving prompt history |
| `CLAUDE_COWORK_MEMORY_EXTRA_GUIDELINES` | string | — | Extra guidelines for cowork memory |
| `CLAUDE_COWORK_MEMORY_GUIDELINES` | string | — | Extra guideline text inserted into the cowork memory system prompt |
| `CLAUDE_COWORK_MEMORY_INDEX_CONTENT` | string | — | Override content for the cowork auto-memory index; empty string disables the index, otherwise parsed in place of the file |
| `CLAUDE_COWORK_MEMORY_PATH_OVERRIDE` | string | — | Override cowork memory path |
| `CLAUDE_MEMORY_STORES` | string | — | JSON-encoded memory-store configuration; parsed at load time and rejected with an error if it is not valid JSON (v2.1.145) |

## Idle, Resume & Background

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PARKED_RUN_BEFORE_CLEAR` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.282.** Parked-permission lane option `runsBeforeClear` (line 1222822). When set, an approved parked permission's call runs at once; unset, the call first waits, with a bound, for confirmation that the parked record was deleted, and is not run (with a warning) if that is not confirmed (line 1230276). |
| `CLAUDE_BG_WORKSPACE_TRUSTED` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.281.** Carries the dispatching terminal's home-directory trust into a background or reattached session. The dispatcher writes `1` into the reattach environment when that trust exists (line 706864), and the daemon re-derives it from the home-trust dialog state (line 703886). In the session it counts only when no trust is recorded for the directory and the original working directory resolves to the home directory; otherwise hooks and project-settings `env` stay off with a warning (line 870459). It is in the strip registry, so it is removed from spawned child environments (line 643390). |
| `CLAUDE_RELAUNCH_SESSION_ADD_DIRS` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.281.** Hands session-scoped additional working directories across a relaunch. The relaunching process writes the directories added during the session as a JSON array (lines 389034, 903758); the new process reads it once, unsets it, keeps the string entries and passes them to permission setup as `relaunchSessionAddDirs` (lines 837206, 837249, 748860). Stripped from spawned child environments (lines 876706, 879151, 879609), and skipped when it appears in a settings file's `env` (line 220268). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_REASON` | string | interrupted_turn | **New at 2.1.268.** Host-supplied reason stamped as `resume_reason` on the stream frames and result of a turn that is automatically re-run after a worker restart (`CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN`), e.g. `host_draining` or `checkpoint_restore` (schema lines 107390, 107562). The value must match `^[a-z_]{1,64}$`; anything else falls back to `interrupted_turn` (line 548174). Stripped from spawned child environments (reg:strip). |
| `CLAUDE_BG_DISPATCHER_RATE_LIMIT_TIER` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Rate-limit tier of the account that dispatched a background session; the dispatching CLI writes it into the session's reattach environment from its OAuth account record (lines 113156, 706862). Inside a `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_KIND=bg` session it is the fallback tier when the session's own account record has none, accepted only if it matches `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,63}$` and ignored when `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` is set (lines 113169, 163265). It is stripped from other child environments (line 643390), and a project-scope settings file cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_BG_DISPATCHER_SUBSCRIPTION_TYPE` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.251.** Subscription type of the account that dispatched a background session; the dispatching CLI writes it into the session's reattach environment from its OAuth account record (lines 113153, 706862). Inside a `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_KIND=bg` session it is the fallback subscription type when the session's own account record has none, accepted only as `max`, `pro`, `team` or `enterprise` and ignored when `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` is set (lines 113168, 163190). It is stripped from other child environments (line 643390), and a project-scope settings file cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_WORKER_CHECKIN_SECONDS` | int (min 1, max 86400, digits only) | unset | **New at 2.1.248.** Interval, in seconds (multiplied by 1000), of the periodic worker check-in notification about still-running background workers. Consulted only when `CLAUDE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_TASKS` is set and coordinator mode is off; in coordinator mode `CLAUDE_CODE_COORDINATOR_WORKER_CHECKIN_SECONDS` is used instead (line 586492). Unset means no check-in timer is armed (line 586587). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_TOLERATES_CONTEXT_APPENDS` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.242.** Lets interrupted-turn resume tolerate context appends: a human- or SDK-origin user message whose text consists only of `<system-reminder>` blocks is treated as a turn companion rather than a new prompt (line 595547), so the resume check walks past it (line 548193). It also enables the `dHo` message-list pass (line 548348) and is copied into the parked-permission options as `toleratesContextAppends` (line 1222823). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOLD_REPORT_PARK_AT_INIT` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.239.** Takes effect only together with `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN`: the pair sets `reportParkAtInit` on the remote worker transport (line 1196411). At worker init the transport then derives the parked state from the stored worker metadata and, when a permission is parked, reports `worker_status: "requires_action"` with its details instead of the default init report that clears `pending_action` and `pending_actions` to null (line 1144729). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PARKED_STOP_RETIRES` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.239.** Parked-permission lane option `parkedStopRetires` (line 1222821). When set, an interrupt that supersedes a parked permission retires the parked turn's tool calls in the transcript even when the permission was not being held (line 1221866); unset, an interrupt retires them only for a held permission. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ADOPT_UNDERIVABLE_PARKED_PERMISSION` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.238.** When a headless session resumes an interrupted turn (`CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN`), a parked permission prompt whose tool-use ID cannot be derived from the restored transcript is normally ignored; with this flag it is adopted when the classifier reports it `underivable_adoptable` (line 1222750). If the adopted prompt is then denied or goes unanswered, the interrupted turn's tool-use IDs are retired and the turn is not re-run (line 1221883). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_TIMEOUT_MS` | — | unset | **Not previously catalogued.** Shortens how long the main agent waits in the foreground on a Bash or PowerShell command that can be auto-backgrounded; when that timeout fires the command is moved to the background instead of being killed (line 593723). Read from the environment passed in, which defaults to `process.env` (line 308355). The effective timeout is `min(requested, max(value, 2000))` ms, so it never lengthens the requested timeout and has a 2-second floor. Non-numeric or non-positive values are ignored; subagents and non-backgroundable commands are unaffected (lines 593626, 1109246). |
| `CLAUDE_AFK_COUNTDOWN_MS` | int | `20000` | Milliseconds of visible countdown shown before the AFK timeout fires; clamped to at most `CLAUDE_AFK_TIMEOUT_MS`. |
| `CLAUDE_AFK_TIMEOUT_MS` | int | `60000` | Idle milliseconds before an interactive prompt auto-resolves via the away-from-keyboard handler. Setting it also forces the AFK machinery on. |
| `CLAUDE_ASYNC_AGENT_STALL_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1, max 2147483647, digits only) | `600000` | Stall timeout (ms) for async-agent turns; turns with no progress for this long are considered stalled. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it became settings-injectable and changed its constraint to `{ min: 1, max: 2147483647, digitsOnly: true }`. |
| `CLAUDE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_TASKS` | bool | `false` | Auto-spawn background tasks |
| `CLAUDE_BG_AUTH_SNAPSHOT_PATH` | string | — | Path to an auth snapshot file used to seed a background job's credentials; consumed and deleted on read |
| `CLAUDE_BG_BACKEND` | string | — | Set to `daemon` to run background tasks via the daemon backend (ignores SIGHUP so the daemon survives terminal disconnects) |
| `CLAUDE_BG_CLAIM_AUTH` | string | — | One-shot auth secret passed to a background session so it can claim its slot. Deleted from the environment immediately after being read. |
| `CLAUDE_BG_ISOLATION` | string | — | Background-session isolation mode; `worktree` instructs the subagent to call `EnterWorktree` before any file or command action |
| `CLAUDE_BG_MEMORY_TOGGLED_OFF` | string | — | Presence (`!== undefined`) signals that auto-memory was toggled off for this background session; consumed and deleted from `process.env` on read (v2.1.154). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.281 it joined the child-environment strip registry (line 643375). |
| `CLAUDE_BG_POST_CLEAR_RESPAWN` | bool | — | Marks a background-daemon respawn that follows a reset to a new session id (as opposed to an interrupted-turn resume), suppressing the agent-not-found check on that respawn. |
| `CLAUDE_BG_PTY_AUTH` | string | — | One-shot auth secret for the background PTY channel. Deleted from the environment immediately after being read. |
| `CLAUDE_BG_RENDEZVOUS_SOCK` | string | — | Unix socket path used by background jobs to rendezvous with the parent daemon; consumed and deleted on read |
| `CLAUDE_BG_RV_AUTH` | string | — | One-shot auth secret for the background rendezvous socket. Deleted from the environment immediately after being read. |
| `CLAUDE_BG_SESSION_PERMISSION_RULES` | string | — | JSON-encoded permission rule overrides applied to a background session (`CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_KIND=bg`); the parent injects it via `JSON.stringify(...)` and the worker consumes and deletes it from `process.env` on read (v2.1.146). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.281 it joined the child-environment strip registry (line 643375). |
| `CLAUDE_BG_SOCKET_TOKENS_PATH` | string | — | Path to the file holding background-session socket tokens. Deleted from the environment immediately after being read. |
| `CLAUDE_BG_SOURCE` | string | `shell` | Origin label for a background session (e.g. `shell`); stripped before passing env to children |
| `CLAUDE_BG_STARTUP_WEDGE_MS` | int | `45000` | Milliseconds to delay (`unref`'d timer) before the background job's startup wedge fires |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DAEMON_COLD_START` | bool | — | Overrides daemon cold-start behavior; accepts `transient` (spawn for this login) or `ask` (prompt to install persistently) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GOAL_CHECKIN_MINUTES` | int (min 0, max 10080, digits only) | `30` | **New at 2.1.234.** Minutes between goal check-in notifications while background work is running; the value is multiplied by 60000 to give the interval (line 543137). The whole feature is gated on the `tengu_saffron_wren` experiment, which defaults to on — when that flag is off the interval is forced to 0 and no check-in fires regardless of this variable. The declared maximum of 10080 is exactly one week. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_IDLE_THRESHOLD_MINUTES` | int | `75` | Minutes of idle time before idle behavior triggers |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_IDLE_TOKEN_THRESHOLD` | int | `100000` | Token threshold for idle detection |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_LOOP_PERSISTENT` | bool | — | Truthy enables persistent loop behavior in long-running session loops |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PRINT_BG_WAIT_CEILING_MS` | int (min 0) | `600000` | Upper bound (ms) on how long `--print` mode waits for background tasks before sweeping and terminating them, with a "Background tasks still running after Ns; terminating." notice on stderr. Setting `0` removes the ceiling and waits indefinitely (lines 1111654, 1111715, 1116665). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN` | bool | — | Resume an interrupted turn/session by identifier or token |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN_MAX_AGE_MS` | string | `3600000` | Maximum age in milliseconds of an interrupted turn that is still eligible for auto-resume; older turns are discarded. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_PROMPT` | string | `Continue from where you left off.` | Prompt text injected when a session is resumed via `--resume` |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_SOURCE_ALIVE` | string | — | Descriptor telling a resumed session whether its source process is still alive, so it can decide between takeover and cooperation. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_THRESHOLD_MINUTES` | int | `70` | Minutes threshold for resume-interrupted-turn eligibility |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_TOKEN_THRESHOLD` | int | `100000` | Token threshold for resume-interrupted-turn eligibility |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` | string | — | Current session UUID; re-randomized on `/clear`-style resets and propagated to child processes |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_KIND` | string | — | Session classification; `bg` marks a background-agent session and forces full-screen / bg-specific code paths |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_LOG` | string | — | Path to write the session log file for the current session |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_NAME` | string | — | Human-readable session label recorded on session metadata |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SPAWN_TIMESTAMP_MS` | int | — | Spawn timestamp (ms) propagated to a child/background process; parsed via `parseInt(..., 10)` and used as a fallback spawn time (v2.1.154) |
| `CLAUDE_JOB_DIR` | string | — | Directory representing the current background job; basename is used as the `jobId` in session metadata |
| `CLAUDE_SERVE_DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | — | Declared but never consumed (reserved). It has a typed declaration and a registry export, but no call site anywhere in the bundle. |

## Miscellaneous

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_GATEWAY_LINEAGE` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.281.** Marks a process as descended from a host-managed gateway session. With a non-empty `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_CREDS_FILE` it satisfies the host-gateway check without a desktop entrypoint: `dW()` requires `CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST` and `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_GATEWAY` plus either a `claude-desktop`/`claude-desktop-3p` entrypoint or this lineage pair (line 25423). The CLI passes it on as `1` to background jobs and teammates when `dW()` holds (lines 705800, 708732, 1606262), deletes it with the other host-managed provider keys (line 663823), and the settings `env` filter drops it (line 220250). Member of the subprocess-forwarding registry. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_CLASSIFIER_OVERLAP` | — | false | **New at 2.1.280.** Would let auto-mode classifier calls for the same key run concurrently in overlapping lanes instead of being serialized (line 408072). Read only through the inert namespace (`Dn`, line 408018), so the value is always `undefined`, falls back to `false`, and classifier calls stay serialized. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_START_ANNOUNCEMENTS_BEFORE_PROMPT` | bool | off | **New at 2.1.277.** When set, the first incoming turn that follows an assistant message and carries session-start announcement attachments plus a user message gets those attachments placed `before_closing_line` (lines 1224586, 409493). The latch clears after one placement or once a turn starts a model query; models without system-message support are skipped, and the outcome is logged as `session_start_placement`. |
| `CLAUDE_GATEWAY_DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1, max 2147000000, digits only) | 25000 | **New at 2.1.274.** Drain window for the Claude apps gateway on SIGTERM or SIGINT: it refuses new connections and gives open requests this many milliseconds to finish (`\|\| 25000`, line 1190624). A hard exit follows 3000 ms after the window, and a second signal exits at once (lines 1190634, 1190641). The 2.1.274 changelog entry names it (line 867882). |
| `CLAUDE_ARTIFACT_HOST_GRANT` | raw string | unset | **New at 2.1.271.** JSON grant, set by the app that launches the session, listing the local `host:<name>` MCP servers and tools an Artifact may declare: `{"v":1,"servers":[{"server":"host:…","tools":[…]}]}` (parsed at line 713339). Unset means declarations are not narrowed; an unparseable value makes a publish that declares any `host:` server fail; otherwise servers and tools outside the grant are dropped from the published capabilities (line 719097). The same grant bounds the file and page publish lanes and creating an Artifact from a type (lines 720521, 1575130). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_SERVER` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.271.** Opts the auto-mode classifier into or out of running server-side. The value is parsed as truthy (`1`/`true`/`yes`/`on`); any other set value counts as off. It is latched per session (line 469808). On Bedrock, Vertex, Foundry, Anthropic AWS/Google Cloud and gateways it overrides the default, which is `!CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS` or `CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST`, and yields server-side with a local fallback. On a first-party connection an explicit off disables the server classifier, and unset defers to `tengu_smooth_chipmunk` (line 470190). Copied into the environment of respawned sessions (line 706833). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ATTENDED` | tri-bool | unset | **New at 2.1.269.** Written by the parent into each child shell environment as `1` or `0` depending on whether the parent session is attended (line 318710), and read back by a child `claude` as `spawnedByAttendedSession` (line 25566). `isHeadlessToolCallOfClaudeCodeSession` requires it to be true together with `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION` and no TTY (line 127558); `claude plugin eval init` uses that to allow its interview without a TTY (line 1908554). Stripped from spawned child environments (reg:strip). |
| `CLAUDE_CHROME_PAIRED_DEVICE_ID` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.268.** Browser-extension device ID for Claude in Chrome, used as the paired device when global config has no stored `chromeExtension.pairedDeviceId` (line 937138). Read as `r?.CLAUDE_CHROME_PAIRED_DEVICE_ID ?? a.CLAUDE_CHROME_PAIRED_DEVICE_ID`; a value not matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$` is ignored with a warning (line 937092). When it is set but no pairing is stored, a failed connection reports that the browser chosen for this session could not be reached (line 937111); a settings-file `env` block cannot set it (line 220347). |

| `CLAUDECODE` | bool | — | Set to `1` in spawned processes |
| `CLAUDE_AFTER_LAST_COMPACT` | string | `false` | Flag set after last compaction |
| `CLAUDE_AGENTS_AUTO_RELAUNCHED_AT` | — | — | Unix-ms timestamp of the last auto-relaunch into the agents launcher after an update; throttles repeat auto-relaunches to at most once per 6 hours. |
| `CLAUDE_AX_SCREEN_READER` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state accessibility override forcing screen-reader mode on or off instead of relying on autodetection. |
| `CLAUDE_BASH_MAINTAIN_PROJECT_WORKING_DIR` | bool | `false` | Maintain working directory between bash calls |
| `CLAUDE_BRIDGE_REATTACH_OUTBOUND_ONLY` | bool | — | Set to `"1"` by the parent to instruct a re-attaching Chrome-bridge connection to operate outbound-only; consumed and deleted from `process.env` on read (v2.1.154) |
| `CLAUDE_BYTE_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | — | Milliseconds a response byte stream may stall before the byte watchdog aborts it. |
| `CLAUDE_CHROME_CLASSIFIER_FLOOR` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state override for the Chrome automode classifier floor; unset defers to the tengu_cowork_chrome_automode_default flag. |
| `CLAUDE_CHROME_PERMISSION_MODE` | string | — | Permission mode for Chrome integration |
| `CLAUDE_CLIENT_PRESENCE_FILE` | string | — | Path to the presence file used to detect whether a client UI is currently attached. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_3P_PROBE_WROTE_OPUS_DEFAULT` | string | — | Records the Opus model id a third-party provider probe wrote as the default, so a later change to `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL` can be told apart from the probe's own value. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_3P_PROBE_WROTE_SONNET_DEFAULT` | string | — | Records the Sonnet model id a third-party provider probe wrote as the default, used the same way as the Opus counterpart. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ACCOUNT_TAGGED_ID` | string | — | Override the tagged account identifier emitted in OpenTelemetry metric attributes |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ACTION` | string | `false` | Running as a GitHub Action |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ACT_DONT_REDERIVE` | tri-bool | `true` | Tri-state override for the `tengu_cedar_lantern` experiment controlling the "act, don't re-derive" system-prompt section. Enabled by default. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AGENT_VIEW_RELAUNCH` | — | — | One-shot internal marker set to `1` when the CLI relaunches itself into the agents view; read once then deleted from the environment. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT` | string | — | Tri-state override for the Artifact tool: truthy force-enables it, an explicitly false value disables it, unset defers to defaults and settings. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACTS_API_BASE_URL` | string | — | Base URL override for the Artifacts API; part of the recognized base-URL set. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_AUTO_OPEN` | string | — | Controls whether a created artifact opens automatically. Auto-open is skipped regardless for background, teammate, remote, desktop-pane and VS Code sessions. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ATTRIBUTION_HEADER` | string | — | Custom attribution header |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_EXTERNAL_PERMISSIONS` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_MODEL` | string | — | No accessor reads its value, so setting it still configures nothing. It is not inert to the runtime, however: since 2.1.222 it belongs to the provider-sensitive set (`Apg`) and the model-override strip set (`Jfg`), so a host-managed CLI refuses it from a `settings.json` `env` block and deletes it from `process.env` on the warm-spare claim path. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BASE_REF` | string | — | Override base git ref for diffs |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BASE_REFS` | string | — | Override base git refs (comma-separated list) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BG_CLASSIFIER_MODEL` | string | — | No accessor reads its value, so setting it still configures nothing. As of 2.1.222 it joined the provider-sensitive set (`Apg`) and the model-override strip set (`Jfg`), with the same handling as `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_MODE_MODEL`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BG_TASKS_REPORT_RUNNING` | tri-bool | — | Controls whether active teammates, running background tasks or pending notifications keep the session reported as running rather than idle. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.269 it was retyped from bool to tri-bool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_SESSION_ID` | string | — | Session id for a bridge-attached session. Written by the parent and removed when the bridge detaches. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION` | bool | — | Marks the process as a child session spawned by another Claude Code instance (for example in a tmux pane), which suppresses duplicate transcript persistence. Also read back out of the tmux global environment. |

| `CLAUDE_CODE_DESIGN_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | string | — | OAuth client id for the Claude Design integration; required when the build has none baked in. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DONT_INHERIT_ENV` | bool | `false` | Don't inherit env vars in spawned processes |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EAGER_FLUSH` | bool | `false` | Flush persisted session data eagerly after writes |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENHANCED_TELEMETRY_BETA` | bool | inherits `ENABLE_ENHANCED_TELEMETRY_BETA` when unset | Alternate flag name for enhanced telemetry beta |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND` | string | — | Environment kind label (e.g. `bridge`) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXECPATH` | — | — | Path to the Claude Code executable, exported so child processes and shell integrations can re-invoke the same binary. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXIT_AFTER_FIRST_RENDER` | bool | `false` | Exit after the first UI render; useful for smoke tests and harnesses |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXIT_AFTER_STOP_DELAY` | int | — | Delay process exit after stop/termination logic |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_OBSERVER_AGENTS` | bool | — | Gate for experimental observer agents, combined with the `tengu_observer_agents_enabled` experiment (default on). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXTRA_BODY` | string | — | Extra JSON body to include in API requests |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EXTRA_METADATA` | string | — | JSON object merged into the emitted `user_id` telemetry payload |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FLEETVIEW_SIMPLE` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_fleetview_simple` experiment, switching FleetView to a simplified UI. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_BRIDGE` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_MID_CONVERSATION_SYSTEM` | bool | — | Truthy forces injection of the mid-conversation system prompt regardless of the usual gating (companion to `CLAUDE_CODE_MID_CONVERSATION_SYSTEM`) (v2.1.154) |
