The comparison uses the raw environment, not the typed accessor, so `true` does not work. Through 2.1.245 it was declared with no consumer. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORWARD_SUBAGENT_TEXT` | bool | — | Forwards subagent text and thinking blocks as assistant/user messages carrying `parent_tool_use_id`. Equivalent to `--forward-subagent-text`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FRAME_TIMING_SAMPLE_EVERY` | int | `1` | Sample every Nth frame for frame-timing instrumentation; values below 1 are clamped to 1. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GB_BASE_URL` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GB_DISK_CACHE_WHEN_TELEMETRY_OFF` | bool | — | Allows GrowthBook flags to be cached on disk even when telemetry is disabled. Only takes effect when DISABLE_GROWTHBOOK is not set. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GB_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS` | int | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GZIP_REQUEST_BODIES` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state override for the `tengu_gzip_request_bodies` experiment controlling gzip compression of outgoing request bodies (default off). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HFI_BEARER_TOKEN` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. Still no reader at 2.1.282. Since 2.1.257 its second occurrence is the auth-token list `UMt` (line 38179), whose names are stripped from spawned child environments; that is a mechanism, not a consumer. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HIDE_SETTINGS_HINT` | string | — | Suppresses the settings hint line for entrypoints that are not already excluded from it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_CREDS_FILE` | string | — | Path to a host-provided credentials file, used when `CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST` is set. Ignored with a warning if the file's permissions or owner are wrong. Redacted from logs. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_INVOKED_SKILLS` | — | — | Present in the allowlist of variables forwarded to Bash-tool shell-snapshot subprocesses, but nothing in this bundle writes its value — treat it as a passthrough slot rather than a knob. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_IS_COWORK` | bool | `false` | Mark the session as cowork/bridge mode for eager flushing and related behaviors |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_KB_COHESION_FIXES` | bool | — | Enables keybinding-cohesion fixes so custom `app:interrupt` / `app:exit` bindings are honored instead of falling back to hardcoded Ctrl-C / Ctrl-D. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_LOOP_KEEPALIVE` | tri-bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_kairos_loop_keepalive` experiment (default off), keeping the agentic loop alive between iterations. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it was retyped from bool to tri-bool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MANAGED_SETTINGS_PATH` | string | — | Overrides the directory managed (enterprise policy) settings are read from. Set internally for sandboxed subprocesses. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION` | — | `200` | Caps how many subagents (Task tool invocations) a single session may spawn. **Its typed declaration was removed at 2.1.224**, so the Type column is now `—`; the name survives only in the settings-injectable registry, meaning it can still be injected from a `settings.json` `env` block but no longer has a typed read path. Verify before relying on it. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET` | string | — | **New at 2.1.224.** Path to a Unix socket used for inter-process messaging. Read through a destructured environment alias rather than `process.env`, so a naive grep misses it. Forwarded into child sessions by the subprocess-forwarding registry while simultaneously listed in the host-managed strip set — i.e. passed down to children the CLI spawns, but not honored from an untrusted parent. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN` | string | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Shared secret authenticating clients of the Unix-domain-socket messaging channel named by `CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET`. Declared in the same second export map as that socket (`ers`, line 42017) and reached through its own accessor `$Y` rather than the main `X` namespace. The parent publishes a generated child token with `$Y.set("CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN", o.childToken)` (line 847979) and clears it with `$Y.unset(...)` on teardown and at startup (lines 847871, 849164). Forwarded to child sessions by the subprocess-forwarding registry (`_Br`, line 827576) while also listed in the host-managed strip set — passed down to children the CLI spawns, but not honoured from an untrusted parent. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_WEB_SEARCHES_PER_SESSION` | int (min 1, digits only) | `200` | Caps how many WebSearch calls a single session may make. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_AUTO_BACKGROUND_MS` | int | `120000` | Milliseconds after which a slow MCP tool call is automatically backgrounded, clamped to [0, 2147483647]. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_TOOL_IDLE_TIMEOUT` | int | `1800000` stdio / `300000` other | Idle milliseconds before an MCP tool call with no response or progress is aborted; the default differs by transport and `0` disables the timeout. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_INIT` | bool | `false` | Use new init flow |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_MODEL_FALLBACK` | bool | — | When exactly true, disables automatic model substitution — an attempted fallback pivot throws a tripwire error instead of quietly switching models. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_OTEL_DIAG_STDERR` | bool | — | Writes OpenTelemetry diagnostic output to stderr. Its presence, like any OTEL_* variable, also marks the process as telemetry-configured. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PACKAGE_MANAGER_AUTO_UPDATE` | bool | — | Truthy opts the installer into automatic package-manager-driven self-updates |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PERFETTO_WRITE_INTERVAL_S` | int | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PERFORCE_MODE` | string | `false` | Enable Perforce-aware code paths (extra safeguards/adaptations for Perforce workspaces) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_POWERUP_ONBOARDING` | string | — | Forces the powerup onboarding presentation to `banner` or `step`. Any other value defers to the `tengu_birch_lantern` experiment (default off); onboarding also shows when the user has not completed onboarding. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PROACTIVE` | bool | — | Truthy turns on the proactive/Kairos assistant mode in the TUI |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PROCESS_WRAPPER` | — | — | Corporate-launcher argv prefix used to re-exec the background-agent supervisor and its sessions/workers. Overrides the `processWrapper` setting and is forwarded to child processes. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PROFILE_QUERY` | — | — | Formerly enabled query-profiling marks. **No reader since 2.1.248**, when its typed declaration went away. The only remaining occurrences are the hint text "Query profiling not enabled (set CLAUDE_CODE_PROFILE_QUERY=1)" (line 498710) and an entry in the telemetry-name allowlist (line 870207), so setting it no longer enables anything. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST` | bool | `false` | Provider credentials are managed by the host application |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_QUESTION_PREVIEW_FORMAT` | string | `markdown` outside SDK mode | Format for question previews: `markdown` or `html` |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REFUSAL_FALLBACK_CATCH_ALL` | tri-bool | `false` | Tri-state controlling whether the refusal-fallback router applies a catch-all retry when no specific refusal-category route matches. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RELAUNCH_TERMINAL_SIZE` | string | — | A `COLSxROWS` string carrying terminal dimensions across a relaunch, consumed once and then removed from the environment. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REPL` | tri-bool | — | Enable REPL mode. **Declared but never consumed at 2.1.282** (and at 2.1.275-2.1.278): its export-map entry (line 24372) is its only occurrence. 2.1.280 briefly had a second occurrence. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REPORT_FINDINGS` | bool | — | Gates the ReportFindings-specific system-prompt instructions, and only for agents whose tool set already includes the ReportFindings tool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REPO_CHECKOUTS` | string | — | Repository checkout paths |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SAFE_MODE` | bool | — | Disables all user customizations at once — CLAUDE.md, skills, plugins, hooks, MCP servers, custom commands and agents, themes and keybindings. Equivalent to `--safe-mode`; also re-set to `1` on respawn so the mode survives relaunch. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SEND_FEEDBACK` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state gate for the in-product feedback feature (the `tengu_juniper_relay` experiment); an explicit false disables it outright. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS` | int (min 1) | — | Timeout for session-end hooks (ms). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.268 it changed its constraint to `{ min: 1 }`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE_SYSTEM_PROMPT` | string | — | Truthy forces the simplified system prompt; falsy explicitly disables it |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_ANTHROPIC_GOOGLE_CLOUD_AUTH` | bool | — | Skips the Anthropic-on-Google-Cloud credential flow, assuming auth is supplied out of band. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_AWS_CRED_CACHE` | bool | — | Disables caching of resolved AWS credentials, forcing the credential chain to run on every request. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_FAST_MODE_NETWORK_ERRORS` | bool | `false` | Skip network errors in fast mode |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_FAST_MODE_ORG_CHECK` | bool | `false` | Skip organization check for fast mode eligibility |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_HFI_VERSION_CHECK` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_PROJECT_BACKFILL` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SKIP_REPO_UPLOAD` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP` | int | — | Raises the cap on how many consecutive times a Stop/SubagentStop hook may block a turn before Claude Code overrides it and ends the turn; parsed as an integer (v2.1.145) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_CACHE_EVICT` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_subagent_cache_evict` experiment, enabling eviction of subagent prompt-cache entries. Requires prompt-cache diagnostics to be active. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB` | tri-bool | `false` | Scrub a built-in set of sensitive credentials from inherited subprocess environments before spawning child processes |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SUPERVISED` | bool | — | Truthy marks the process as supervised; uncaught exceptions/rejections exit instead of being swallowed |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SUPPRESS_SESSION_ATTRIBUTION` | bool | — | Suppresses the session-URL attribution otherwise attached to outbound content such as commit trailers. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SESSION_REFS` | bool | — | Enables remote syncing of session reference data, gated on a session-persistence path being configured. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS_INSTALL_TIMEOUT_MS` | int | `30000` | Timeout in milliseconds for installing a skill during skill sync. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TAGS` | string | — | Tags for telemetry |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TAG_ISMETA_MESSAGES` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TASK_LIST_ID` | string | — | Task list ID override |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TERMINAL_MCP_TOOLS` | string | `""` | Comma-separated MCP tool names that get special inline terminal rendering of their tool_use output; blank entries ignored. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TERMINAL_RECORDING` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TODO_REMINDER_MODE` | enum baseline \| off | `baseline` | Overrides the `tengu_soft_slate_nudge` experiment for the todo-list reminder in the system prompt: `baseline` emits the standard reminders, `off` suppresses them. When unset the experiment flag decides, itself defaulting to `baseline`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TOTAL_TOKENS_REMINDER` | string | — | Selects the total-tokens reminder mode — `off`, `infinite`, `fixed`, `countdown` or `padded-countdown` — which emits a `<total_tokens>N tokens left</total_tokens>` block into the prompt. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TOTAL_TOKENS_REMINDER_AFTER_USER_TURN` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state control over whether the total-tokens reminder block is also emitted after each regular user turn. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TOTAL_TOKENS_REMINDER_BUDGET` | int | `15000000` | Starting token budget used by the total-tokens reminder's `padded-countdown` mode. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TRANSCRIPT_LOCAL_GC` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state override for the `tengu_transcript_local_gc` experiment controlling local garbage collection of old transcript files (default off). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TRIGGER_ID` | string | — | Identifier of the trigger that started this session; attached to telemetry as trigger_id. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TWO_STAGE_CLASSIFIER` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_ANTHROPIC_GOOGLE_CLOUD` | bool | — | Routes requests through the Anthropic-on-Google-Cloud provider. One of the mutually exclusive provider selectors. Settings-injectable. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_GATEWAY` | bool | — | Routes API traffic through the Anthropic cloud gateway. Requires both `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` and `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`; without them it is ignored with a warning. Gateway session expiry tells you to refresh the token rather than run /login. Settings-injectable. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.280 it started being read through the capability-checked engine `env.get` call (host handler at line 467331). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_VOICE_FORWARD_INTERIMS_TYPED` | bool | — | Truthy forwards interim voice transcription results as typed input |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKER_EPOCH` | int | — | Worker epoch for process management |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKSPACE_HOST_PATHS` | string | — | Pipe-separated host workspace paths attached to telemetry events |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_ADOPT` | bool | — | Disables adopting an existing session; when unset adoption is enabled. |
| `CLAUDE_EFFORT` | — | — | Injected into hook and Bash subprocess environments (and `${CLAUDE_EFFORT}` template substitutions) with the turn's active reasoning effort: `low`, `medium`, `high`, `xhigh` or `max`. |
| `CLAUDE_FORCE_DISPLAY_SURVEY` | bool | `false` | Force the feedback survey to appear when the user is otherwise eligible |
| `CLAUDE_GATEWAY_ALLOW_LOOPBACK` | bool | — | Allows the cloud gateway to accept loopback addresses, which is otherwise refused. |
| `CLAUDE_IMPORT_CONVERSATIONS` | bool | — | Truthy enables importing prior conversations on startup (gated by `!xH(process.env.CLAUDE_IMPORT_CONVERSATIONS)`) (v2.1.154) |
| `CLAUDE_INTERNAL_FC_OVERRIDES` | string | — | Intended to carry JSON-encoded GrowthBook feature-flag overrides, but **dead code at this tag**: the reading function returns unconditionally before it ever reaches the `process.env` read, so the value is never parsed. |
| `CLAUDE_LOCAL_OAUTH_API_BASE` | string | `http://localhost:8000` | Local OAuth API base URL |
| `CLAUDE_LOCAL_OAUTH_APPS_BASE` | string | `http://localhost:4000` | Local OAuth apps base URL |
| `CLAUDE_LOCAL_OAUTH_CONSOLE_BASE` | string | `http://localhost:3000` | Local OAuth console base URL |
| `CLAUDE_PID` | — | — | The CLI's own process id (`String(process.pid)`), injected into child and hook subprocess environments. |
| `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` | — | — | Project root path, set on hook and subprocess environments and substituted for `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}` in hook commands. Preserved when the environment is scrubbed for child processes. |
| `CLAUDE_PROJECT_UUID` | string | — | Associates the working directory with a claude.ai Project by UUID so that Project's context is fetched and synced. The value is trimmed; an empty string counts as unset. |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_ACTIVITY_FD` | int (min 3) | — | File descriptor number the runner writes activity heartbeats to, so the supervising process can tell the run is alive. |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_DISABLE_AWAITING_ACTION_OVERRIDE` | bool | — | Suppresses the awaiting-action state override in the self-hosted runner. Declared but unconsumed at 2.1.222; it gained a real reader at 2.1.224, consumed negated so an unset value leaves the override active. |
| `CLAUDE_RUNNER_FETCH_DEPTH` | string | — | Git fetch depth for self-hosted-runner session checkouts. Declared but unconsumed at 2.1.222; it gained a real reader at 2.1.224 which logs `Ignoring CLAUDE_RUNNER_FETCH_DEPTH=<value>` and falls back when the value is not a valid depth. |
| `CLAUDE_SDK_CAN_USE_TOOL_SHADOWED` | — | — | **Not an environment variable.** This is the `code` of a Node process warning emitted when bare `allowedTools` entries shadow the SDK `canUseTool` callback. Listed here only because the name appears env-var-shaped; setting it does nothing. |
| `CLAUDE_SNIP` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_SSH_LOCAL_BINARY` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_SSH_VERSION` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_STAGE_FILE_ROOT` | string | `/mnt/user-data/uploads` | Staging root that file-write tools use for staging-kind writes instead of the real filesystem (e.g. remote or read-only-FS mode). Must be an absolute path — a relative value throws. |
| `CLAUDE_WORKFLOW_NAME_ONLY` | bool | — | Restricts the Workflow tool to named bundled workflows, blocking `workflow({scriptPath})` invocations. |
| `CLAUDE_WORKSHOP_PROGRESS` | — | enabled | Would disable workshop authoring-progress hooks, tested as `Kd.CLAUDE_WORKSHOP_PROGRESS !== false` (line 827095). Read through the inert accessor namespace `Kd` (built over an empty map at line 43932), so the property is always `undefined` no matter what the real environment holds. Since `undefined !== false`, the test always passes and the hooks cannot be switched off. |
| `COREPACK_ENABLE_AUTO_PIN` | — | forced to `0` | Corepack auto-pin is disabled by Claude Code at startup |
| `DATABASE_URL` | — | — | Not read by the CLI. At 2.1.222 it appears only as an example row ("Postgres connection string") inside an embedded documentation template; the typed-schema declaration it carried through 2.1.221 was dropped this release. Setting it has no effect, and had none before. |
| `DEMO_VERSION` | string | — | Demo mode version string |
| `DO_NOT_TRACK` | string | — | Standard signal to disable telemetry (respects the do-not-track convention) |
| `ENVIRONMENT_SERVICE_KEY` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. Still no reader at 2.1.282. Since 2.1.257 its second occurrence is the remote-sink redaction list (line 412189). |
| `FALLBACK_FOR_ALL_PRIMARY_MODELS` | string | — | Enable fallback for all primary models |
| `FORCE_HYPERLINK` | — | — | Standard supports-hyperlinks override. Its mere presence in the environment forces terminal hyperlink support on, regardless of value. |
| `GCM_INTERACTIVE` | string | `never` (when unset) | Git Credential Manager interactivity. Claude Code injects `never` into git subprocess environments only when the variable is undefined, suppressing GCM prompts while preserving an explicit value. |
| `GIT_ASKPASS` | string | `""` (when unset) | Program git uses to prompt for credentials. Claude Code injects an empty string into git subprocess environments only when the variable is undefined, disabling the askpass GUI helper without clobbering an existing setting. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.274 it started being read through the case-tolerant env helper (`xF`/`fa`, line 110090). |
| `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` | string | `ssh` | SSH command git uses. Claude Code wraps it (defaulting to `ssh`) with `-o BatchMode=yes -o StrictHostKeyChecking=yes`, may route it through the sandbox SOCKS proxy, and uses its presence to gate agent-proxy GitHub URL rewriting. |
| `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT` | string | `0` (when unset) | Controls whether git may prompt on the terminal. Claude Code injects `0` into git subprocess environments only when the variable is undefined, so an explicit value you set is preserved. |
| `HISTFILE` | string | — | Shell history file path, read only behind explicit user opt-in during an environment/context-gathering scan. |
| `INK_SCREEN_READER` | bool | — | Forces the renderer into screen-reader mode; only honored when stdout is a TTY. |
| `JAVA_HOME` | string | — | Java installation root; used to locate `keytool` when a Java trust store must be modified. |
| `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS` | string | — | Standard JVM options variable. Claude Code appends `-Dhttps.proxyHost` / trust-store options to any existing value when propagating a detected proxy or custom CA, rather than replacing it. |
| `LOCAL_BRIDGE` | bool | `false` | Alias flag that enables local OAuth / local bridge behavior |
| `MAX_STRUCTURED_OUTPUT_RETRIES` | int | `5` | Maximum retries for structured-output validation loops |
| `MODE` | — | — | Not read from `process.env` by the CLI at this tag — it appears only inside an embedded esbuild `define` map (`import.meta.env.MODE`) for the bundled Storybook-to-image sub-bundler. Setting it has no effect on Claude Code. |
| `NODE_ENV` | — | — | Standard Node environment name. Lost its literal `process.env` read at 2.1.246. Since 2.1.280 it is read only through the capability-checked engine `env.get` call, as `nodeEnv` in the environment attributes of a first-party plugin (line 1616997). The CLI still writes `NODE_ENV: "production"` into the environments of its helper subprocesses (lines 129733, 630104). |
| `NoDefaultCurrentDirectoryInExePath` | string | forced to `1` | Windows security: prevent current directory in exe path resolution |
| `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `SYSTEMROOT` | string | `C:\Windows` | Windows system root, used to locate system executables such as cmd.exe. Falls back to C:\Windows. |
| `TEMP` | string | — | Windows temporary directory; part of the Windows env allowlist forwarded to child processes. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.247 it started being read through the case-tolerant env helper (`xF`/`fa`, line 110090). |
| `TEST_ENABLE_SESSION_PERSISTENCE` | bool | `false` | Enable session persistence in test mode |
| `ULTRAPLAN_PROMPT_FILE` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `USE_LOCAL_OAUTH` | bool | `false` | Use local OAuth / local bridge endpoints instead of the production OAuth flow |
| `USE_STAGING_OAUTH` | bool | `false` | Use staging OAuth |
| `VCR_RECORD` | bool | — | VCR recording mode for CI test replays. **Declared but never consumed since 2.1.239**: its export-map entry (line 24492) is its only occurrence. |
| `VOICE_STREAM_BASE_URL` | string | — | WebSocket URL for voice streaming |
| `all_proxy` | string | — | Lowercase generic proxy URL, checked as a fallback by bundled HTTP clients when no protocol-specific proxy is set. |

## Experiment & Feature Gates

Each of these forces on a server-side experiment gate that would otherwise be decided by a `tengu_*` flag. Most inject or remove a specific system-prompt section, so they are the closest thing the bundle has to a switchboard for model behavior.

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ELEGANT_MEADOW` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_elegant_meadow) | **New at 2.1.282.** Overrides the `tengu_elegant_meadow` gate (default false) (line 403711). The gate enables transcript request-replay recording for main and subagent API requests (line 403762) and the per-transcript replay index that is updated on `fork-context-ref` entries and message appends (lines 605289, 605306). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SQUISHY_NEWT` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_squishy_newt, on) | **New at 2.1.282.** Override for `BKe()` (line 595522), which otherwise reads GrowthBook `tengu_squishy_newt` (default `true`). While on, a queued command whose origin is a projects-relay task notification and that carries `hearthRelayMessageIds` is skipped when the main thread collects queued commands, and so are some prompts queued after it (lines 410526, 410540). Instead, each newly seen held message injects one `critical_system_reminder` saying a message from the project's coordinator session will arrive after the turn ends (lines 811233, 818602); a non-empty `tengu_harmonic_cook` string replaces that text (line 595524). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_INHERITED_TYPE_GRANT` | — | — (`tengu_swirling_squid` decides, off) | **New at 2.1.281.** Read only through the inert namespace: `Dn.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_INHERITED_TYPE_GRANT ?? x("tengu_swirling_squid", false) === true` (line 982451), so the variable never has an effect and the `tengu_swirling_squid` gate alone decides, latched once per session. When on, the Artifact publish path consults the type's stored capability grant for type-locked artifacts published without their own capabilities or `contract` (lines 1578430, 1579436, 1585402). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TEXT_VARIANT` | enum v0 \| v1 \| v2 | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_sloe, v0) | **New at 2.1.281.** Selects the Artifact tool prompt text variant. Latched once per session (line 1116649): env value, else `v0` when `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ORIGIN` is `claude_ai_chat`, else `x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_sloe", "v0")`. Anything but `v1`/`v2` becomes `v0`, and the choice is logged as `tengu_artifact_text_variant`. The variant feeds the tool prompt builder (line 1579734) and a prompt-variant tag (line 1116719). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RUSTLING_PIXEL` | string | unset (defers to tengu_rustling_pixel) | **New at 2.1.280.** Sets `keepForeignThinking` for API requests (line 506256): `all` (logged as `api_keep_thinking_on_model_change`), `upgrade` (logged as `api_keep_thinking_on_upgrade`) or `none`; any other value logs a warning and becomes `upgrade` (line 501251). Evaluated only on the first-party API (otherwise `none`); when unset the value comes from client data, then the `tengu_rustling_pixel` gate (default `all`). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PARSED_WILLOW` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_parsed_willow) | **New at 2.1.277.** Marks queued prompt commands from a human origin as `humanTurn` (line 561788) and, for those, strips the `<system-reminder>` wrapper when the queued command is rendered into the API messages, so the text reaches the model as a plain user turn (line 596692). Unset defers to `tengu_parsed_willow` (default on) (line 595519). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PER_TURN_TIMING` | — | effectively unset | **New at 2.1.277.** Read only through the inert namespace as `!Dn.CLAUDE_CODE_PER_TURN_TIMING` (line 137240), which is always true, so `cjn()` always returns false and per-turn timing is pinned off. As a result `perTurnTiming` is false in beta selection (line 147503), the `timing-2026-09-09` beta is never attached through it (line 147442), and the per-turn `now` field is not sent (line 505553). Setting the variable has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_FRESH_READ` | tri-bool | on | **New at 2.1.274.** Only an explicit `false` has an effect; unset and `true` leave it on. It gates the handling of type-created artifacts the session owns: the fresh-instance branch when reading a type-locked artifact with no files of its own or with content in the `pages` (Claude Docs) store (line 1075598), and, at create time, opening a `pages`-backed artifact immediately under `auto_open: after_first_write` (lines 1582648, 1585598). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_OPENING_PREFETCH` | tri-bool | on (`remote_cowork` only) | **New at 2.1.274.** Only an explicit `false` has an effect: it turns off the opening-turn artifact prefetch (line 562191). Otherwise the prefetch runs only in `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT=remote_cowork` sessions where the Appifact REPL tool or quickstart is on, attaching `artifact_opening_prefetch` content before the main agent's first assistant message; it is skipped when a PreToolUse hook may cover the Artifact tool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_START_KIT` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_concurrent_candle) | **New at 2.1.274.** Enables the Artifact quickstart "start kit": `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_START_KIT ?? x("tengu_concurrent_candle", false) === true` (line 983885). When on and the Bash tool is in the tool pool (line 1915379), a `slides` or `design` quickstart with a matched type saves the design system's and the type's published files locally (line 1566686). It also changes which font files the file saver skips (line 984343). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PATH_PIN` | tri-bool | true | **New at 2.1.271.** Per-file publish guard for multi-file artifacts, on by default (`a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PATH_PIN ?? true`, line 784759). When on, the CLI records the sha256 and version of each file Claude read, listed or wrote, and refuses a publish that would replace or remove a file Claude has not read or that changed since ("Not published: ..."). It is latched at schema build and only applies when multi-file publishing is on (line 785386). The refusal offers the `overwrite_unread` escape (line 785018) except when `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` is `remote_cowork` or `remote_cowork_trigger`; there it tells Claude to read the file even if the user wants it replaced. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_QUICKSTART` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_woad) | **New at 2.1.271.** Adds the Artifact tool's read-only `quickstart` action (pass `intent`) and its `intent`/`design_systems` inputs. `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_QUICKSTART ?? x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_woad", false) === true` (line 779700). The action only appears when the type catalog (`CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPE_CATALOG`) and create-from-type are also on (line 785357). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_SLEEPY_SNOWFLAKE` | — | unset (defers to tengu_sleepy_snowflake) | **New at 2.1.271.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 501807), so the per-model kept-reminder delivery scope (`off`, `threads` or `all`) always comes from the recorded session value, the `tengu_sleepy_snowflake` client-data map (model pattern to scope), or the `tengu_sleepy_snowflake` gate (default `all`) (line 501907). The scope is computed only on the first-party API, and `threads` degrades to `off` unless the tether-live gate is on (`My()`, line 501781). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_REPL` | tri-bool | — | **New at 2.1.269.** Declared but never consumed: besides its export-map entry, the only occurrence is the project-scoped-settings deny list (line 220184), so setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FLAG_FETCH_WAIT_MS` | int (min 0) | 3000 | **New at 2.1.269.** Startup wait budget in milliseconds for GrowthBook feature-flag initialization in a non-interactive remote session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` set) with GrowthBook enabled (lines 396839, 840436). The effective wait is the larger of this value and 1500 ms, the latter applying when event logging is on and the flag cache is empty; 0 removes this part of the wait. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ATTRIBUTION_ANNOUNCEMENT` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_reflective_wolf, true) | **New at 2.1.268.** Chooses how commit and PR attribution reaches the model. When on (the default), the Bash tool's git instructions say to end commits and PR bodies with "the attribution lines given in the conversation's system-reminder" and the literal lines arrive in a system-reminder (lines 450002, 813686). When off, the literal trailer text is embedded in the tool prompt instead (line 450178). `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ATTRIBUTION_ANNOUNCEMENT ?? x("tengu_reflective_wolf", true)`, latched per session (line 449991). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_HOT` | tri-bool | unset (defers to `tengu_copper_hinge_wren`) | **New at 2.1.267.** Enables the Artifact publish `deadline` option: when on, a publish that carries a `deadline` sends it with the request, and the result schema accepts `deadline` and `deadlineDropped` (lines 717466, 1552920). Unset defers to `tengu_copper_hinge_wren`, off by default (line 716933); the value is frozen as `hotOn` for the session (line 785382). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_DB_STR_REPLACE` | tri-bool | unset (defers to `tengu_umber_stile`) | **New at 2.1.265.** Enables string-replace edits in the Artifact tool's database verbs. It applies only while the artifact database is on (`CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_DB ?? true`); unset defers to `tengu_umber_stile`, off by default (line 777217). The result is frozen once per session (lines 779044, 785347). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_FIVE_CLASS_ASKS` | — | — (`tengu_cobalt_plinth_bryony` decides, off) | **New at 2.1.265.** Read only through the inert namespace: `Dn.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_FIVE_CLASS_ASKS ?? x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_bryony", false) === true` (line 366579), so the variable never has an effect and the `tengu_cobalt_plinth_bryony` gate alone decides, latched once per session. When on, an artifact read-consent probe that reports `public` sharing replaces the recorded mode (line 366818). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TETHER_LIVE` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_curious_tower) | **New at 2.1.265.** Declared in its own shape (line 1120695) and read through the dedicated namespace `vZe` (line 451024). | `CLAUDE_CODE_POLISHED_DEWDROP` | enum drop \| block \| off | unset (defers to tengu_polished_dewdrop) | **New at 2.1.263.** Selects the thinking prefix-mismatch behaviour: `drop` maps to `drop_block`, `block` to `error`, and `off` sends nothing (lines 501067, 501081). It is evaluated only on the first-party API with a first-party base URL; when unset the `tengu_polished_dewdrop` string gate (default empty, i.e. nothing sent) supplies the value. The mapped value is sent as `thinking.block_binding.prefix_mismatch_behavior` when thinking is adaptive or enabled, alongside the `thinking-binding-controls-2026-08-01` beta (lines 506521, 507071). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COMPILED_ACORN` | — | `tengu_compiled_acorn` (off) | **New at 2.1.261.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 534376), so the variable is always undefined and has no effect; the result is the `tengu_compiled_acorn` gate (default `false`), evaluated only when `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` is `claude_in_slack` or `claude-in-slack`. With that gate on, human posts relayed from the bound Slack thread are delivered as server `<wake …>` envelopes instead of carrying the `[Verified human message relayed from the bound Slack thread]:` prefix, and the system-prompt guidance describes them that way (lines 535662, 536460). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_COZY_TEAPOT` | enum strict \| relaxed | unset (defers to tengu_cozy_teapot) | **New at 2.1.260.** Selects the wording of the bash-first steer in the auto-mode and bypass-permissions reminder: `strict` tells the model to do its work through the Bash tool wherever it can, `relaxed` says Bash is fine when it is the simpler route and to prefer the dedicated edit tools for fragile edits (lines 191183, 599282). Unset uses the per-session variant: a client-data value for `tengu_cozy_teapot`, then `relaxed` for models with the `opus_5_5_prompt_bundle` capability, then the `tengu_cozy_teapot` flag, else `strict`. Applies only when the bash-first steer is attached (line 562163). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PIN` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_holly) | **New at 2.1.259.** Adds the `pin` and `unpin` actions to the Artifact tool, which add or remove an artifact from the user's claude.ai sidebar (line 785235). `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PIN ?? x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_holly", false)` (line 784571) is forced off in a remote session unless it is an Anthropic cloud session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` with no `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND`). | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_MULTI_FILE` | tri-bool | unset (defers to `tengu_cobalt_plinth_bracken`) | **New at 2.1.257.** Enables multi-file artifacts (supporting files published next to the page); with it off, a publish that carries supporting files is refused as `multifile_flag_off` (line 719326). Unset defers to `tengu_cobalt_plinth_bracken`, off by default (line 716912). It is frozen per session as `multiFileOn` and is a prerequisite for artifact types (line 785352). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_OPEN_ACTION` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_willow) | **New at 2.1.257.** Adds the `open` action to the Artifact tool, which shows the user an existing artifact at `url` without changing it, together with its description clause. `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_OPEN_ACTION ?? x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_willow", false)` (line 784715) is evaluated once when the tool's input schema is built (line 785373). Listed in the set of keys that project-scoped settings (`.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`) may not set, so such an entry is dropped with a warning (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TOOLSET` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_damson) | **New at 2.1.257.** Splits the artifact tool surface. | `CLAUDE_CODE_STELLAR_DRIFT` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_stellar_drift) | **New at 2.1.257.** Override for `gy()` (line 451029), which sets `echoApiBlockOrder` for request building and message normalisation (lines 505526, 511302). | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PRESENCE` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_brass_plover) | **New at 2.1.251.** Enables artifact presence: when a live subscription to an artifact opens, the CLI opens a per-artifact presence WebSocket, authenticated with a minted agent token, with reconnect and backoff handling (lines 742697, 745958). `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PRESENCE ?? x("tengu_brass_plover", false)` (line 742683). When off, arming returns `flag_off` silently. Listed in the set of keys that project-scoped settings (`.claude/settings.json`, `.claude/settings.local.json`) may not set, so such an entry is dropped with a warning (line 220184). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISPATCH_V2D` | — | tengu_dreamy_frost | **New at 2.1.251.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 504986), so it is always `undefined` and has no effect. | `CLAUDE_CODE_GENTLE_PARASOL` | string | unset (falls back to client_data tengu_gentle_parasol) | **New at 2.1.251.** Text of a `secondary_reminder` attachment inserted after the last message of a turn (lines 810276, 810363). | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PREVIEW` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_aspen) | **New at 2.1.247.** Would add the Artifact tool's `preview` action (a Chrome-rendered preview). The action is also suppressed when `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` is set. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WILLOW_TERN` | bool | unset (model rules, then tengu_willow_tern) | **New at 2.1.247.** Forces on the `willow_tern` system-prompt section, a "# Writing for the user" style guide (lines 473750, 473764, 473991). Unset: a boolean `tengu_willow_tern` value in clientData wins; otherwise the section is on for models in the Fable 5.1 prompt bundle, off for models outside the Opus 5 prompt bundle, and otherwise follows GrowthBook `tengu_willow_tern` (default `false`). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BREEZY_HORIZON` | string | unset (client_data breezy_horizon map) | **New at 2.1.246.** Model id used in place of the session's model when choosing prompt text (the "model for prompt" swap); it does not change the inference model. An env value applies to every model. When unset, the per-model `breezy_horizon` map in server client data is used. A falsy value (`0`/`false`/`no`/`off`) disables the swap entirely. Invalid model ids or self-mappings are ignored with a warning, and each swap is logged as `tengu_breezy_horizon` (line 191330). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TICKLISH_WHISPER` | — | — (inert; tengu_ticklish_whisper, off) | **New at 2.1.246.** Read only through the inert namespace (`Dn.CLAUDE_CODE_TICKLISH_WHISPER`, line 538194), so it is always `undefined` and `KFo()` falls through to GrowthBook `tengu_ticklish_whisper` (default `false`). That gate (`tz()`) controls the auto-mode classifier denial-limit fallback: when on and a permission dialog is available, reaching the consecutive-denial limit yields a blocking or timed prompt (`denialLimitFallback`) instead of the legacy fallback (line 539829). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TICKLISH_WHISPER_TIMEOUT_MS` | — | 120000 (inert) | **New at 2.1.246.** Read only through the inert namespace (`Dn.CLAUDE_CODE_TICKLISH_WHISPER_TIMEOUT_MS`, line 538241), so `YFo()` always returns the built-in 120000 ms. | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPE_CATALOG` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_larch) | **New at 2.1.242.** Enables the Artifact type catalog: adds the `type_query` and `type` inputs to `list_types`/`list` and the type-catalog description clause. `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPE_CATALOG ?? x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_larch", false)` (line 779697) is the same gate as `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPES`. | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPE_CLOUD_CREATE` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_cobalt_plinth_larch) | **New at 2.1.242.** Allows creating an artifact from a type (`type_url`) in Anthropic cloud sessions. With `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` set, create-from-type needs either an Anthropic cloud session (no `CLAUDE_CODE_ENVIRONMENT_KIND`) with this flag on, or the separate `tengu_cobalt_plinth_linden` path (line 779320). `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPE_CLOUD_CREATE ?? x("tengu_cobalt_plinth_larch", false)` (line 779314). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HANDBACK_PROVENANCE` | — | effectively unset | **New at 2.1.242.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 372761), so the `e !== undefined` override in `uie()` never fires and the decision always falls to the `tengu_melodic_wolf` gate (default on). That gate controls the subagent hand-back provenance frame: a `[Subagent hand-back]` header stating the report is model output with no user authority, with every report line indented (lines 755893, 761480, 1548241). Setting the variable has no effect in the shipped build. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_PROMPT_PROVENANCE` | — | — (inert; tengu_bubbly_harbor, on) | **New at 2.1.242.** Read only through the inert namespace (`Dn.CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_PROMPT_PROVENANCE`, line 993029), so `tZn()` always falls through to GrowthBook `tengu_bubbly_harbor` (default `true`). That gate decides whether workflow agent prompts carry `[Workflow harness — …]` provenance headers marking computed task text, the relayed user request or an automated trigger (lines 996780, 997741). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_DELETE` | tri-bool | unset (defers to `tengu_cobalt_plinth_alder`) | **New at 2.1.239.** Enables the Artifact tool's `delete` action, which permanently deletes a published artifact (line 1116792). Captured as `deleteOn` when the tool's session configuration is frozen (line 785369). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_INTRO_FRAME` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_ochre_wren) | **New at 2.1.239.** Selects the opening sentence of the system prompt when no output style is active: on, "You are an agent working with the user toward their goals, using your own judgment along the way."; off, "You are an interactive agent that helps users with software engineering tasks." (lines 473804, 473815). Unset defers to `tengu_ochre_wren` (default off). The result is memoized, and when on it is logged as `intro_frame_arm_active` with its source (`env` or `growthbook`). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_ROOM` | tri-bool | — | **New at 2.1.238.** Declared but never consumed: its only non-export occurrence is the project-scoped-settings deny list (line 220184). | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_VERIFY` | tri-bool | unset (defers to tengu_osier_pylon_trace) | **New at 2.1.238.** Adds the Artifact tool's `verify` action, which reads the page's diagnostics from `/api/frame/diag/<slug>/<ver>`. `a.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_VERIFY ?? x("tengu_osier_pylon_trace", false)` (line 778868), forced off when `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` is set. | `CLAUDE_CODE_AMBER_ASTROLABE` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_amber_astrolabe` experiment, which injects system-prompt guidance telling autonomous sessions to act without asking for confirmation. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_ASSETS` | tri-bool | `true` | **New at 2.1.234.** Enables the artifact asset store (uploading and serving files attached to an artifact). Unusually for this table it defaults to **on** rather than deferring to an experiment: `G.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_ASSETS ?? true` (line 636362), so the variable exists mainly to force the feature off with an explicit `0`. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENTS` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_teal_corbel` (`false`) | Enables commenting on artifacts. When unset the `tengu_teal_corbel` experiment decides, defaulting to off (line 589808). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENTS_AUTOREACT` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_sorrel_trellis` (`false`) | Enables automatic reactions to artifact comments. When unset the `tengu_sorrel_trellis` experiment decides, defaulting to off; the result is memoised on first read (line 591667). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENT_FAST_ACK_FIXED` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_gorse_sill` (`false`) | **New at 2.1.235.** Second-stage gate for the artifact-comment fast acknowledgement, layered on `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENT_FAST_ACK`: the fast-ack path must already be enabled before this is consulted, and then `G.CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENT_FAST_ACK_FIXED ?? nt("tengu_gorse_sill", false)` decides (line 639886). | `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_TYPES` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_cobalt_plinth_larch` (`false`) | **New at 2.1.236.** Enables artifact types (the typed-artifact create/lock routes). | `CLAUDE_CODE_BASALT_COVE` | bool | — | Forces on the internal `basalt_cove` feature override, bypassing the per-item config-override checklist that normally gates it. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_BISON_CAIRN` | tri-bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_bison_cairn` experiment, which adds the "delivering work" system-prompt section. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.257 it was retyped from bool to tri-bool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_GORSE_PLOVER` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_gorse_plover` experiment, which adds system-prompt guidance that commands are cheap to run so the agent should just run one. |

| `CLAUDE_CODE_JUNIPER_SUNDIAL` | int (min 1, digits only) | `10` | Number of non-meta user turns that must elapse in ultra-effort mode before the sparse `ultra_effort_enter` maintenance reminder is re-injected. When unset the value falls through the cached `tengu_juniper_sundial` dynamic config, then the same-named experiment flag, then the built-in `TURNS_BETWEEN_MAINTENANCE` of 10; non-integer values are discarded at each step (lines 714479-714492, 716799). |

| `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_DB` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_umber_lattice` (`false`) | **New at 2.1.224.** Enables the artifact database backend; unset defers to the experiment, which is off by default. |

A companion `tengu_harbor_kite_limits` object drives a peer guard. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it was retyped from bool to string. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE_CLOUD` | bool | experiment `tengu_harbor_kite_cloud` (`false`) | **New at 2.1.232.** Cloud-scoped sibling of `CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE`, gating the same codenamed capability for cloud sessions. Read with the same one-way operator — `Boolean(Y.CLAUDE_CODE_HARBOR_KITE_CLOUD) \|\| rt("tengu_harbor_kite_cloud", false)` (line 535889) — so it can only turn the feature on, never off. Also named in the child-environment registry at line 361732, so it propagates to spawned sessions. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_LARCH_CISTERN` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_larch_cistern` experiment, which adds the "overcorrection" system-prompt section. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PARCHMENT_FERN` | bool | — | **New at 2.1.223.** Gates an unnamed capability behind a codename. Checked at two sites that first exclude a set of older models; the second additionally requires the `tengu_velvet_mallet` experiment for the specific model in play. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_NANKEEN_KESTREL` | bool | — | Forces on the `tengu_nankeen_kestrel` experiment, which enables Windows sandbox support for the Bash tool. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PEWTER_OWL` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state override for the pewter-owl experiment family (brief, model-scoped tool descriptions), overriding the per-model rollout. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_PEWTER_OWL_TOOL` | tri-bool | — | Tri-state override for the `pewter_owl_tool` sub-experiment specifically; falls back to `CLAUDE_CODE_PEWTER_OWL` and then the flag when unset. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_THISTLE_GREBE` | string | — | Overrides the model-steering mode — `default`, `no_nudges` or `counter_steer` — that otherwise comes from client data or a GrowthBook experiment. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_thrifty_sonic` (`false`) | Codename gate. When unset the `tengu_thrifty_sonic` experiment decides, defaulting to off (line 143358). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TURN_UPDATES` | tri-bool | client data (`false`) | **New at 2.1.229.** Overrides the `turn_updates` model capability. Unlike the `tengu_*` gates in this table it does not defer to a GrowthBook experiment: the resolver `FYf(name, envValue, ctx)` (line 816252) returns the environment value whenever it is defined, otherwise consults the per-request context and then the cached client-data payload `Wx()?.["turn_updates"]`, defaulting to off and emitting `tengu_model_capability_from_client_data` when the server payload is what enabled it. Read at line 816288. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_WEB_FETCH_AGENT` | tri-bool | experiment `tengu_clever_orbit` (`false`) | **New at 2.1.232.** Routes WebFetch through an agent-backed path instead of the direct fetch. When unset the `tengu_clever_orbit` experiment decides, defaulting to off: `M3d ??= Y.CLAUDE_CODE_WEB_FETCH_AGENT ?? rt("tengu_clever_orbit", false)` (line 358061), memoised on first read so the value is fixed for the process. |

## Testing & Development Hooks

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `USER_TYPE` | — | `external` | **Newly read at 2.1.273.** Build audience marker. Engine plugins read it through the capability-checked `env.get` call (lines 1616996, 1617885), but the host handler pins it: `env.get("USER_TYPE")` always returns `"external"`, whatever the real environment holds (line 467331). The CLI also writes `USER_TYPE: "external"` into the environments of its own helper subprocesses. Setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_FIRST_LAUNCH` | — | — | **New at 2.1.246.** Read only through the inert namespace (line 1348433), so it is always `undefined` and the reader returns no override. The code would treat a version string as the last-seen version and a truthy value as a first launch for the entrance animation and the startup release-notes summary (lines 1348802, 1352953); in the shipped build both use the stored last-seen versions. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEST_ALLOW_REAL_NETWORK` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.239.** Read directly as `Oe(process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_TEST_ALLOW_REAL_NETWORK)` (line 105522); the typed declaration (line 24737) is not consumed. It would let a test runtime make a live non-loopback request, logged to stderr, instead of throwing `Blocked under test` (lines 105525, 105547). In the shipped build the guard never engages: the test-runtime check `as()` returns `false` and the axios interceptor installer `vy()` is never called, so the variable has no effect. The fixture-recording hint that names it (line 439009) sits behind `q9()`, which also returns `false`. |
| `BUGHUNTER_DEV_BUNDLE_B64` | — | — | Base64-encoded bughunter dev bundle |
| `BUGHUNTER_FLEET_SIZE` | — | `5` | Number of parallel Bug Hunter subagents to launch; default sourced from GrowthBook (`fleet_size`, capped 5–20) |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DOWNLOAD_DEADLINE_MS_FOR_TESTING` | string | `600000` | Test-only override of the download deadline in milliseconds. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_ALLOW_ARTIFACT_PUBLISH` | — | — | **New at 2.1.234. Cannot take effect.** Intended as the operator opt-in that lets a plugin-eval run publish real artifacts. It is read through the inert accessor namespace (`Kd.CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_ALLOW_ARTIFACT_PUBLISH === true`, line 940684), which is built over an empty map and therefore always yields `undefined` — see the note on the inert namespace in *How this was derived*. The eval runner's own `artifactPublish` option is the only thing that can enable publishing. Also stripped from child environments and blanked in bridge-spawned sessions. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_ALLOW_FLAG_OVERRIDES` | — | — | **New at 2.1.234. Cannot take effect.** Comma-separated allowlist of GrowthBook flags an eval case may override; names outside the list are dropped and reported (line 940315). Read through the same inert namespace (line 940688), so the environment can never contribute to the allowlist and only the runner's `allowFlagOverrides` option does. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_ARTIFACT_STUB_DIR` | — | — | **New at 2.1.234. Cannot take effect.** Directory of stubbed artifact responses for plugin-eval runs. The parent *writes* it into the child environment it spawns (line 938839), but the reader resolves it through the inert namespace (line 154385) and so always sees `undefined` and returns `null`. The write and the read are therefore disconnected in the shipped build. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_INTERVIEW_SESSION` | bool | — | **New at 2.1.233.** Internal marker, not an operator knob: the CLI sets it on itself with `G.set("CLAUDE_CODE_EVAL_INTERVIEW_SESSION", true)` immediately before spawning an interactive plugin-eval interview (line 942302). While set, eval report publishing is suppressed unless explicitly requested (line 941991). Deleted from sanitized child environments (line 1033914) and stripped from subprocess environments. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_TIP_ID` | string | — | **Gained a reader at 2.1.273.** Forces a specific startup tip by id. The tips engine plugin declares it in its `env.reads` (line 1617659) and reads it through the capability-checked `env.get` call (line 1617886), whose host handler returns `process.env` (line 467331). Through 2.1.272 it was declared with no consumer. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MOCK_REMOTE_SETTINGS` | string | — | Supplies mock remote settings instead of fetching them; part of the proxy/settings test env set. **Retyped at 2.1.235** from `bool` to `str` (`XZy = Ge.str()`, line 43277). The retype is inert in practice: the name has exactly two occurrences at both 2.1.232 and 2.1.237 — its export-map entry and its membership in the proxy/host-managed registry — so nothing in the bundle reads it. It remains live only in the sense that a host-credentials file may set it and it is frozen against change on refresh. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_MOCK_TRIAL` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_OVERRIDE_DATE` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_STALL_TIMEOUT_MS_FOR_TESTING` | string | test default | Override the internal stall timeout in test harnesses |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEST_FIXTURES_ROOT` | string | current workspace | Override the root directory used for JSON test fixtures |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEST_FORCE_DENY` | raw string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.248 it was retyped from bool to raw string. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEST_NO_GIT_BASH` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_TEST_NO_PWSH` | string | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ULTRAREVIEW_PREFLIGHT_FIXTURE` | string | — | Override the preflight fixture path for ultrareview |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ULTRAREVIEW_QUOTA_FIXTURE` | string | — | **New at 2.1.224.** JSON fixture that replaces the `GET /v1/ultrareview/quota` call outright. The value is parsed and schema-validated; a malformed value returns `null` rather than falling back to the network, so a bad fixture disables the quota check instead of being ignored |
| `CLAUDE_MOCK_HEADERLESS_429` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `FORCE_VCR` | bool | — | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. |
| `IS_DEMO` | string | `false` | Running in demo mode |
| `SWE_BENCH_INSTANCE_ID` | — | — | SWE-bench instance identifier for telemetry |
| `SWE_BENCH_RUN_ID` | — | — | SWE-bench run identifier for telemetry |
| `SWE_BENCH_TASK_ID` | — | — | SWE-bench task identifier for telemetry |

## Azure Identity (Bundled SDK)

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DEFAULT_IDENTITY_CLIENT_ID` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.DEFAULT_IDENTITY_CLIENT_ID]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Client ID sent with system-assigned managed-identity token requests on Azure Machine Learning (line 1137944). |
| `IDENTITY_ENDPOINT` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.IDENTITY_ENDPOINT]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Managed-identity token endpoint for Azure App Service, Azure Arc and Service Fabric. App Service needs it with `IDENTITY_HEADER`, Azure Arc with `IMDS_ENDPOINT`, and Service Fabric with both `IDENTITY_HEADER` and `IDENTITY_SERVER_THUMBPRINT`; each source is skipped when its variables are incomplete (lines 1137630, 1137672, 1137885). |
| `IDENTITY_HEADER` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.IDENTITY_HEADER]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Secret header value that accompanies `IDENTITY_ENDPOINT` for App Service and Service Fabric managed identity (lines 1137631, 1137886). Also a member of the subprocess secret-scrub list since 2.1.251 (line 69940). |
| `IDENTITY_SERVER_THUMBPRINT` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.IDENTITY_SERVER_THUMBPRINT]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Service Fabric managed-identity server certificate thumbprint; the Service Fabric source is used only when it, `IDENTITY_ENDPOINT` and `IDENTITY_HEADER` are all set (line 1137886). |
| `IMDS_ENDPOINT` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.IMDS_ENDPOINT]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Azure Arc instance-metadata endpoint, paired with `IDENTITY_ENDPOINT`. When either is missing, the SDK falls back to Arc file detection on the host (line 1137673). |
| `MSI_ENDPOINT` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.MSI_ENDPOINT]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Azure Machine Learning managed-identity endpoint, paired with `MSI_SECRET` (line 1137924). On its own it also selects the Cloud Shell source, which reads only this variable (line 1137771). |
| `MSI_SECRET` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Read by the bundled `@azure/msal-node` managed-identity client through a constants map, as `process.env[w.MSI_SECRET]`, so a literal-name grep never sees it; present at 2.1.237 too. Azure Machine Learning managed-identity secret, paired with `MSI_ENDPOINT` (line 1137924). Also a member of the subprocess secret-scrub list since 2.1.251 (line 69940). |

| `AZURE_CLIENT_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD` | — | ambient | Password for Azure AD client certificate. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it joined the subprocess secret-scrub list (line 69940). |
| `AZURE_CLIENT_CERTIFICATE_PATH` | — | ambient | Path to Azure AD client certificate |
| `AZURE_CLIENT_ID` | — | ambient | Azure AD application (client) ID |
| `AZURE_CLIENT_SECRET` | — | ambient | Azure AD client secret |
| `AZURE_CLIENT_SEND_CERTIFICATE_CHAIN` | — | ambient | Send certificate chain for SNI |
| `AZURE_FEDERATED_TOKEN_FILE` | — | ambient | Path to federated token file for workload identity. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.251 it joined the subprocess secret-scrub list (line 69940). |
| `AZURE_IDENTITY_DISABLE_MULTITENANTAUTH` | — | ambient | Disable multi-tenant authentication |

## System & Shell

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ALLUSERSPROFILE` | string | ambient | **New at 2.1.282.** Windows all-users profile root. Its only value read is the third fallback, after `ProgramData` and `PROGRAMDATA` and before the literal `C:\ProgramData`, for the directory whose `Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu` folder joins a Windows startup-location directory list (line 317781); that list builder (`PS`, line 317760) is referenced nowhere else in its chunk, so the value has no effect at this tag. The name also appears in the Bash dangerous-`rm` list of variables that are normally set (line 524765) and in the environment passthrough allowlist for plugin-eval case runs (line 1903171). |
| `GIT_ATTR_SOURCE` | — | ambient | **New at 2.1.282.** When set, git attribute resolution reports the source as `attr_source_env` and stops looking at `core.attributesFile` (line 324337). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `HOMESHARE` | string | unset | **Newly typed at 2.1.281**; earlier builds only listed it in the dangerous-`rm` advice set and the plugin-eval strip set. Windows home share: a path whose drive letter equals `HOMEDRIVE` is resolved under `HOMESHARE` before the CLI queries `HKCU\Network\<drive>` for a mapped drive's remote path (line 318284), and `%HOMESHARE%` is expanded in `User Shell Folders` registry values (line 318117). Both feed the Windows profile-folder list (Documents, AppData, Start Menu) that makes remote tool serving refuse with `launched_from_home`; it is read through `ur()`, which prefers the pre-settings environment snapshot over the typed accessor (line 317787). The plugin-eval child environment always drops it (line 1903169); project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `OneDrive` | string | unset | **Newly typed at 2.1.281**; before that the name appeared only in a settings description string. `<OneDrive>\Documents` is added to the Documents folders whose `PowerShell` and `WindowsPowerShell` subdirectories count as Windows profile folders, so a working directory overlapping them makes remote tool serving refuse with `launched_from_home` (lines 317750, 317784, 318504). Read through `ur()`, which prefers the pre-settings environment snapshot over the typed accessor, and ignored unless it passes a local-path check (line 317787). Project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184, matched as `ONEDRIVE`). |
| `OneDriveCommercial` | string | unset | **Newly typed at 2.1.281**; before that the name appeared only in a settings description string. Third OneDrive root candidate after `OneDrive` and `OneDriveConsumer`: `<value>\Documents` joins the Documents folders whose PowerShell profile subdirectories count as Windows profile folders for the remote-tool-serving `launched_from_home` refusal (lines 317784, 318504). Read through `ur()` (pre-settings snapshot first, then the typed accessor) and ignored unless it passes a local-path check (line 317787). Project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184, matched as `ONEDRIVECOMMERCIAL`). |
| `OneDriveConsumer` | string | unset | **Newly typed at 2.1.281**; before that the name appeared only in a settings description string. Second OneDrive root candidate after `OneDrive`: `<value>\Documents` joins the Documents folders whose PowerShell profile subdirectories count as Windows profile folders for the remote-tool-serving `launched_from_home` refusal (lines 317784, 318504). Read through `ur()` (pre-settings snapshot first, then the typed accessor) and ignored unless it passes a local-path check (line 317787). Project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184, matched as `ONEDRIVECONSUMER`). |
| `OS` | — | ambient | **Newly read at 2.1.280.** Windows `OS` value (`Windows_NT`), reported as `os` in the environment attributes collected by a first-party engine plugin through the capability-checked `env.get` call (line 1617018), whose host handler reads `process.env` (line 467331). The token itself is old; this is its first read. |
| `PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE` | — | ambient | **Newly read at 2.1.280.** Windows processor architecture, reported as `processorArchitecture` by the same engine-plugin `env.get` environment collector (line 1617018). |
| `CDPATH` | string | ambient | **Newly typed at 2.1.275.** Before that the name appeared only as a string in shell-safety lists, and the typed declaration still has no property read. The Bash permission checker treats a `cd`/`chdir`/`pushd` to a bare relative directory as unresolvable when `CDPATH` is present in the spawned shell's environment or that environment is unknown (line 531334), and an inline assignment to `CDPATH` makes a command opaque to it (line 442628). A project-scope settings file cannot set it (line 220184). |
| `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH` | — | ambient | **New at 2.1.274.** Git's partial-clone lazy-fetch switch. When it is truthy in the CLI's own environment or the operator's git environment, the lazy-fetch retry is abandoned with the reason "GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH is set" (line 305367). The CLI writes it as `"1"` or `"0"` into its own git children (lines 305458, 590887). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `GIT_CONFIG_PARAMETERS` | — | unset | **Newly read through a process.env alias at 2.1.271.** Git's own `-c` override variable. Since 2.1.251 the `claude plugin eval` sandbox builder has parsed it through its env helper and aborts when it does not parse the way git would (line 1901499). At 2.1.282 Claude Code also parses it with the `GIT_CONFIG_COUNT` pairs to vet the launch environment's git configuration: sensitive keys, includes of files inside a checkout, hard-linked or unreadable files are reported as reasons a checkout cannot be captured (lines 324340, 325480, 353493). A separate reader re-exports only its `safe.directory` entries as `GIT_CONFIG_KEY_n`/`GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_n` pairs (lines 383054, 383071). The CLI also writes it: `http.proxyAuthMethod=basic` for the sandbox proxy (line 265505), shell-snapshot `export` lines (line 444378), and the runner's fallback for git older than 2.31 (lines 623502, 637518). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_WINDOWS_SHELL_LAUNCHER` | bool | unset | **New at 2.1.269.** Windows only: stops PowerShell commands from being launched through `cmd.exe` (line 444530). By default, while `tengu_parallel_trinket` is on (default true), `cmd.exe /d /s /c` runs `chcp 65001` and then PowerShell, which reads the script from `CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL_LAUNCHER_SCRIPT` and writes its output to a file; scripts over 32000 chars, paths of 260+ chars, UNC working directories or an unusable `cmd.exe` fall back to a direct spawn. With this set, PowerShell commands always spawn directly. |
| `ANDROID_HOME` | string | ambient | **New at 2.1.260.** Android SDK location probed for the Remote Control host profile: when `ANDROID_HOME` (falling back to `ANDROID_SDK_ROOT`) names a path that passes the path-safety check and is a directory, the `android-sdk` tag is added to the host toolchain tags registered with the bridge environment (line 1656169). The profile is collected only when the `tengu_bridge_host_profile` gate (default `off`) and the `remoteControl.shareHostProfile` setting allow it (line 1655924). |
| `ANDROID_SDK_ROOT` | string | ambient | **New at 2.1.260.** Fallback for `ANDROID_HOME` in the Remote Control host-profile probe (`a.ANDROID_HOME ?? a.ANDROID_SDK_ROOT`, line 1656169), so it is consulted only when `ANDROID_HOME` is unset or empty. An existing directory adds the `android-sdk` host tag; collection requires the `tengu_bridge_host_profile` gate, `off` by default (line 1655924). |
| `GIT_ALLOW_PROTOCOL` | — | ambient | **New at 2.1.251.** Git's colon-separated protocol allowlist. The CLI intersects the operator's value with its own list when deciding whether a lazy-fetch retry may run (line 305376) and turns it into `-c protocol.<p>.allow=never` arguments for anything outside the list (line 59852). It also writes the variable into the environments of its own git children (`none` for sandboxed reads, lines 590887 and 1621842). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `GIT_SSH` | — | ambient | **New at 2.1.251.** Git's SSH program override. The CLI folds it into a hardened `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` (BatchMode, strict host-key checking) and clears `GIT_SSH` in the child environment (lines 59808, 59819). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `GIT_SSH_VARIANT` | — | `auto` | **New at 2.1.251.** Git's SSH-variant hint (`ssh`, `plink`, `putty`, `tortoiseplink`, `simple`), used when building the hardened SSH command; `auto` or empty means detect from the program name (line 59825). Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |
| `HOMEDRIVE` | string | unset | **Newly typed at 2.1.242**; earlier builds read it only in the bundled AWS SDK home-directory helper (`HOMEDRIVE`+`HOMEPATH` fallback, still at line 951046). Windows home drive, joined with `HOMEPATH` as the home-directory fallback when `HOME` is unset for the git config-file candidates, the repository-settings start roots and the check that rejects a relative value under the private git directory layout (lines 322285, 322997, 324363). It also expands `%HOMEDRIVE%` in `User Shell Folders` registry values and, when a path's drive letter matches it, maps that path under `HOMESHARE` (lines 318118, 318285); a settings tier that changes it counts as moving the settings location (line 322904). In the proxy/host-managed set (line 46781); project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184); the plugin-eval child gets the drive of its sandbox home (line 1903480). |
| `HOMEPATH` | string | unset | **Newly typed at 2.1.242**; earlier builds read it only in the bundled AWS SDK home-directory helper (still at line 951046). Windows home path without the drive, appended to `HOMEDRIVE` as the home-directory fallback when `HOME` is unset for the git config-file candidates, the repository-settings start roots and the check that rejects a relative value under the private git directory layout, whose message then tells the user to set `HOME` (lines 322286, 322997, 324363, 353657). It also expands `%HOMEPATH%` in `User Shell Folders` registry values (line 318119), and a settings tier that changes it counts as moving the settings location (line 322904). In the proxy/host-managed set (line 46781); project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184); the plugin-eval child gets its sandbox home minus the drive (line 1903481). |
| `PROGRAMDATA` | string | unset | **New at 2.1.242.** Windows ProgramData directory. On Windows it adds `%PROGRAMDATA%\Git\config` to the git config-file candidates unless `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM` is set (line 322241), a relative value is rejected under the private git directory layout (line 324364), and it is the second choice after `ProgramData` for locating the Start Menu in the Windows profile-folder list (line 317781). It is in the git-sensitive set whose pre-settings value the CLI's own git commands receive (line 323335). In the proxy/host-managed set (line 46781); project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184); on the plugin-eval environment allow-list (line 1903171). |
| `WINDIR` | — | unset | **Newly read at 2.1.242**; earlier builds listed it only in the policy-helper strip set and a bundled NSIS keyword list. Computer use on Windows reads `process.env.WINDIR` to treat `%WINDIR%\explorer.exe` and shell hosts under `%WINDIR%\SystemApps\` as the system shell, which then takes Explorer's app tier; unset disables that mapping (line 939603). The policy-helper subprocess drops it and sets `windir=C:\Windows` (lines 70725, 70757); project-scoped settings cannot set it (line 220184); on the plugin-eval environment allow-list (line 1903171). |
| `ZDOTDIR` | string | ~ | **Newly typed at 2.1.242**; the shell-config resolver already read it from `process.env` before that. Directory whose `.zshrc` the CLI scans for `alias claude=` lines and rewrites when removing that alias, falling back to the home directory (lines 618204, 618269, 619275); the read is direct, not through the typed accessor. The policy-helper subprocess drops it and pins it to `/var/empty/claude-code-policy-helper` (lines 70725, 70762), the plugin-eval child environment always drops it (line 1903169), and it is in the code-execution name set that the git-sensitive filter also matches (lines 323202, 323338). |
| `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM` | string | unset | **Newly read at 2.1.242; typed declaration added at 2.1.251 (line 24063).** Up to 2.1.241 the CLI only wrote `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1` into environments it builds for git and sandboxed children, which it still does (lines 70749, 590885, 1903517). The read goes through the case-insensitive env helper `fa()`, not the typed accessor: when truthy by git's boolean rules (`true`, `yes`, `on` or a non-zero integer), the git config files Claude Code resolves and inspects for a repository omit the system-level ones (`GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM`, `/etc/gitconfig`, `<prefix>/etc/gitconfig`, `%PROGRAMDATA%\Git\config`) (lines 322239, 323699). Also among the git variables snapshotted for layout pinning (line 355857). |
| `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM` | — | ambient | **Not previously catalogued.** Path of git's system-level config file. It is carried, together with `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL`, into the environments the CLI builds for its git subprocesses (lines 324849, 357453), and pinned to `/dev/null` for isolated probes (line 630655). At 2.1.237 it was read through a destructured `process.env` alias. Read only through the case-tolerant env-lookup helper (`xF(env, "NAME")` / `fa(env, "NAME")`, which falls back to a case-insensitive match on Windows, line 110090), so a literal `process.env` grep misses it. |

| `CLAUDE_PTY_HEARTBEAT_MS` | int (min 1) | `60000` | Heartbeat interval in milliseconds for the PTY channel; unparseable values fall back to 60000. |

Used directly when set; otherwise the path is built from `SystemRoot`, falling back to `C:\Windows` (line 1122200). |

It is a member of the 64-name inherited-environment strip list, so it is deleted from a child process's environment unless explicitly re-supplied, preventing a spawned git or ssh from inheriting the parent session's askpass helper (lines 762812, 764143). **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.274 it started being read through the case-tolerant env helper (`xF`/`fa`, line 110090). |

Used together with `SUDO_UID`/`SUDO_USER` to decide whether a root-owned file is really owned by the invoking human; the claimed gid must match the primary or a supplementary group reported by `id` before it is trusted (lines 936953, 936958). |
| `SUDO_UID` | int (min 0, digits only) | ambient | **Newly typed at 2.1.235.** User id of the sudo invoker. Verified against `getent`/`id` output before use (line 936941), and the whole check is skipped unless the process is actually running as root. |
| `SUDO_USER` | string | ambient | **Newly typed at 2.1.235.** Login name of the sudo invoker. Only consulted when `process.getuid() === 0`, and rejected unless it matches `/^[A-Za-z0-9._][A-Za-z0-9._-]*\$?$/` (line 936928). Once all three sudo variables are verified, file-ownership errors name the invoker instead of claiming root ownership (line 937071). |

| `TMPDIR` | string | — | POSIX temporary directory, forwarded to spawned processes so temporary files land in the expected place. **Since 2.1.237:** At 2.1.247 it started being read through the case-tolerant env helper (`xF`/`fa`, line 110090). |
