## Dependency / Library Internals

| Variable | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CHOKIDAR_INTERVAL` | — | ambient | Chokidar polling interval |
| `CLIPBOARD_NAPI_NODE_PATH` | — | — | **New at 2.1.228.** Intended override naming a prebuilt `clipboard-napi` native module to `require()` in place of the vendored `clipboard-napi.node` binaries. **Unreachable at this tag**: the loader `GOo()` executes `return null;` immediately before the `if (process.env.CLIPBOARD_NAPI_NODE_PATH)` branch (lines 244349-244351), so both the override and the vendored-path fallback below it are dead code, and clipboard native loading always resolves to `null`. |
| `CHOKIDAR_USEPOLLING` | — | ambient | Force chokidar to use polling for file watching |
| `SRT_DEBUG` | — | ambient | Suppress structured-clone debug output |

## Third-Party Credentials (Subprocess Scrub List)

New at 2.1.251. The secret-redaction registry grew from 24 to 95 names that release, and 57 of the additions are third-party tokens, passwords and webhook URLs that Claude Code itself never reads. They matter for one reason: when subprocess scrubbing is armed, every name on the list, and its GitHub Actions `INPUT_<NAME>` spelling, is deleted from the environment of every subprocess the CLI spawns (the list is `Eo`, line 69940; the `INPUT_` expansion is at line 70157; the deletion loop is at line 113602). Scrubbing is armed when `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB` is truthy (latched on first read, line 113360), or when `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` is `local-agent` and the scrub variable is not explicitly false (line 113369). The same list masks these names as `*` when MCP server allow/deny policies are matched (line 426727). A second list, `ho` (line 69941), adds credential-file pointers such as `KUBECONFIG`, `NETRC` and `DOCKER_CONFIG` to the scrub only when it is armed. That list is not one of the tracked registries and its members are not catalogued here unless read elsewhere.

## Removed / Legacy

Documented in earlier revisions of this file but gone from `cli.unpack.js` at `2.1.282`. Setting these has no effect. Every entry was re-checked at 2.1.282 with a whole-token occurrence count. The 23 entries added in this revision have **zero** occurrences. Five older entries now have one or two occurrences that are **mentions, not reads**. `CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE` sits in the telemetry-name allowlist (line 870207). The two `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_METRICS_CLIENT_*` names are in the telemetry-settings name list (line 38122) and the project-settings deny list. `NODE_V8_COVERAGE` is in a toolchain name list (line 323202). `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_CCR_V2` is written as `"1"` into the environment of spawned CCR sessions (line 632945) and is never read. It already had that one occurrence at 2.1.237, so the previous revision's "zero matches" claim was wrong for it. `DATABASE_URL` is deliberately not listed despite losing its declaration at 2.1.222: its one occurrence is prose inside a sandbox-policy explanation (line 37451), so it fails the zero-match definition. `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION` is excluded for a similar reason: it lost its typed declaration at 2.1.224 but is still named in the settings-injectable registry and the telemetry allowlist.

| Variable | Last seen | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_CODE_HOLD_UNANSWERED_PARKED_PERMISSION` | 2.1.274 | Changes what happens to a parked permission request that has no persisted `control_response` when the wait times out: instead of cancelling and re-asking, the session moves to `requires_action` and holds the request answerable, without re-running the interrupted turn (line 1286182). Had a typed `bool` declaration; zero whole-token occurrences from 2.1.275 through 2.1.282. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_RETIRE_UNANSWERED_PARKED_PERMISSION` | 2.1.274 | On the same timeout path, retires the unanswered parked permission outright rather than re-running the interrupted turn (line 1286222). Had a typed `bool` declaration; zero whole-token occurrences from 2.1.275 through 2.1.282. |
| `CLAUDE_CONTEXT_COLLAPSE` | 2.1.274 | Declared in the typed env registry but with no consumer anywhere else in the bundle (verified by both string-literal and minified-symbol search) — reserved or dead at this tag; setting it has no effect. Had a typed `bool` declaration; zero whole-token occurrences from 2.1.275 through 2.1.282. |
| `CLAUDE_CONTEXT_COLLAPSE_MODEL` | 2.1.274 | No accessor reads its value, so setting it still configures nothing. Had a typed `str` declaration; zero whole-token occurrences from 2.1.275 through 2.1.282. |
